1/-
2# HRS.Equilibrium.ReferenceMarginal -- first-marginal bounds for the calibrated reference `R_N` (EQ-02, reference half)
3
4Source: main paper `Q:eq:reference-input` (TeX lines 4449-4488) and the reference part of
5Proposition 4.2 `M:A:prop:marginal` (TeX 3664-3672 and its proof): for `π_N = R_N^{(1)}`,
6`dπ_N/dμ ≤ C`, `‖dπ_N/dμ - 1‖_{L²(μ)} ≤ C δ_N`, `‖dπ_N/dμ - 1‖_∞ → 0`, uniformly over the
7densities `ρ ≤ Λ`.
8
9Main results (end of the file): `reference_marginal_density_le`, `reference_marginal_density_L2`,
10`reference_marginal_density_uniform`.
11
12Proof (the source's cavity argument).  `π_N = Θ/∫Θ · μ` with `Θ = e^{φ} u`, `u` the pole-insertion
13factor of the `(N-1)`-particle cavity ensemble (`marginal_eq_normalized`); `log u = -m + D` with the
14compensated correction `D ≤ C δ_N` (truncation + Wu's linear variance bound, `cavD_torus`);
15`t_N·Ξ = -β g_L*(π - μ)` (`hh_eq`); cavity versus full marginal `C/N` (`event_pi_nu`, RvH); the
16`L²` convolution estimate with the weak-`L^p` kernel (`cv_L2_rate`); absorption by the positive
17definiteness of `g` (`absorption`) for `L²`; for the sup-norm, the exact cavity equation with
18`‖t_N·Ξ‖_∞ → 0` (`small_field`) and `π⁰_N → μ` in total variation (`tv_R0_mu`).
19-/

38/-- **One-coordinate marginal domination**: every one-coordinate marginal of the pinned weak ensemble
39is at most `e^{M+M'+βγ} μ`. -/
40theorem marginal_le_smul (V : WeakPotential μ) {β : ℝ} (hβ : 0 ≤ β) {N n : ℕ} (hN0 : 0 < N)
41    (hN : n + Fintype.card B ≤ N) (a : B → E) (i : Fin n) :
42    (pinnedLaw V (β / N) n a).map (fun y => y i) ≤
43      ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + β * V.γ)) • μ := by
44  have hlam : 0 ≤ β / N := div_nonneg hβ (Nat.cast_nonneg N)
45  have hψ := isField_pinField V hlam a
46  set S := fieldSpec (V := V) hψ hlam n with hS
47  refine Measure.le_iff.2 fun A hA => ?_
48  rw [Measure.map_apply (measurable_pi_apply i) hA]
49  have hmeas : Measurable fun x : Fin n → E => (fun z : Fin n → E => z i) ⁻¹' A |>.indicator
50      (fun _ => (1 : ℝ≥0∞)) x := measurable_const.indicator (measurable_pi_apply i hA)
51  have h1 := S.invariant i (fun x => ((fun z : Fin n → E => z i) ⁻¹' A).indicator (fun _ => (1 : ℝ≥0∞)) x) hmeas
52  have e1 : pinnedLaw V (β / N) n a ((fun z : Fin n → E => z i) ⁻¹' A) =
53      ∫⁻ x, ((fun z : Fin n → E => z i) ⁻¹' A).indicator (fun _ => (1 : ℝ≥0∞)) x ∂S.P := by
54    have := lintegral_indicator_one (μ := S.P) (measurable_pi_apply i hA)
55    have e : S.P = pinnedLaw V (β / N) n a := rfl
56    rw [e] at this ⊢
57    rw [← this]
58    rfl
59  rw [e1, ← h1]
60  have hG : ∀ x, ∫⁻ z, ((fun z : Fin n → E => z i) ⁻¹' A).indicator (fun _ => (1 : ℝ≥0∞)) (Function.update x i z)
61      ∂(S.G i x) = S.G i x A := by
62    intro x
63    have : (fun z => ((fun z : Fin n → E => z i) ⁻¹' A).indicator (fun _ => (1 : ℝ≥0∞)) (Function.update x i z))
64        = A.indicator (fun _ => 1) := by
65      funext z
66      by_cases hz : z ∈ A <;> simp [Set.indicator, hz]
67    rw [this]
68    have := lintegral_indicator_one (μ := S.G i x) hA
69    exact this
70  simp_rw [hG]
71  have hle : ∀ x, S.G i x A ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + β * V.γ)) * μ A := by
72    intro x
73    have := weak_siteKernel_le (B := B) V hβ hN0 hN a i x
74    have h2 := this A
75    simpa [hS, fieldSpec_G] using h2
76  calc ∫⁻ x, S.G i x A ∂S.P ≤ ∫⁻ _x, ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + β * V.γ)) * μ A ∂S.P :=
77        lintegral_mono hle
78    _ = _ := by
79        rw [lintegral_const, measure_univ, mul_one]
80        simp [Measure.smul_apply]

83/-- Adding the pin `x` multiplies the unnormalised weight by `exp(-lam ∑_k q(y_k, x))`. -/
84lemma fieldWeight_pinsWith (V : WeakPotential μ) (lam : ℝ) (a : B → E) {n : ℕ} (x : E)
85    (y : Fin n → E) :
86    fieldWeight V (pinField V lam (pinsWith a x)) lam y =
87      fieldWeight V (pinField V lam a) lam y *
88        ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (-(lam * ∑ k, V.q (y k) x))) := by
89  unfold fieldWeight
90  rw [← ENNReal.ofReal_mul (Real.exp_pos _).le, ← Real.exp_add]
91  congr 2
92  simp_rw [pinField_pinsWith, Finset.sum_sub_distrib, ← Finset.mul_sum]
93  have h3 : ∑ k, V.q (y k) x = ∑ k, V.q x (y k) := Finset.sum_congr rfl fun k _ => V.q_symm _ _
94  ring
95
96
97/-- The cavity function `u(x) = E_{P⁰_n}[exp(-lam ∑_k q(y_k, x))]` (the pole insertion factor). -/
98def cavU (V : WeakPotential μ) (lam : ℝ) (n : ℕ) (a : B → E) (x : E) : ℝ :=
99  ∫ y, Real.exp (-(lam * ∑ k, V.q (y k) x)) ∂(pinnedLaw V lam n a)
100
101lemma measurable_pinPole (V : WeakPotential μ) (lam : ℝ) (n : ℕ) :
102    Measurable fun p : (Fin n → E) × E => Real.exp (-(lam * ∑ k, V.q (p.1 k) p.2)) := by
103  refine Real.measurable_exp.comp (Measurable.neg (Measurable.const_mul
104    (Finset.measurable_sum _ fun k _ => ?_) _))
105  have := V.measurable_q.comp (((measurable_pi_apply k).comp measurable_fst).prodMk measurable_snd :
106    Measurable fun p : (Fin n → E) × E => (p.1 k, p.2))
107  exact this
108
109omit [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] in
110lemma pinPole_le_one (V : WeakPotential μ) {lam : ℝ} (hlam : 0 ≤ lam) {n : ℕ} (y : Fin n → E) (x : E) :
111    Real.exp (-(lam * ∑ k, V.q (y k) x)) ≤ 1 := by
112  rw [Real.exp_le_one_iff, neg_nonpos]
113  exact mul_nonneg hlam (Finset.sum_nonneg fun k _ => V.q_nonneg _ _)
114
115lemma lintegral_fieldWeight_pinsWith (V : WeakPotential μ) {lam : ℝ} (hlam : 0 ≤ lam) (a : B → E)
116    (n : ℕ) (x : E) :
117    fieldNorm V (pinField V lam (pinsWith a x)) lam n =
118      fieldNorm V (pinField V lam a) lam n * ENNReal.ofReal (cavU V lam n a x) := by
119  have hψ := isField_pinField V hlam a
120  haveI := isProb_pinnedLaw V hlam n a
121  have hW := measurable_fieldWeight hψ.measurable V lam (n := n)
122  have hP : Measurable fun y : Fin n → E => ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (-(lam * ∑ k, V.q (y k) x))) :=
123    ENNReal.measurable_ofReal.comp ((measurable_pinPole V lam n).comp (measurable_id.prodMk measurable_const))
124  have hint : Integrable (fun y : Fin n → E => Real.exp (-(lam * ∑ k, V.q (y k) x)))
125      (pinnedLaw V lam n a) := by
126    refine Integrable.of_bound (C := 1) ((measurable_pinPole V lam n).comp
127      (measurable_id.prodMk measurable_const)).aestronglyMeasurable (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => ?_)
128    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
129    exact pinPole_le_one V hlam y x
130  have hL : ENNReal.ofReal (cavU V lam n a x) =
131      ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (-(lam * ∑ k, V.q (y k) x))) ∂(pinnedLaw V lam n a) := by
132    unfold cavU
133    rw [ofReal_integral_eq_lintegral_ofReal hint (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun _ => (Real.exp_pos _).le)]
134  rw [hL]
135  unfold fieldNorm
136  simp_rw [fieldWeight_pinsWith]
137  unfold pinnedLaw fieldLaw
138  rw [lintegral_withDensity_eq_lintegral_mul _ (measurable_fieldDensity hψ.measurable V lam n) hP]
139  simp only [Pi.mul_apply]
140  unfold fieldDensity
141  have h1 : fieldNorm V (pinField V lam a) lam n ≠ 0 := (fieldNorm_pos hψ.measurable V lam n).ne'
142  have h2 : fieldNorm V (pinField V lam a) lam n ≠ ⊤ := (fieldNorm_lt_top hψ hlam n).ne
143  have : ∀ y : Fin n → E, fieldWeight V (pinField V lam a) lam y *
144      ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (-(lam * ∑ k, V.q (y k) x))) =
145      fieldNorm V (pinField V lam a) lam n *
146        ((fieldWeight V (pinField V lam a) lam y * (fieldNorm V (pinField V lam a) lam n)⁻¹) *
147          ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (-(lam * ∑ k, V.q (y k) x)))) := by
148    intro y
149    calc _ = (fieldNorm V (pinField V lam a) lam n * (fieldNorm V (pinField V lam a) lam n)⁻¹) *
150          (fieldWeight V (pinField V lam a) lam y *
151            ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (-(lam * ∑ k, V.q (y k) x)))) := by
152          rw [ENNReal.mul_inv_cancel h1 h2, one_mul]
153      _ = _ := by ring
154  simp_rw [this]
155  rw [lintegral_const_mul' _ _ h2]
156  rfl

158/-- **One-coordinate marginal of the `(n+1)`-particle ensemble** (cavity formula): its density with respect
159to `μ` is `c · e^{ψ(x)} u(x)`, `u = cavU` the pole-insertion factor of the `n`-particle ensemble. -/
160theorem marginal_eq_withDensity (V : WeakPotential μ) {lam : ℝ} (hlam : 0 ≤ lam) (a : B → E) {n : ℕ}
161    (i : Fin (n + 1)) :
162    (pinnedLaw V lam (n + 1) a).map (fun y => y i) =
163      μ.withDensity (fun x =>
164        (fieldNorm V (pinField V lam a) lam n * (fieldNorm V (pinField V lam a) lam (n + 1))⁻¹) *
165          (ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (pinField V lam a x)) * ENNReal.ofReal (cavU V lam n a x))) := by
166  have hψ := isField_pinField V hlam a
167  have hψx : ∀ x, IsField μ (pinField V lam (pinsWith a x)) V.M
168      (V.M' + lam * Fintype.card (Option B) * V.γ) := fun x => isField_pinField V hlam _
169  ext A hA
170  have hS : MeasurableSet ((fun y : Fin (n + 1) → E => y i) ⁻¹' A) := measurable_pi_apply _ hA
171  rw [Measure.map_apply (measurable_pi_apply _) hA]
172  change (fieldLaw V (pinField V lam a) lam (n + 1)) _ = _
173  unfold fieldLaw
174  rw [withDensity_apply _ hS, ← lintegral_indicator hS]
175  set e := MeasurableEquiv.piFinSuccAbove (fun _ : Fin (n + 1) => E) i with he
176  have hmp := measurePreserving_piFinSuccAbove (fun _ : Fin (n + 1) => μ) i
177  have hsymm : ∀ (x : E) (y : Fin n → E), e.symm (x, y) = insertAt i x y := fun _ _ => rfl
178  set h : (Fin (n + 1) → E) → ℝ≥0∞ := fun y =>
179    ((fun y : Fin (n + 1) → E => y i) ⁻¹' A).indicator
180      (fieldDensity V (pinField V lam a) lam (n + 1)) y with hh
181  have hhm : Measurable h :=
182    (measurable_fieldDensity hψ.measurable V lam (n + 1)).indicator hS
183  have step1 : ∫⁻ y, h y ∂(Measure.pi fun _ : Fin (n + 1) => μ)
184      = ∫⁻ p, h (e.symm p) ∂(μ.prod (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin n => μ)) := by
185    have := hmp.lintegral_comp_emb e.measurableEmbedding (fun p => h (e.symm p))
186    rw [← this]
187    exact lintegral_congr fun y => by rw [e.symm_apply_apply]
188  rw [step1, lintegral_prod (fun p => h (e.symm p)) (hhm.comp e.symm.measurable).aemeasurable]
189  rw [withDensity_apply _ hA, ← lintegral_indicator hA]
190  refine lintegral_congr fun x => ?_
191  simp only [hsymm]
192  have hx : ∀ y : Fin n → E, h (insertAt i x y)
193      = A.indicator (fun _ => (ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (pinField V lam a x))
194            * (fieldNorm V (pinField V lam a) lam (n + 1))⁻¹)) x
195            * fieldWeight V (pinField V lam (pinsWith a x)) lam y := by
196    intro y
197    by_cases hm : x ∈ A
198    · have h1 : insertAt i x y ∈ (fun y : Fin (n + 1) → E => y i) ⁻¹' A := by
199        simp [hm]
200      simp only [hh]
201      rw [Set.indicator_of_mem h1, Set.indicator_of_mem hm]
202      unfold fieldDensity
203      rw [fieldWeight_insertAt]; ring
204    · have h1 : insertAt i x y ∉ (fun y : Fin (n + 1) → E => y i) ⁻¹' A := by
205        simp [hm]
206      simp only [hh]
207      rw [Set.indicator_of_notMem h1, Set.indicator_of_notMem hm]
208      simp
209  simp_rw [hx]
210  have hFW : Measurable (fieldWeight V (pinField V lam (pinsWith a x)) lam (n := n)) :=
211    measurable_fieldWeight (hψx x).measurable V lam
212  rw [lintegral_const_mul _ hFW]
213  have hn : ∫⁻ y, fieldWeight V (pinField V lam (pinsWith a x)) lam y ∂(Measure.pi fun _ : Fin n => μ)
214      = fieldNorm V (pinField V lam a) lam n * ENNReal.ofReal (cavU V lam n a x) := by
215    rw [← lintegral_fieldWeight_pinsWith V hlam a n x]; rfl
216  rw [hn]
217  by_cases hm : x ∈ A
218  · simp only [Set.indicator_of_mem hm]
219    ring
220  · simp [Set.indicator_of_notMem hm]
221
222lemma measurable_cavU (V : WeakPotential μ) {lam : ℝ} (hlam : 0 ≤ lam) (n : ℕ) (a : B → E) :
223    Measurable (cavU V lam n a) := by
224  haveI := isProb_pinnedLaw V hlam n a
225  unfold cavU
226  exact ((measurable_pinPole V lam n).stronglyMeasurable.integral_prod_left'
227    (μ := pinnedLaw V lam n a)).measurable
228
229lemma integrable_pinPole (V : WeakPotential μ) {lam : ℝ} (hlam : 0 ≤ lam) (a : B → E) (n : ℕ) (x : E) :
230    Integrable (fun y : Fin n → E => Real.exp (-(lam * ∑ k, V.q (y k) x))) (pinnedLaw V lam n a) := by
231  haveI := isProb_pinnedLaw V hlam n a
232  refine Integrable.of_bound (C := 1) ((measurable_pinPole V lam n).comp
233    (measurable_id.prodMk measurable_const)).aestronglyMeasurable (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => ?_)
234  rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
235  exact pinPole_le_one V hlam y x
236
237lemma cavU_pos (V : WeakPotential μ) {lam : ℝ} (hlam : 0 ≤ lam) (a : B → E) (n : ℕ) (x : E) :
238    0 < cavU V lam n a x := by
239  haveI := isProb_pinnedLaw V hlam n a
240  exact integral_exp_pos (f := fun y : Fin n → E => -(lam * ∑ k, V.q (y k) x))
241    (integrable_pinPole V hlam a n x)
242
243lemma cavU_le_one (V : WeakPotential μ) {lam : ℝ} (hlam : 0 ≤ lam) (a : B → E) (n : ℕ) (x : E) :
244    cavU V lam n a x ≤ 1 := by
245  haveI := isProb_pinnedLaw V hlam n a
246  unfold cavU
247  calc ∫ y, Real.exp (-(lam * ∑ k, V.q (y k) x)) ∂(pinnedLaw V lam n a)
248      ≤ ∫ _y, (1 : ℝ) ∂(pinnedLaw V lam n a) :=
249        integral_mono (integrable_pinPole V hlam a n x) (integrable_const _)
250          fun y => pinPole_le_one V hlam y x
251    _ = 1 := by simp
252
253/-- The unnormalised one-point density `Θ(x) = e^{ψ(x)} u(x)`. -/
254def cavTheta (V : WeakPotential μ) (lam : ℝ) (n : ℕ) (a : B → E) (x : E) : ℝ :=
255  Real.exp (pinField V lam a x) * cavU V lam n a x
256
257lemma measurable_cavTheta (V : WeakPotential μ) {lam : ℝ} (hlam : 0 ≤ lam) (a : B → E) (n : ℕ) :
258    Measurable (cavTheta V lam n a) :=
259  ((isField_pinField V hlam a).measurable.exp).mul (measurable_cavU V hlam n a)
260
261lemma cavTheta_pos (V : WeakPotential μ) {lam : ℝ} (hlam : 0 ≤ lam) (a : B → E) (n : ℕ) (x : E) :
262    0 < cavTheta V lam n a x := mul_pos (Real.exp_pos _) (cavU_pos V hlam a n x)
263
264lemma cavTheta_le (V : WeakPotential μ) {lam : ℝ} (hlam : 0 ≤ lam) (a : B → E) (n : ℕ) (x : E) :
265    cavTheta V lam n a x ≤ Real.exp V.M := by
266  unfold cavTheta
267  calc Real.exp (pinField V lam a x) * cavU V lam n a x ≤ Real.exp V.M * 1 := by
268        refine mul_le_mul (Real.exp_le_exp.2 ((isField_pinField V hlam a).le x)) (cavU_le_one V hlam a n x)
269          (cavU_pos V hlam a n x).le (Real.exp_pos _).le
270    _ = _ := mul_one _
271
272lemma integrable_cavTheta (V : WeakPotential μ) {lam : ℝ} (hlam : 0 ≤ lam) (a : B → E) (n : ℕ) :
273    Integrable (cavTheta V lam n a) μ :=
274  Integrable.of_bound (C := Real.exp V.M) (measurable_cavTheta V hlam a n).aestronglyMeasurable
275    (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => by
276      rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_pos (cavTheta_pos V hlam a n x)]; exact cavTheta_le V hlam a n x)
277
278lemma integral_cavTheta_pos (V : WeakPotential μ) {lam : ℝ} (hlam : 0 ≤ lam) (a : B → E) (n : ℕ) :
279    0 < ∫ x, cavTheta V lam n a x ∂μ :=
280  integral_pos_iff_support_of_nonneg (fun x => (cavTheta_pos V hlam a n x).le)
281    (integrable_cavTheta V hlam a n) |>.2 (by
282      have : Function.support (cavTheta V lam n a) = Set.univ := by
283        ext x; simp [(cavTheta_pos V hlam a n x).ne']
284      rw [this]; simp)
285
286/-- **Normalised cavity formula**: the one-coordinate marginal of the `(n+1)`-particle pinned weak
287ensemble has `μ`-density `Θ / ∫ Θ`. -/
288theorem marginal_eq_normalized (V : WeakPotential μ) {lam : ℝ} (hlam : 0 ≤ lam) (a : B → E) {n : ℕ}
289    (i : Fin (n + 1)) :
290    (pinnedLaw V lam (n + 1) a).map (fun y => y i) =
291      μ.withDensity (fun x => ENNReal.ofReal (cavTheta V lam n a x / ∫ x', cavTheta V lam n a x' ∂μ)) := by
292  have hmain := marginal_eq_withDensity V hlam a i (n := n)
293  haveI := isProb_pinnedLaw V hlam (n + 1) a
294  set Z := ∫ x', cavTheta V lam n a x' ∂μ with hZ
295  have hZpos : 0 < Z := integral_cavTheta_pos V hlam a n
296  set c := fieldNorm V (pinField V lam a) lam n * (fieldNorm V (pinField V lam a) lam (n + 1))⁻¹ with hc
297  have hΘ : ∀ x, ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (pinField V lam a x)) * ENNReal.ofReal (cavU V lam n a x)
298      = ENNReal.ofReal (cavTheta V lam n a x) := fun x =>
299    (ENNReal.ofReal_mul (Real.exp_pos _).le).symm
300  simp_rw [hΘ] at hmain
301  have hZl : ∫⁻ x, ENNReal.ofReal (cavTheta V lam n a x) ∂μ = ENNReal.ofReal Z :=
302    (ofReal_integral_eq_lintegral_ofReal (integrable_cavTheta V hlam a n)
303      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => (cavTheta_pos V hlam a n x).le)).symm
304  have hmass := measure_univ (μ := (pinnedLaw V lam (n + 1) a).map (fun y => y i))
305  rw [hmain, withDensity_apply _ MeasurableSet.univ, Measure.restrict_univ,
306    lintegral_const_mul c (f := fun x => ENNReal.ofReal (cavTheta V lam n a x))
307      (ENNReal.measurable_ofReal.comp (measurable_cavTheta V hlam a n)), hZl] at hmass
308  have hc' : c = (ENNReal.ofReal Z)⁻¹ := by
309    have hne : ENNReal.ofReal Z ≠ 0 := by simpa using hZpos
310    exact ENNReal.eq_inv_of_mul_eq_one_left hmass
311  rw [hmain]
312  congr 1
313  funext x
314  rw [hc', ENNReal.ofReal_div_of_pos hZpos, div_eq_mul_inv, mul_comm]

317theorem pinnedLaw_empty (V : WeakPotential μ) (lam : ℝ)
318    (n : ℕ) (a : Empty → E) : pinnedLaw V lam n a = fieldLaw V V.φ lam n := by
319  unfold pinnedLaw
320  congr 1
321  funext z
322  simp [pinField]

329theorem exp_le_one_add_add_sq {c : ℝ} (hc : 0 ≤ c) {x : ℝ} (hx : x ≤ c) :
330    Real.exp x ≤ 1 + x + x ^ 2 / 2 * Real.exp c := by
331  set g : ℝ → ℝ := fun x => 1 + x + x ^ 2 / 2 * Real.exp c - Real.exp x with hg
332  have hg' : ∀ x, HasDerivAt g (1 + x * Real.exp c - Real.exp x) x := by
333    intro x
334    have h1 := (((hasDerivAt_id x).const_add 1).add (((hasDerivAt_pow 2 x).div_const 2).mul_const (Real.exp c)))
335    have h2 := h1.sub (Real.hasDerivAt_exp x)
336    refine (h2.congr_deriv ?_)
337    simp
338  set g1 : ℝ → ℝ := fun x => 1 + x * Real.exp c - Real.exp x with hg1
339  have hg1' : ∀ x, HasDerivAt g1 (Real.exp c - Real.exp x) x := by
340    intro x
341    have h1 := (((hasDerivAt_id x).mul_const (Real.exp c)).const_add 1).sub (Real.hasDerivAt_exp x)
342    refine (h1.congr_deriv ?_)
343    simp
344  have hmono : MonotoneOn g1 (Set.Iic c) := by
345    refine monotoneOn_of_deriv_nonneg (convex_Iic c) (fun x _ => (hg1' x).continuousAt.continuousWithinAt)
346      (fun x _ => (hg1' x).differentiableAt.differentiableWithinAt) fun x hx => ?_
347    rw [(hg1' x).deriv]
348    have : x ≤ c := by simpa using interior_subset hx
349    linarith [Real.exp_le_exp.2 this]
350  have h0 : g1 0 = 0 := by simp [hg1]
351  have e0 : g 0 = 0 := by simp [hg]
352  rcases le_total x 0 with hx0 | hx0
353  · have hanti : AntitoneOn g (Set.Iic 0) := by
354      refine antitoneOn_of_deriv_nonpos (convex_Iic 0) (fun x _ => (hg' x).continuousAt.continuousWithinAt)
355        (fun x _ => (hg' x).differentiableAt.differentiableWithinAt) fun y hy => ?_
356      rw [(hg' y).deriv]
357      have hy0 : y ≤ 0 := by simpa using interior_subset hy
358      have := hmono (show y ∈ Set.Iic c from le_trans hy0 hc) (show (0:ℝ) ∈ Set.Iic c from hc) hy0
359      change g1 y ≤ g1 0 at this
360      rw [h0] at this
361      exact this
362    have := hanti (show x ∈ Set.Iic 0 from hx0) (show (0:ℝ) ∈ Set.Iic 0 from Set.mem_Iic.2 le_rfl) hx0
363    rw [e0] at this
364    simp only [hg] at this
365    linarith
366  · have hmon : MonotoneOn g (Set.Icc 0 c) := by
367      refine monotoneOn_of_deriv_nonneg (convex_Icc 0 c) (fun x _ => (hg' x).continuousAt.continuousWithinAt)
368        (fun x _ => (hg' x).differentiableAt.differentiableWithinAt) fun y hy => ?_
369      rw [(hg' y).deriv]
370      rw [interior_Icc] at hy
371      have := hmono (show (0:ℝ) ∈ Set.Iic c from hc) (show y ∈ Set.Iic c from hy.2.le) hy.1.le
372      change g1 0 ≤ g1 y at this
373      rw [h0] at this
374      exact this
375    have := hmon (show (0:ℝ) ∈ Set.Icc 0 c from ⟨le_rfl, hc⟩) (show x ∈ Set.Icc 0 c from ⟨hx0, hx⟩) hx0
376    rw [e0] at this
377    simp only [hg] at this
378    linarith
379
380
381theorem log_integral_exp_neg_add_le {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
382    (F F' : Ω → ℝ) (hF : Measurable F) (hF' : Measurable F') (h0 : ∀ ω, 0 ≤ F' ω)
383    (hle : ∀ ω, F' ω ≤ F ω) (hF'2 : MemLp F' 2 P) :
384    Real.log (∫ ω, Real.exp (-F ω) ∂P) + ∫ ω, F ω ∂P ≤
385      Real.exp (∫ ω, F' ω ∂P) / 2 * ProbabilityTheory.variance F' P + (∫ ω, F ω ∂P - ∫ ω, F' ω ∂P) := by
386  set m := ∫ ω, F' ω ∂P with hm
387  have hm0 : 0 ≤ m := integral_nonneg h0
388  have hF'i : Integrable F' P := hF'2.integrable one_le_two
389  have hbd : ∀ G : Ω → ℝ, Measurable G → (∀ ω, 0 ≤ G ω) → Integrable (fun ω => Real.exp (-G ω)) P := by
390    intro G hG hG0
391    refine Integrable.of_bound (C := 1) (Real.measurable_exp.comp hG.neg).aestronglyMeasurable
392      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun ω => ?_)
393    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _), Real.exp_le_one_iff]
394    linarith [hG0 ω]
395  have hF0 : ∀ ω, 0 ≤ F ω := fun ω => (h0 ω).trans (hle ω)
396  have hiF := hbd F hF hF0
397  have hiF' := hbd F' hF' h0
398  have hpos : 0 < ∫ ω, Real.exp (-F ω) ∂P := integral_exp_pos (f := fun ω => -F ω) hiF
399  have h1 : ∫ ω, Real.exp (-F ω) ∂P ≤ ∫ ω, Real.exp (-F' ω) ∂P :=
400    integral_mono hiF hiF' fun ω => Real.exp_le_exp.2 (neg_le_neg (hle ω))
401  set v := ProbabilityTheory.variance F' P with hv
402  have hvar : ∫ ω, (F' ω - m) ^ 2 ∂P = v := by
403    rw [hv, ProbabilityTheory.variance_eq_integral hF'.aemeasurable]
404  have hsq : Integrable (fun ω => (m - F' ω) ^ 2) P := by
405    have := (hF'2.sub (memLp_const m)).integrable_sq
406    refine (this.congr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun ω => ?_))
407    simp only [Pi.sub_apply]
408    ring
409  have hX : Integrable (fun ω => m - F' ω) P := (integrable_const m).sub hF'i
410  have hXint : ∫ ω, (m - F' ω) ∂P = 0 := by
411    rw [integral_sub (integrable_const m) hF'i]
412    simp [hm]
413  have hX2 : ∫ ω, (m - F' ω) ^ 2 ∂P = v := by
414    rw [← hvar]; congr 1; funext ω; ring
415  have h2 : ∫ ω, Real.exp (-F' ω) ∂P ≤ Real.exp (-m) * (1 + Real.exp m / 2 * v) := by
416    have hpt : ∀ ω, Real.exp (-F' ω) ≤ Real.exp (-m) * (1 + (m - F' ω) + (m - F' ω) ^ 2 / 2 * Real.exp m) := by
417      intro ω
418      have := exp_le_one_add_add_sq hm0 (x := m - F' ω) (by linarith [h0 ω])
419      have e : Real.exp (-F' ω) = Real.exp (-m) * Real.exp (m - F' ω) := by
420        rw [← Real.exp_add]; congr 1; ring
421      rw [e]
422      exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left this (Real.exp_pos _).le
423    have hi2 : Integrable (fun ω => (m - F' ω) ^ 2 / 2 * Real.exp m) P :=
424      (hsq.div_const 2).mul_const _
425    have hi1 : Integrable (fun ω => 1 + (m - F' ω)) P := (integrable_const 1).add hX
426    have hint : Integrable (fun ω => Real.exp (-m) * (1 + (m - F' ω) + (m - F' ω) ^ 2 / 2 * Real.exp m)) P :=
427      (hi1.add hi2).const_mul _
428    refine (integral_mono hiF' hint hpt).trans (le_of_eq ?_)
429    rw [integral_const_mul, integral_add hi1 hi2, integral_add (integrable_const 1) hX, hXint,
430      integral_mul_const, integral_div, hX2]
431    simp
432    ring
433  have h3 : Real.log (∫ ω, Real.exp (-F ω) ∂P) ≤ -m + Real.exp m / 2 * v := by
434    have hv0 : 0 ≤ v := ProbabilityTheory.variance_nonneg _ _
435    have hlog : Real.log (∫ ω, Real.exp (-F ω) ∂P) ≤ Real.log (Real.exp (-m) * (1 + Real.exp m / 2 * v)) :=
436      Real.log_le_log hpos (h1.trans h2)
437    rw [Real.log_mul (Real.exp_pos _).ne' (by positivity), Real.log_exp] at hlog
438    have := Real.log_le_sub_one_of_pos (show 0 < 1 + Real.exp m / 2 * v by positivity)
439    linarith
440  linarith
441
442theorem log_integral_exp_neg_add_nonneg {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
443    (F : Ω → ℝ) (hF : Measurable F) (h0 : ∀ ω, 0 ≤ F ω) (hFi : Integrable F P) :
444    0 ≤ Real.log (∫ ω, Real.exp (-F ω) ∂P) + ∫ ω, F ω ∂P := by
445  have hbd : Integrable (fun ω => Real.exp (-F ω)) P := by
446    refine Integrable.of_bound (C := 1) (Real.measurable_exp.comp hF.neg).aestronglyMeasurable
447      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun ω => ?_)
448    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _), Real.exp_le_one_iff]
449    linarith [h0 ω]
450  have hpos : 0 < ∫ ω, Real.exp (-F ω) ∂P := integral_exp_pos (f := fun ω => -F ω) hbd
451  have hJ := convexOn_exp.map_integral_le (μ := P) (f := fun ω => -F ω) Real.continuous_exp.continuousOn
452    isClosed_univ (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun _ => Set.mem_univ _) hFi.neg hbd
453  rw [integral_neg] at hJ
454  have := Real.log_le_log (Real.exp_pos _) hJ
455  rw [Real.log_exp] at this
456  linarith
457
458
459end Prob
460
461section CavD
462
463variable {E : Type*} [MeasurableSpace E] {μ : Measure E} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ]
464
465lemma measurable_qx (V : WeakPotential μ) (x : E) : Measurable fun y => V.q y x :=
466  V.measurable_q.comp (measurable_id.prodMk measurable_const)
467
468/-- The cavity marginal `ν`: the one-coordinate marginal of the `n`-particle ensemble without pins. -/
469def cavMarg (V : WeakPotential μ) (lam : ℝ) (n : ℕ) [NeZero n] : Measure E :=
470  (pinnedLaw V lam n (fun e : Empty => e.elim)).map (fun y => y 0)
471
472lemma pinnedLaw_marginal_eq (V : WeakPotential μ) {lam : ℝ} (n : ℕ) [NeZero n] (k : Fin n) :
473    (pinnedLaw V lam n (fun e : Empty => e.elim)).map (fun y => y k) = cavMarg V lam n := by
474  unfold cavMarg
475  rw [pinnedLaw_empty]
476  exact map_eval_eq_of_symm μ _ (measurable_fieldDensity V.measurable_φ V lam n)
477    (fun σ y => fieldDensity_comp_perm V _ _ σ y) k 0
478
479theorem cavD_bounds (V : WeakPotential μ) {β : ℝ} (hβ : 0 ≤ β) {N n : ℕ} [NeZero n] (hN0 : 0 < N)
480    (hn : n ≤ N) (hκ : weakKappa V β ≤ 1 / 2) (x : E) {mb : ℝ}
481    (hm : β / N * n * ∫ y, V.q y x ∂(cavMarg V (β / N) n) ≤ mb) :
482    0 ≤ Real.log (cavU V (β / N) n (fun e : Empty => e.elim) x) +
483        β / N * n * ∫ y, V.q y x ∂(cavMarg V (β / N) n) ∧
484    Real.log (cavU V (β / N) n (fun e : Empty => e.elim) x) +
485        β / N * n * ∫ y, V.q y x ∂(cavMarg V (β / N) n) ≤
486      Real.exp mb * (n * ∫ y, (min (β / N * V.q y x) 1) ^ 2 ∂(cavMarg V (β / N) n)) +
487        n * ∫ y, max (β / N * V.q y x - 1) 0 ∂(cavMarg V (β / N) n) := by
488  set lam : ℝ := β / N with hlam_def
489  have hlam : 0 ≤ lam := div_nonneg hβ (Nat.cast_nonneg N)
490  set a : Empty → E := fun e => e.elim with ha
491  set P := pinnedLaw V lam n a with hP
492  haveI : IsProbabilityMeasure P := isProb_pinnedLaw V hlam n a
493  set ν := cavMarg V lam n with hν
494  have hmarg : ∀ k : Fin n, P.map (fun y => y k) = ν := fun k => pinnedLaw_marginal_eq V n k
495  haveI : IsProbabilityMeasure ν := by
496    rw [← hmarg 0]; infer_instance
497  have hνle : ν ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + β * V.γ)) • μ := by
498    have := marginal_le_smul (B := Empty) V hβ hN0 (n := n) (by simpa using hn) a 0
499    exact this
500  set g : E → ℝ := fun y => V.q y x with hg
501  have hgm : Measurable g := measurable_qx V x
502  have hg0 : ∀ y, 0 ≤ g y := fun y => V.q_nonneg _ _
503  have hgi : Integrable g ν := by
504    have hμ : Integrable g μ := integrable_q_left V x
505    exact Integrable.of_measure_le_smul ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top hνle hμ
506  -- the statistic `F_x = lam ∑_k g(y_k)` and its truncation
507  set Fx : (Fin n → E) → ℝ := fun y => lam * ∑ k, g (y k) with hFx
508  have hFxm : Measurable Fx := Measurable.const_mul (Finset.measurable_sum _ fun k _ =>
509    hgm.comp (measurable_pi_apply k)) _
510  have hFx0 : ∀ y, 0 ≤ Fx y := fun y => mul_nonneg hlam (Finset.sum_nonneg fun k _ => hg0 _)
511  have hint_k : ∀ k : Fin n, Integrable (fun y : Fin n → E => g (y k)) P := by
512    intro k
513    have := hgi
514    rw [← hmarg k] at this
515    exact (integrable_map_measure hgm.aestronglyMeasurable (measurable_pi_apply k).aemeasurable).1 this
516  have hint_k' : ∀ k : Fin n, ∫ y, g (y k) ∂P = ∫ y, g y ∂ν := by
517    intro k
518    rw [← hmarg k, integral_map (measurable_pi_apply k).aemeasurable hgm.aestronglyMeasurable]
519  have hFxi : Integrable Fx P := (integrable_finset_sum _ fun k _ => hint_k k).const_mul lam
520  have hEF : ∫ y, Fx y ∂P = lam * n * ∫ y, g y ∂ν := by
521    simp only [hFx]
522    rw [integral_const_mul, integral_finset_sum _ fun k _ => hint_k k]
523    simp_rw [hint_k']
524    simp
525    ring
526  have hcav : cavU V lam n a x = ∫ y, Real.exp (-Fx y) ∂P := rfl
527  refine ⟨?_, ?_⟩
528  · rw [hcav, ← hEF]
529    exact log_integral_exp_neg_add_nonneg P Fx hFxm hFx0 hFxi
530  set f : E → ℝ := fun u => min (lam * g u) 1 with hf
531  have hfm : Measurable f := (hgm.const_mul lam).min measurable_const
532  have hf0 : ∀ u, 0 ≤ f u := fun u => le_min (mul_nonneg hlam (hg0 u)) zero_le_one
533  have hf1 : ∀ u, f u ≤ 1 := fun u => min_le_right _ _
534  set F' : (Fin n → E) → ℝ := fun y => ∑ k, f (y k) with hF'
535  have hF'm : Measurable F' := Finset.measurable_sum _ fun k _ => hfm.comp (measurable_pi_apply k)
536  have hF'0 : ∀ y, 0 ≤ F' y := fun y => Finset.sum_nonneg fun k _ => hf0 _
537  have hF'le : ∀ y, F' y ≤ Fx y := by
538    intro y
539    simp only [hF', hFx, Finset.mul_sum]
540    exact Finset.sum_le_sum fun k _ => min_le_left _ _
541  have hF'b : ∀ y, ‖F' y‖ ≤ n := by
542    intro y
543    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (hF'0 y)]
544    calc F' y ≤ ∑ _k : Fin n, (1 : ℝ) := Finset.sum_le_sum fun k _ => hf1 _
545      _ = n := by simp
546  have hF'2 : MemLp F' 2 P := MemLp.of_bound hF'm.aestronglyMeasurable n (Filter.Eventually.of_forall hF'b)
547  have hfi : ∀ k : Fin n, Integrable (fun y : Fin n → E => f (y k)) P := by
548    intro k
549    exact Integrable.of_bound (hfm.comp (measurable_pi_apply k)).aestronglyMeasurable 1
550      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => by
551        rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (hf0 _)]; exact hf1 _)
552  have hfνi : Integrable f ν := Integrable.of_bound hfm.aestronglyMeasurable 1
553    (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => by
554      rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (hf0 _)]; exact hf1 _)
555  have hfk : ∀ k : Fin n, ∫ y, f (y k) ∂P = ∫ y, f y ∂ν := by
556    intro k
557    rw [← hmarg k, integral_map (measurable_pi_apply k).aemeasurable hfm.aestronglyMeasurable]
558  have hEF' : ∫ y, F' y ∂P = n * ∫ y, f y ∂ν := by
559    simp only [hF']
560    rw [integral_finset_sum _ fun k _ => hfi k]
561    simp_rw [hfk]
562    simp
563  have hvar : variance F' P ≤ 2 * n * variance f ν := by
564    have hf2 : MemLp f 2 ν := MemLp.of_bound hfm.aestronglyMeasurable 1
565      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => by
566        rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (hf0 _)]; exact hf1 _)
567    exact weak_linear_variance_real V hβ hN0 (B := Empty) (n := n) (by simpa using hn) a hκ ν hmarg f hfm hf2
568  have hvar2 : variance f ν ≤ ∫ y, (f y) ^ 2 ∂ν := by
569    have := variance_le_expectation_sq (μ := ν) (X := f) hfm.aestronglyMeasurable
570    simpa using this
571  have hmaxint : ∫ y, max (lam * g y - 1) 0 ∂ν = lam * ∫ y, g y ∂ν - ∫ y, f y ∂ν := by
572    have e : ∀ y, max (lam * g y - 1) 0 = lam * g y - f y := by
573      intro y
574      simp only [hf]
575      rcases le_total (lam * g y) 1 with h | h
576      · rw [min_eq_left h, max_eq_right (by linarith)]; ring
577      · rw [min_eq_right h, max_eq_left (by linarith)]
578    simp_rw [e]
579    rw [integral_sub (hgi.const_mul lam) hfνi, integral_const_mul]
580  have hdiff : ∫ y, Fx y ∂P - ∫ y, F' y ∂P = n * ∫ y, max (lam * g y - 1) 0 ∂ν := by
581    rw [hEF, hEF', hmaxint]; ring
582  have hEFle : ∫ y, F' y ∂P ≤ ∫ y, Fx y ∂P :=
583    integral_mono (integrable_finset_sum _ fun k _ => hfi k) hFxi hF'le
584  have hmain := log_integral_exp_neg_add_le P Fx F' hFxm hF'm hF'0 hF'le hF'2
585  rw [← hcav] at hmain
586  have hexp : Real.exp (∫ y, F' y ∂P) ≤ Real.exp mb := by
587    refine Real.exp_le_exp.2 (hEFle.trans ?_)
588    rw [hEF]; exact hm
589  have hv0 : 0 ≤ variance F' P := variance_nonneg _ _
590  have hn0 : (0 : ℝ) ≤ n := Nat.cast_nonneg n
591  have h1 : Real.exp (∫ y, F' y ∂P) / 2 * variance F' P ≤
592      Real.exp mb / 2 * (2 * n * ∫ y, (f y) ^ 2 ∂ν) := by
593    have hv : variance F' P ≤ 2 * n * ∫ y, (f y) ^ 2 ∂ν :=
594      hvar.trans (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hvar2 (by positivity))
595    calc Real.exp (∫ y, F' y ∂P) / 2 * variance F' P
596        ≤ Real.exp mb / 2 * variance F' P := by gcongr
597      _ ≤ _ := by gcongr
598  rw [← hEF]
599  have e2 : Real.exp mb / 2 * (2 * n * ∫ y, (f y) ^ 2 ∂ν) = Real.exp mb * (n * ∫ y, (f y) ^ 2 ∂ν) := by ring
600  have : Real.exp mb * (n * ∫ y, (f y) ^ 2 ∂ν) + n * ∫ y, max (lam * g y - 1) 0 ∂ν =
601      Real.exp mb * (n * ∫ y, (min (lam * V.q y x) 1) ^ 2 ∂ν) + n * ∫ y, max (lam * V.q y x - 1) 0 ∂ν := rfl
602  rw [← this]
603  linarith [hmain, h1, hdiff, e2]
604
605end CavD
606
607section Kernel
608
609variable {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ}
610
611/-- Volume of a small Euclidean ball on the torus. -/
612theorem volume_eucBall_le {R : ℝ} (hR : 0 ≤ R) :
613    (volume : Measure (Torus d)) {z : Torus d | Torus.eucDist z 0 < R} ≤ ENNReal.ofReal ((2 * R) ^ d) := by
614  have hmeas : MeasurableSet {z : Torus d | Torus.eucDist z 0 < R} := by
615    have : Continuous fun z : Torus d => Torus.eucDist z 0 := by
616      unfold Torus.eucDist
617      refine Real.continuous_sqrt.comp (continuous_finsetSum _ fun i _ => ?_)
618      refine (continuous_norm.comp ((continuous_apply i).sub continuous_const)).pow 2
619    exact measurableSet_lt this.measurable measurable_const
620  have h1 := KernelAux.lintegral_torus_box (d := d) ({z : Torus d | Torus.eucDist z 0 < R}.indicator 1)
621  rw [lintegral_indicator_one hmeas] at h1
622  rw [h1]
623  have h2 : ∀ y ∈ KernelAux.Box d, ({z : Torus d | Torus.eucDist z 0 < R}.indicator (1 : Torus d → ℝ≥0∞))
624      (KernelAux.boxPt y) ≤ (Set.pi Set.univ fun _ : Fin d => Set.Ioo (-R) R).indicator 1 y := by
625    intro y hy
626    by_cases h : Torus.eucDist (KernelAux.boxPt y) 0 < R
627    · have hmem : ∀ i, y i ∈ Set.Ioo (-R) R := by
628        intro i
629        have hb := KernelAux.Box_abs_le hy
630        rw [eucDist_boxPt hb] at h
631        have : |y i| ≤ Real.sqrt (∑ i, (y i) ^ 2) := by
632          rw [← Real.sqrt_sq_eq_abs]
633          exact Real.sqrt_le_sqrt (Finset.single_le_sum (f := fun i => (y i) ^ 2) (fun i _ => sq_nonneg _)
634            (Finset.mem_univ i))
635        have := lt_of_le_of_lt this h
636        exact ⟨by linarith [neg_abs_le (y i)], by linarith [le_abs_self (y i)]⟩
637      have hm : y ∈ Set.pi Set.univ fun _ : Fin d => Set.Ioo (-R) R := fun i _ => hmem i
638      simp [Set.indicator, h, hm]
639    · simp [Set.indicator, h]
640  calc ∫⁻ y in KernelAux.Box d, ({z : Torus d | Torus.eucDist z 0 < R}.indicator (1 : Torus d → ℝ≥0∞))
641        (KernelAux.boxPt y)
642      ≤ ∫⁻ y in KernelAux.Box d, (Set.pi Set.univ fun _ : Fin d => Set.Ioo (-R) R).indicator 1 y :=
643        setLIntegral_mono' (KernelAux.measurableSet_Box d) h2
644    _ ≤ ∫⁻ y, (Set.pi Set.univ fun _ : Fin d => Set.Ioo (-R) R).indicator 1 y :=
645        setLIntegral_le_lintegral _ _
646    _ = volume (Set.pi Set.univ fun _ : Fin d => Set.Ioo (-R) R) := by
647        rw [lintegral_indicator_one]
648        exact MeasurableSet.univ_pi fun _ => measurableSet_Ioo
649    _ = ENNReal.ofReal ((2 * R) ^ d) := by
650        rw [Real.volume_pi_Ioo]
651        simp only [Finset.prod_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin]
652        rw [ENNReal.ofReal_pow (by linarith)]
653        congr 2
654        ring
655
656
657/-- Exponent of the weak-`L^p` bound of the singular kernel: `p = d/s` (`s > 0`), `p = 3` (`s = 0`). -/
658def pOf (d : ℕ) (s : ℝ) : ℝ := if s = 0 then 3 else (d : ℝ) / s
659
660theorem one_lt_pOf (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) : 1 < pOf d s := by
661  unfold pOf
662  split_ifs with h
663  · norm_num
664  · have hs : 0 < s := lt_of_le_of_ne hs0 (Ne.symm h)
665    rw [lt_div_iff₀ hs]; linarith
666
667theorem eucDist_le_half_sqrt (z : Torus d) : Torus.eucDist z 0 ≤ Real.sqrt d / 2 := by
668  unfold Torus.eucDist
669  have h1 : ∀ i, ‖z i - (0 : Torus d) i‖ ^ 2 ≤ 1 / 4 := by
670    intro i
671    have : ‖z i - (0 : Torus d) i‖ ≤ 1 / 2 := by
672      have := AddCircle.norm_le_half_period (p := (1 : ℝ)) (x := z i - (0 : Torus d) i) one_ne_zero
673      simpa using this
674    nlinarith [norm_nonneg (z i - (0 : Torus d) i)]
675  have h2 : ∑ i, ‖z i - (0 : Torus d) i‖ ^ 2 ≤ (d : ℝ) / 4 := by
676    calc ∑ i, ‖z i - (0 : Torus d) i‖ ^ 2 ≤ ∑ _i : Fin d, (1 / 4 : ℝ) := Finset.sum_le_sum fun i _ => h1 i
677      _ = (d : ℝ) / 4 := by simp; ring
678  calc Real.sqrt (∑ i, ‖z i - (0 : Torus d) i‖ ^ 2) ≤ Real.sqrt ((d : ℝ) / 4) := Real.sqrt_le_sqrt h2
679    _ = Real.sqrt d / 2 := by
680        rw [Real.sqrt_div (Nat.cast_nonneg d)]
681        have : Real.sqrt 4 = 2 := by
682          rw [show (4 : ℝ) = 2 ^ 2 by norm_num]; exact Real.sqrt_sq (by norm_num)
683        rw [this]
684
685
686theorem one_le_singEnvelope'' {s e : ℝ} (he : 0 ≤ e) : 1 ≤ singEnvelope s e := one_le_singEnvelope' he
687
688theorem eucDist_nonneg_aux (z : Torus d) : 0 ≤ Torus.eucDist z 0 := Real.sqrt_nonneg _
689
690theorem eucDist_pos_of_ne {z : Torus d} (hz : z ≠ 0) : 0 < Torus.eucDist z 0 := by
691  rcases (eucDist_nonneg_aux z).eq_or_lt with h | h
692  · exfalso
693    apply hz
694    unfold Torus.eucDist at h
695    have h0 := (Real.sqrt_eq_zero (Finset.sum_nonneg fun i _ => sq_nonneg _)).1 h.symm
696    funext i
697    have := (Finset.sum_eq_zero_iff_of_nonneg (fun i _ => sq_nonneg (‖z i - (0 : Torus d) i‖))).1 h0 i
698      (Finset.mem_univ i)
699    have h1 : ‖z i - (0 : Torus d) i‖ = 0 := pow_eq_zero_iff (two_ne_zero) |>.1 this
700    simpa using h1
701  · exact h
702
703/-- **Weak-`L^p` bound** for a kernel with the singular envelope `q ≤ B (1 + |x|^{-s})`
704(`1 + |log|x||` for `s = 0`): `|{q > r}| ≤ K₀ r^{-p}` with `p = pOf d s`. -/
705theorem weakLp_of_envelope (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {B : ℝ} (hB : 0 ≤ B)
706    {q : Torus d → ℝ} (hq : ∀ x, x ≠ 0 → q x ≤ B * singEnvelope s (Torus.eucDist x 0)) :
707    ∃ K0 : ℝ, 0 < K0 ∧ ∀ r : ℝ, 0 < r →
708      (volume : Measure (Torus d)) {z : Torus d | r < q z} ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (K0 * r ^ (-(pOf d s))) := by
709  set B' := B + 1 with hB'
710  have hB'pos : 0 < B' := by linarith
711  have hq' : ∀ x, x ≠ 0 → q x ≤ B' * singEnvelope s (Torus.eucDist x 0) := fun x hx =>
712    (hq x hx).trans (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (by linarith) (by
713      have := one_le_singEnvelope' (s := s) (eucDist_nonneg_aux x); linarith))
714  have hp1 := one_lt_pOf hd hs0 hsd
715  set p := pOf d s with hp
716  have hp0 : 0 < p := by linarith
717  have hsub : ∀ (R : ℝ), 0 ≤ R → ∀ r : ℝ, (∀ z : Torus d, z ≠ 0 → r < q z → Torus.eucDist z 0 < R) →
718      (volume : Measure (Torus d)) {z : Torus d | r < q z} ≤ ENNReal.ofReal ((2 * R) ^ d) := by
719    intro R hR r h
720    calc (volume : Measure (Torus d)) {z : Torus d | r < q z}
721        ≤ (volume : Measure (Torus d)) ({z : Torus d | Torus.eucDist z 0 < R} ∪ {0}) := by
722          refine measure_mono fun z hz => ?_
723          by_cases hz0 : z = 0
724          · exact Or.inr hz0
725          · exact Or.inl (h z hz0 hz)
726      _ ≤ (volume : Measure (Torus d)) {z : Torus d | Torus.eucDist z 0 < R} +
727          (volume : Measure (Torus d)) {0} := measure_union_le _ _
728      _ = (volume : Measure (Torus d)) {z : Torus d | Torus.eucDist z 0 < R} := by
729          rw [volume_singleton_torus (by omega : 1 ≤ d) 0, add_zero]
730      _ ≤ _ := volume_eucBall_le hR
731  by_cases hs : s = 0
732  · have hp3 : p = 3 := by rw [hp]; unfold pOf; simp [hs]
733    set L₁ := |Real.log (Real.sqrt d / 2)| with hL₁
734    set r₁ := 2 * B' * (1 + L₁) with hr₁
735    have hL₁0 : 0 ≤ L₁ := abs_nonneg _
736    have hr₁pos : 0 < r₁ := by positivity
737    have hd0 : (0 : ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast (by omega : 0 < d)
738    set C₃ : ℝ := (3 : ℝ) ^ (3 : ℝ) * Real.exp (-3) with hC₃
739    refine ⟨r₁ ^ 3 + 2 ^ d * C₃ * (2 * B' / d) ^ 3, by positivity, fun r hr => ?_⟩
740    rw [hp3]
741    by_cases hr2 : r < r₁
742    · calc (volume : Measure (Torus d)) {z : Torus d | r < q z} ≤ 1 := prob_le_one
743        _ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal ((r₁ ^ 3 + 2 ^ d * C₃ * (2 * B' / d) ^ 3) * r ^ (-(3 : ℝ))) := by
744          rw [← ENNReal.ofReal_one]
745          refine ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal ?_
746          have h1 : (1 : ℝ) ≤ (r₁ / r) ^ 3 := one_le_pow₀ (by rw [le_div_iff₀ hr]; linarith)
747          rw [div_pow] at h1
748          rw [one_le_div (by positivity)] at h1
749          rw [Real.rpow_neg hr.le, ← div_eq_mul_inv]
750          have e3 : r ^ (3 : ℝ) = r ^ 3 := by exact_mod_cast Real.rpow_natCast r 3
751          rw [e3, le_div_iff₀ (by positivity)]
752          have : 0 ≤ 2 ^ d * C₃ * (2 * B' / d) ^ 3 := by positivity
753          linarith
754    · have hr2 := not_lt.1 hr2
755      set E0 := Real.exp (-(r / (2 * B'))) with hE0
756      have hE0pos : 0 < E0 := Real.exp_pos _
757      have key := hsub E0 hE0pos.le r (fun z hz hrz => by
758        by_contra hcon
759        have hcon := not_lt.1 hcon
760        have he := eucDist_pos_of_ne hz
761        have h5 := hq' z hz
762        have h6 : singEnvelope s (Torus.eucDist z 0) = 1 + |Real.log (Torus.eucDist z 0)| := by
763          unfold singEnvelope; simp [hs]
764        rw [h6] at h5
765        have hr2B : 2 * B' ≤ r := by
766          have : 2 * B' ≤ r₁ := by rw [hr₁]; nlinarith
767          linarith
768        by_cases he1 : 1 ≤ Torus.eucDist z 0
769        · have hlog : |Real.log (Torus.eucDist z 0)| ≤ L₁ := by
770            rw [abs_of_nonneg (Real.log_nonneg he1)]
771            have : Real.log (Torus.eucDist z 0) ≤ Real.log (Real.sqrt d / 2) :=
772              Real.log_le_log he (eucDist_le_half_sqrt z)
773            exact this.trans (le_abs_self _)
774          have : q z ≤ B' * (1 + L₁) := h5.trans (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (by linarith) hB'pos.le)
775          nlinarith
776        · have he1' := not_le.1 he1
777          have hlog : |Real.log (Torus.eucDist z 0)| ≤ r / (2 * B') := by
778            rw [abs_of_neg (Real.log_neg he he1')]
779            have : -(r / (2 * B')) ≤ Real.log (Torus.eucDist z 0) := by
780              rw [← Real.log_exp (-(r / (2 * B')))]
781              exact Real.log_le_log hE0pos hcon
782            linarith
783          have : q z ≤ B' * (1 + r / (2 * B')) :=
784            h5.trans (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (by linarith) hB'pos.le)
785          have e : B' * (1 + r / (2 * B')) = B' + r / 2 := by field_simp
786          linarith)
787      refine key.trans (ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal ?_)
788      have hv : 0 < (d : ℝ) * r / (2 * B') := by positivity
789      have h1 := KernelAux.exp_neg_le_rpow (q := (3 : ℝ)) (by norm_num) hv
790      have e1 : (2 * E0) ^ d = 2 ^ d * Real.exp (-((d : ℝ) * r / (2 * B'))) := by
791        rw [mul_pow, hE0, ← Real.exp_nat_mul]
792        congr 2
793        field_simp
794      rw [e1]
795      have e3 : r ^ (-(3 : ℝ)) = (r ^ 3)⁻¹ := by
796        rw [Real.rpow_neg hr.le]; congr 1; exact_mod_cast Real.rpow_natCast r 3
797      have e4 : ((d : ℝ) * r / (2 * B')) ^ (-(3 : ℝ)) = (2 * B' / d) ^ 3 * (r ^ 3)⁻¹ := by
798        rw [Real.rpow_neg hv.le]
799        have : (((d : ℝ) * r / (2 * B')) ^ (3 : ℝ)) = ((d : ℝ) * r / (2 * B')) ^ 3 := by
800          exact_mod_cast Real.rpow_natCast _ 3
801        rw [this]
802        field_simp
803      rw [e4] at h1
804      rw [e3]
805      have hfin : 2 ^ d * Real.exp (-((d : ℝ) * r / (2 * B'))) ≤
806          2 ^ d * (C₃ * ((2 * B' / d) ^ 3 * (r ^ 3)⁻¹)) :=
807        mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h1 (by positivity)
808      refine hfin.trans ?_
809      have : 0 ≤ (r ^ 3)⁻¹ := by positivity
810      have : 0 ≤ 2 ^ d * C₃ * (2 * B' / d) ^ 3 * (r ^ 3)⁻¹ := by positivity
811      nlinarith [pow_nonneg hr.le 3, mul_nonneg (pow_nonneg hr₁pos.le 3) (inv_nonneg.2 (pow_nonneg hr.le 3))]
812  · have hspos : 0 < s := lt_of_le_of_ne hs0 (Ne.symm hs)
813    have hpdef : p = (d : ℝ) / s := by rw [hp]; unfold pOf; simp [hs]
814    refine ⟨2 ^ d * (2 * B') ^ p, by positivity, fun r hr => ?_⟩
815    by_cases hr2 : r < 2 * B'
816    · calc (volume : Measure (Torus d)) {z : Torus d | r < q z} ≤ 1 := prob_le_one
817        _ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (2 ^ d * (2 * B') ^ p * r ^ (-p)) := by
818          rw [← ENNReal.ofReal_one]
819          refine ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal ?_
820          have h1 : 1 ≤ ((2 * B') / r) ^ p := Real.one_le_rpow (by rw [le_div_iff₀ hr]; linarith) hp0.le
821          rw [Real.div_rpow (by positivity) hr.le] at h1
822          rw [one_le_div (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hr p)] at h1
823          have h2 : (1 : ℝ) ≤ 2 ^ d := one_le_pow₀ (by norm_num)
824          rw [Real.rpow_neg hr.le, ← div_eq_mul_inv]
825          calc (1 : ℝ) ≤ (2 * B') ^ p / r ^ p := by rw [le_div_iff₀ (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hr p)]; linarith
826            _ ≤ 2 ^ d * (2 * B') ^ p / r ^ p := by
827              gcongr
828              nlinarith [Real.rpow_pos_of_pos (show 0 < 2 * B' by linarith) p]
829    · have hr2 := not_lt.1 hr2
830      set R := ((2 * B') / r) ^ (1 / s) with hR
831      have hRpos : 0 < R := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos (by positivity) _
832      have key := hsub R hRpos.le r (fun z hz hrz => by
833        by_contra hcon
834        have hcon := not_lt.1 hcon
835        have he := eucDist_pos_of_ne hz
836        have h3 : Torus.eucDist z 0 ^ (-s) ≤ R ^ (-s) :=
837          Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_nonpos hRpos hcon (by linarith)
838        have h4 : R ^ (-s) = r / (2 * B') := by
839          rw [hR, ← Real.rpow_mul (by positivity)]
840          have : 1 / s * -s = -1 := by field_simp
841          rw [this, Real.rpow_neg_one, inv_div]
842        have h5 := hq' z hz
843        have h6 : singEnvelope s (Torus.eucDist z 0) = 1 + Torus.eucDist z 0 ^ (-s) := by
844          unfold singEnvelope; simp [hs]
845        rw [h6] at h5
846        have h7 : r < B' * (1 + r / (2 * B')) := by
847          calc r < q z := hrz
848            _ ≤ B' * (1 + Torus.eucDist z 0 ^ (-s)) := h5
849            _ ≤ B' * (1 + r / (2 * B')) := by
850              rw [← h4]; gcongr
851        have : B' * (1 + r / (2 * B')) = B' + r / 2 := by field_simp
852        linarith)
853      refine key.trans (ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal (le_of_eq ?_))
854      rw [mul_pow, hR]
855      have e1 : (((2 * B') / r) ^ (1 / s)) ^ d = ((2 * B') / r) ^ p := by
856        rw [← Real.rpow_natCast, ← Real.rpow_mul (by positivity), hpdef]
857        congr 1
858        field_simp
859      rw [e1, Real.div_rpow (by positivity) hr.le, Real.rpow_neg hr.le, div_eq_mul_inv]
860      ring
861

863section Layer
864
865variable {q : Torus d → ℝ}
866
867/-- Second moment of the truncated kernel via the distribution function. -/
868theorem lintegral_minsq_le (hq : Measurable q) (hq0 : ∀ z, 0 ≤ q z) {K : ℝ} (hK : 1 ≤ K) {p K0 : ℝ}
869    (hp : 1 < p) (hK0 : 0 < K0)
870    (hw : ∀ r : ℝ, 0 < r → (volume : Measure (Torus d)) {z : Torus d | r < q z} ≤
871      ENNReal.ofReal (K0 * r ^ (-p))) :
872    ∫⁻ z, ENNReal.ofReal (min (q z) K ^ 2) ≤
873      ENNReal.ofReal (1 + 2 * K0 * ∫ t in (1 : ℝ)..K, t ^ (1 - p)) := by
874  have hf : Measurable fun z => min (q z) K := hq.min measurable_const
875  have hf0 : ∀ z, 0 ≤ min (q z) K := fun z => le_min (hq0 z) (by linarith)
876  have e1 : ∀ z, ENNReal.ofReal (min (q z) K ^ 2) =
877      ENNReal.ofReal (∫ t in (0 : ℝ)..min (q z) K, 2 * t) := by
878    intro z
879    congr 1
880    rw [intervalIntegral.integral_const_mul, integral_id]
881    ring
882  simp_rw [e1]
883  rw [lintegral_comp_eq_lintegral_meas_lt_mul (volume : Measure (Torus d)) (f := fun z => min (q z) K)
884    (Filter.Eventually.of_forall hf0) hf.aemeasurable (g := fun t => 2 * t)
885    (fun t _ => (continuous_const.mul continuous_id).intervalIntegrable _ _)
886    ((ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioi).2 (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun t ht => by
887      have : (0 : ℝ) < t := ht
888      show 0 ≤ 2 * t
889      linarith))]
890  set H : ℝ → ℝ≥0∞ := fun t => (Set.Ioc (0 : ℝ) 1).indicator (fun t => ENNReal.ofReal (2 * t)) t +
891    (Set.Ioc (1 : ℝ) K).indicator (fun t => ENNReal.ofReal (2 * K0 * t ^ (1 - p))) t with hH
892  have hpt : ∀ t ∈ Set.Ioi (0 : ℝ), (volume : Measure (Torus d)) {a | t < min (q a) K} *
893      ENNReal.ofReal (2 * t) ≤ H t := by
894    intro t ht
895    have ht0 : 0 < t := ht
896    by_cases ht1 : t ≤ 1
897    · have hm : t ∈ Set.Ioc (0 : ℝ) 1 := ⟨ht0, ht1⟩
898      have hn : t ∉ Set.Ioc (1 : ℝ) K := fun h => absurd h.1 (not_lt.2 ht1)
899      simp only [hH, Set.indicator_of_mem hm, Set.indicator_of_notMem hn, add_zero]
900      calc (volume : Measure (Torus d)) {a | t < min (q a) K} * ENNReal.ofReal (2 * t)
901          ≤ 1 * ENNReal.ofReal (2 * t) := by gcongr; exact prob_le_one
902        _ = _ := one_mul _
903    · have ht1' := not_le.1 ht1
904      by_cases htK : t ≤ K
905      · have hm : t ∈ Set.Ioc (1 : ℝ) K := ⟨ht1', htK⟩
906        have hn : t ∉ Set.Ioc (0 : ℝ) 1 := fun h => absurd h.2 ht1
907        simp only [hH, Set.indicator_of_mem hm, Set.indicator_of_notMem hn, zero_add]
908        calc (volume : Measure (Torus d)) {a | t < min (q a) K} * ENNReal.ofReal (2 * t)
909            ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (K0 * t ^ (-p)) * ENNReal.ofReal (2 * t) := by
910              gcongr
911              refine le_trans (measure_mono fun a ha => ?_) (hw t ht0)
912              have ha' : t < min (q a) K := ha
913              exact lt_of_lt_of_le ha' (min_le_left _ _)
914          _ = ENNReal.ofReal (2 * K0 * t ^ (1 - p)) := by
915              rw [← ENNReal.ofReal_mul (by positivity)]
916              congr 1
917              rw [show (1 - p) = 1 + (-p) by ring, Real.rpow_add ht0, Real.rpow_one]
918              ring
919      · have htK' := not_le.1 htK
920        have hempty : {a : Torus d | t < min (q a) K} = ∅ := by
921          ext a
922          simp only [Set.mem_setOf_eq, Set.mem_empty_iff_false, iff_false, not_lt]
923          exact (min_le_right _ _).trans htK'.le
924        rw [hempty]
925        simp
926  refine (lintegral_mono_ae ((ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioi).2 (Filter.Eventually.of_forall hpt))).trans ?_
927  have hmeas1 : MeasurableSet (Set.Ioc (0 : ℝ) 1) := measurableSet_Ioc
928  have hmeas2 : MeasurableSet (Set.Ioc (1 : ℝ) K) := measurableSet_Ioc
929  have hf1 : Measurable fun t : ℝ => ENNReal.ofReal (2 * t) :=
930    ENNReal.measurable_ofReal.comp (measurable_const.mul measurable_id)
931  have hf2 : Measurable fun t : ℝ => ENNReal.ofReal (2 * K0 * t ^ (1 - p)) :=
932    ENNReal.measurable_ofReal.comp (measurable_const.mul (measurable_id.pow_const _))
933  simp only [hH]
934  rw [lintegral_add_left (hf1.indicator hmeas1), lintegral_indicator hmeas1, lintegral_indicator hmeas2,
935    Measure.restrict_restrict hmeas1, Measure.restrict_restrict hmeas2]
936  have i1 : Set.Ioc (0 : ℝ) 1 ∩ Set.Ioi 0 = Set.Ioc 0 1 := Set.inter_eq_left.2 fun x hx => hx.1
937  have i2 : Set.Ioc (1 : ℝ) K ∩ Set.Ioi 0 = Set.Ioc 1 K := Set.inter_eq_left.2 fun x hx => by
938    exact lt_trans zero_lt_one hx.1
939  rw [i1, i2]
940  have j1 : ∫⁻ t in Set.Ioc (0 : ℝ) 1, ENNReal.ofReal (2 * t) = ENNReal.ofReal 1 := by
941    rw [← ofReal_integral_eq_lintegral_ofReal]
942    · congr 1
943      rw [← intervalIntegral.integral_of_le zero_le_one, intervalIntegral.integral_const_mul, integral_id]
944      norm_num
945    · exact (continuous_const.mul continuous_id).integrableOn_Ioc
946    · exact (ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioc).2 (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun t ht => by
947        have : 0 < t := ht.1
948        show 0 ≤ 2 * t
949        linarith)
950  have hcont : ContinuousOn (fun t : ℝ => 2 * K0 * t ^ (1 - p)) (Set.Icc 1 K) :=
951    continuousOn_const.mul (ContinuousOn.rpow_const continuousOn_id fun x hx => Or.inl (by
952      have : (1 : ℝ) ≤ x := hx.1
953      exact ne_of_gt (lt_of_lt_of_le zero_lt_one this)))
954  have j2 : ∫⁻ t in Set.Ioc (1 : ℝ) K, ENNReal.ofReal (2 * K0 * t ^ (1 - p)) =
955      ENNReal.ofReal (2 * K0 * ∫ t in (1 : ℝ)..K, t ^ (1 - p)) := by
956    rw [← intervalIntegral.integral_const_mul, ← ofReal_integral_eq_lintegral_ofReal]
957    · congr 1
958      rw [intervalIntegral.integral_of_le hK]
959    · exact hcont.integrableOn_Icc.mono_set Set.Ioc_subset_Icc_self
960    · exact (ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioc).2 (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun t ht => by
961        have : 0 < t := lt_trans zero_lt_one ht.1
962        positivity)
963  rw [j1, j2, ← ENNReal.ofReal_add zero_le_one (by
964    have : 0 ≤ ∫ t in (1 : ℝ)..K, t ^ (1 - p) :=
965      intervalIntegral.integral_nonneg hK fun t ht => Real.rpow_nonneg (by linarith [ht.1]) _
966    positivity)]
967
968/-- Tail of the kernel above level `K` via the distribution function. -/
969theorem lintegral_tail_le (hq : Measurable q) (hq0 : ∀ z, 0 ≤ q z) {K : ℝ} (hK : 0 < K) {p K0 : ℝ}
970    (hp : 1 < p) (hK0 : 0 < K0)
971    (hw : ∀ r : ℝ, 0 < r → (volume : Measure (Torus d)) {z : Torus d | r < q z} ≤
972      ENNReal.ofReal (K0 * r ^ (-p))) :
973    ∫⁻ z, ENNReal.ofReal (q z - K) ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (K0 * K ^ (1 - p) * (p / (p - 1))) := by
974  have hf : Measurable fun z => max (q z - K) 0 := (hq.sub_const K).max measurable_const
975  have e1 : ∀ z, ENNReal.ofReal (q z - K) = ENNReal.ofReal (max (q z - K) 0) := by
976    intro z
977    rw [ENNReal.ofReal_max]
978    simp
979  simp_rw [e1]
980  rw [lintegral_eq_lintegral_meas_lt (volume : Measure (Torus d)) (f := fun z => max (q z - K) 0)
981    (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun z => le_max_right _ _) hf.aemeasurable]
982  have hpt : ∀ t ∈ Set.Ioi (0 : ℝ), (volume : Measure (Torus d)) {a | t < max (q a - K) 0} ≤
983      (Set.Ioc (0 : ℝ) K).indicator (fun _ => ENNReal.ofReal (K0 * K ^ (-p))) t +
984        (Set.Ioi K).indicator (fun t => ENNReal.ofReal (K0 * t ^ (-p))) t := by
985    intro t ht
986    have ht0 : 0 < t := ht
987    have hsub : {a : Torus d | t < max (q a - K) 0} ⊆ {a | K + t < q a} := by
988      intro a ha
989      have ha' : t < max (q a - K) 0 := ha
990      rcases lt_max_iff.1 ha' with h | h
991      · show K + t < q a
992        linarith
993      · exact absurd h (not_lt.2 ht0.le)
994    have hKt : 0 < K + t := by linarith
995    by_cases htK : t ≤ K
996    · have hm : t ∈ Set.Ioc (0 : ℝ) K := ⟨ht0, htK⟩
997      have hn : t ∉ Set.Ioi K := fun h => absurd h (not_lt.2 htK)
998      rw [Set.indicator_of_mem hm, Set.indicator_of_notMem hn, add_zero]
999      refine (measure_mono hsub).trans ((hw _ hKt).trans (ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal ?_))
1000      refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ hK0.le
1001      exact Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_nonpos hK (by linarith) (by linarith)
1002    · have htK' := not_le.1 htK
1003      have hm : t ∈ Set.Ioi K := htK'
1004      have hn : t ∉ Set.Ioc (0 : ℝ) K := fun h => absurd h.2 htK
1005      rw [Set.indicator_of_mem hm, Set.indicator_of_notMem hn, zero_add]
1006      refine (measure_mono hsub).trans ((hw _ hKt).trans (ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal ?_))
1007      refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ hK0.le
1008      exact Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_nonpos ht0 (by linarith) (by linarith)
1009  refine (lintegral_mono_ae ((ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioi).2 (Filter.Eventually.of_forall hpt))).trans ?_
1010  have hmeas1 : MeasurableSet (Set.Ioc (0 : ℝ) K) := measurableSet_Ioc
1011  have hmeas2 : MeasurableSet (Set.Ioi K) := measurableSet_Ioi
1012  have hf2 : Measurable fun t : ℝ => ENNReal.ofReal (K0 * t ^ (-p)) :=
1013    ENNReal.measurable_ofReal.comp (measurable_const.mul (measurable_id.pow_const _))
1014  rw [lintegral_add_left (measurable_const.indicator hmeas1), lintegral_indicator hmeas1,
1015    lintegral_indicator hmeas2, Measure.restrict_restrict hmeas1, Measure.restrict_restrict hmeas2]
1016  have i1 : Set.Ioc (0 : ℝ) K ∩ Set.Ioi 0 = Set.Ioc 0 K := Set.inter_eq_left.2 fun x hx => hx.1
1017  have i2 : Set.Ioi K ∩ Set.Ioi (0 : ℝ) = Set.Ioi K := Set.inter_eq_left.2 fun x hx => lt_trans hK hx
1018  rw [i1, i2, setLIntegral_const, Real.volume_Ioc]
1019  have hint : IntegrableOn (fun t : ℝ => t ^ (-p)) (Set.Ioi K) :=
1020    integrableOn_Ioi_rpow_of_lt (by linarith) hK
1021  have hI : ∫⁻ t in Set.Ioi K, ENNReal.ofReal (K0 * t ^ (-p)) =
1022      ENNReal.ofReal (K0 * (K ^ (1 - p) / (p - 1))) := by
1023    rw [← ofReal_integral_eq_lintegral_ofReal (hint.const_mul K0)
1024      ((ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioi).2 (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun t ht => by
1025        have : 0 < t := lt_trans hK ht
1026        positivity))]
1027    congr 1
1028    rw [integral_const_mul, integral_Ioi_rpow_of_lt (by linarith) hK]
1029    have h1 : -p + 1 = 1 - p := by ring
1030    rw [h1]
1031    congr 1
1032    rw [neg_div, ← div_neg, neg_sub]
1033  rw [hI, ← ENNReal.ofReal_mul (by positivity), ← ENNReal.ofReal_add (by positivity) (by
1034    have : 0 < p - 1 := by linarith
1035    have := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hK (1 - p)
1036    positivity)]
1037  refine ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal (le_of_eq ?_)
1038  have e : K ^ (1 - p) = K * K ^ (-p) := by
1039    rw [show (1 - p) = 1 + (-p) by ring, Real.rpow_add hK, Real.rpow_one]
1040  have hp1 : p - 1 ≠ 0 := by linarith
1041  rw [sub_zero, e]
1042  field_simp
1043  ring
1044
1045end Layer
1046
1047end Kernel

1049section Rates

1051variable {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ}

1053
1054theorem one_lt_pOf_aux (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) : 1 < pOf d s := by
1055  unfold pOf
1056  split_ifs with h
1057  · norm_num
1058  · have hs : 0 < s := lt_of_le_of_ne hs0 (Ne.symm h)
1059    rw [lt_div_iff₀ hs]; linarith
1060
1061theorem pOf_cases (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
1062    (s < (d : ℝ) / 2 ∧ 2 < pOf d s) ∨ (s = (d : ℝ) / 2 ∧ pOf d s = 2) ∨
1063      ((d : ℝ) / 2 < s ∧ pOf d s = (d : ℝ) / s ∧ pOf d s < 2) := by
1064  have hd0 : (0 : ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast (by omega : 0 < d)
1065  by_cases hs : s = 0
1066  · left
1067    have hp3 : pOf d s = 3 := by unfold pOf; simp [hs]
1068    refine ⟨by rw [hs]; positivity, by rw [hp3]; norm_num⟩
1069  · have hspos : 0 < s := lt_of_le_of_ne hs0 (Ne.symm hs)
1070    have hpdef : pOf d s = (d : ℝ) / s := by unfold pOf; simp [hs]
1071    rcases lt_trichotomy s ((d : ℝ) / 2) with h | h | h
1072    · left
1073      refine ⟨h, ?_⟩
1074      rw [hpdef, lt_div_iff₀ hspos]; linarith
1075    · right; left
1076      refine ⟨h, ?_⟩
1077      rw [hpdef, h]; field_simp
1078    · right; right
1079      refine ⟨h, hpdef, ?_⟩
1080      rw [hpdef, div_lt_iff₀ hspos]; linarith
1081
1082/-- The growth function `g(K) = N δ_N`-profile: `1` (`s < d/2`), `log(eK)` (`s = d/2`), `K^{2-d/s}`. -/
1083def gFun (d : ℕ) (s : ℝ) (K : ℝ) : ℝ :=
1084  if s < (d : ℝ) / 2 then 1 else if s = (d : ℝ) / 2 then Real.log (Real.exp 1 * K) else K ^ (2 - (d : ℝ) / s)
1085
1086theorem deltaN_eq_gFun (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {N : ℕ} (hN : 1 ≤ N) :
1087    deltaN d s N = (N : ℝ)⁻¹ * gFun d s N := by
1088  have hN0 : (0 : ℝ) < N := by exact_mod_cast hN
1089  unfold deltaN gFun
1090  by_cases h1 : s < (d : ℝ) / 2
1091  · simp [h1]
1092  · by_cases h2 : s = (d : ℝ) / 2
1093    · simp [h2]
1094    · simp only [h1, h2, if_false]
1095      rw [show (2 : ℝ) - d / s = 1 + (1 - d / s) by ring, Real.rpow_add hN0, Real.rpow_one]
1096      field_simp
1097
1098theorem one_le_gFun (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {K : ℝ} (hK : 1 ≤ K) :
1099    1 ≤ gFun d s K := by
1100  unfold gFun
1101  have hK0 : 0 < K := by linarith
1102  split_ifs with h1 h2
1103  · exact le_rfl
1104  · have : Real.exp 1 ≤ Real.exp 1 * K := by nlinarith [Real.exp_pos 1]
1105    calc (1 : ℝ) = Real.log (Real.exp 1) := (Real.log_exp 1).symm
1106      _ ≤ _ := Real.log_le_log (Real.exp_pos 1) this
1107  · have h3 : (d : ℝ) / 2 < s := lt_of_le_of_ne (not_lt.1 h1) (Ne.symm h2)
1108    have hs : 0 < s := by
1109      have : (0 : ℝ) < d / 2 := by
1110        have : (0 : ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast (by omega : 0 < d)
1111        positivity
1112      linarith
1113    exact Real.one_le_rpow hK (by rw [sub_nonneg, div_le_iff₀ hs]; linarith)
1114
1115theorem integral_one_rpow_le (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {K : ℝ} (hK : 1 ≤ K) :
1116    (s < (d : ℝ) / 2 → ∫ t in (1 : ℝ)..K, t ^ (1 - pOf d s) ≤ 1 / (pOf d s - 2)) ∧
1117    (s = (d : ℝ) / 2 → ∫ t in (1 : ℝ)..K, t ^ (1 - pOf d s) = Real.log K) ∧
1118    ((d : ℝ) / 2 < s → ∫ t in (1 : ℝ)..K, t ^ (1 - pOf d s) ≤ K ^ (2 - pOf d s) / (2 - pOf d s)) := by
1119  have hK0 : 0 < K := by linarith
1120  have hcases := pOf_cases hd hs0 hsd
1121  refine ⟨fun h => ?_, fun h => ?_, fun h => ?_⟩
1122  · have hp : 2 < pOf d s := by
1123      rcases hcases with ⟨_, h2⟩ | ⟨h1, _⟩ | ⟨h1, _⟩
1124      · exact h2
1125      · linarith
1126      · linarith
1127    rw [integral_rpow (Or.inr ⟨by linarith, by
1128      rw [Set.mem_uIcc]; push Not; constructor <;> intro h <;> linarith⟩)]
1129    have : 1 - pOf d s + 1 = 2 - pOf d s := by ring
1130    rw [this]
1131    have h1 : (1 : ℝ) ^ (2 - pOf d s) = 1 := Real.one_rpow _
1132    rw [h1]
1133    have h2 : 0 < K ^ (2 - pOf d s) := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hK0 _
1134    have e : (K ^ (2 - pOf d s) - 1) / (2 - pOf d s) = (1 - K ^ (2 - pOf d s)) / (pOf d s - 2) := by
1135      rw [div_eq_div_iff (by linarith) (by linarith)]; ring
1136    rw [e]
1137    exact div_le_div_of_nonneg_right (by linarith) (by linarith)
1138  · have hp : pOf d s = 2 := by
1139      rcases hcases with ⟨h1, _⟩ | ⟨_, h2⟩ | ⟨h1, _⟩
1140      · linarith
1141      · exact h2
1142      · linarith
1143    rw [hp, show (1 : ℝ) - 2 = -1 by norm_num]
1144    simp_rw [Real.rpow_neg_one]
1145    rw [integral_inv_of_pos one_pos hK0, div_one]
1146  · have hp : pOf d s < 2 := by
1147      rcases hcases with ⟨h1, _⟩ | ⟨h1, _⟩ | ⟨_, _, h3⟩
1148      · linarith
1149      · linarith
1150      · exact h3
1151    have hp1 := one_lt_pOf_aux hd hs0 hsd
1152    rw [integral_rpow (Or.inl (by linarith))]
1153    have : 1 - pOf d s + 1 = 2 - pOf d s := by ring
1154    rw [this]
1155    have h1 : (1 : ℝ) ^ (2 - pOf d s) = 1 := Real.one_rpow _
1156    rw [h1]
1157    have : 0 < 2 - pOf d s := by linarith
1158    rw [div_le_div_iff_of_pos_right this]
1159    linarith
1160
1161theorem second_moment_gFun (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {K0 : ℝ} (hK0 : 0 < K0) :
1162    ∃ c₁ : ℝ, 0 < c₁ ∧ ∀ K : ℝ, 1 ≤ K →
1163      1 + 2 * K0 * ∫ t in (1 : ℝ)..K, t ^ (1 - pOf d s) ≤ c₁ * gFun d s K := by
1164  have hcases := pOf_cases hd hs0 hsd
1165  have hd0 : (0 : ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast (by omega : 0 < d)
1166  rcases hcases with ⟨h1, h2⟩ | ⟨h1, h2⟩ | ⟨h1, h2, h3⟩
1167  · refine ⟨1 + 2 * K0 / (pOf d s - 2), by have : 0 < pOf d s - 2 := by linarith
1168                                           positivity, fun K hK => ?_⟩
1169    have hI := (integral_one_rpow_le hd hs0 hsd hK).1 h1
1170    have hg : gFun d s K = 1 := by unfold gFun; simp [h1]
1171    rw [hg, mul_one]
1172    have : 0 < pOf d s - 2 := by linarith
1173    calc 1 + 2 * K0 * ∫ t in (1 : ℝ)..K, t ^ (1 - pOf d s) ≤ 1 + 2 * K0 * (1 / (pOf d s - 2)) := by gcongr
1174      _ = 1 + 2 * K0 / (pOf d s - 2) := by ring
1175  · refine ⟨1 + 2 * K0, by positivity, fun K hK => ?_⟩
1176    have hI := (integral_one_rpow_le hd hs0 hsd hK).2.1 h1
1177    have hg : gFun d s K = Real.log (Real.exp 1 * K) := by
1178      unfold gFun; simp [h1]
1179    have hK0 : 0 < K := by linarith
1180    rw [hg, hI, Real.log_mul (Real.exp_pos 1).ne' hK0.ne', Real.log_exp]
1181    have : 0 ≤ Real.log K := Real.log_nonneg hK
1182    nlinarith
1183  · refine ⟨1 + 2 * K0 / (2 - pOf d s), by have : 0 < 2 - pOf d s := by linarith
1184                                           positivity, fun K hK => ?_⟩
1185    have hI := (integral_one_rpow_le hd hs0 hsd hK).2.2 (by linarith)
1186    have hs : 0 < s := by linarith
1187    have hg : gFun d s K = K ^ (2 - pOf d s) := by
1188      unfold gFun
1189      have h4 : ¬ s < (d : ℝ) / 2 := by linarith
1190      have h5 : s ≠ (d : ℝ) / 2 := by linarith
1191      simp [h4, h5, h2]
1192    rw [hg]
1193    have hKp : 1 ≤ K ^ (2 - pOf d s) := Real.one_le_rpow hK (by linarith)
1194    have : 0 < 2 - pOf d s := by linarith
1195    calc 1 + 2 * K0 * ∫ t in (1 : ℝ)..K, t ^ (1 - pOf d s)
1196        ≤ 1 + 2 * K0 * (K ^ (2 - pOf d s) / (2 - pOf d s)) := by gcongr
1197      _ ≤ (1 + 2 * K0 / (2 - pOf d s)) * K ^ (2 - pOf d s) := by
1198        have : 2 * K0 * (K ^ (2 - pOf d s) / (2 - pOf d s)) = 2 * K0 / (2 - pOf d s) * K ^ (2 - pOf d s) := by
1199          ring
1200        rw [this]
1201        nlinarith
1202
1203theorem gFun_mul_le (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {c : ℝ} (hc : 0 < c) :
1204    ∃ c' : ℝ, 0 < c' ∧ ∀ K : ℝ, 1 ≤ K → 1 ≤ c * K → gFun d s (c * K) ≤ c' * gFun d s K := by
1205  by_cases h1 : s < (d : ℝ) / 2
1206  · refine ⟨1, one_pos, fun K hK hcK => ?_⟩
1207    unfold gFun; simp [h1]
1208  · by_cases h2 : s = (d : ℝ) / 2
1209    · refine ⟨1 + |Real.log c|, by positivity, fun K hK hcK => ?_⟩
1210      have hg : ∀ K', gFun d s K' = Real.log (Real.exp 1 * K') := fun K' => by unfold gFun; simp [h2]
1211      rw [hg, hg]
1212      have hK0 : 0 < K := by linarith
1213      have e : Real.log (Real.exp 1 * (c * K)) = Real.log (Real.exp 1 * K) + Real.log c := by
1214        rw [show Real.exp 1 * (c * K) = (Real.exp 1 * K) * c by ring,
1215          Real.log_mul (by positivity) hc.ne']
1216      have h3 : 1 ≤ Real.log (Real.exp 1 * K) := by
1217        rw [Real.log_mul (Real.exp_pos 1).ne' hK0.ne', Real.log_exp]
1218        linarith [Real.log_nonneg hK]
1219      rw [e]
1220      nlinarith [le_abs_self (Real.log c), abs_nonneg (Real.log c)]
1221    · have h3 : (d : ℝ) / 2 < s := lt_of_le_of_ne (not_lt.1 h1) (Ne.symm h2)
1222      refine ⟨c ^ (2 - (d : ℝ) / s), by positivity, fun K hK hcK => ?_⟩
1223      have hg : ∀ K', gFun d s K' = K' ^ (2 - (d : ℝ) / s) := fun K' => by unfold gFun; simp [h1, h2]
1224      rw [hg, hg, Real.mul_rpow hc.le (by linarith)]
1225
1226/-- `K^{1-p} ≤ K⁻¹ g(K)`. -/
1227theorem rpow_one_sub_le_gFun (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {K : ℝ} (hK : 1 ≤ K) :
1228    K ^ (1 - pOf d s) ≤ K⁻¹ * gFun d s K := by
1229  have hK0 : 0 < K := by linarith
1230  have hcases := pOf_cases hd hs0 hsd
1231  rcases hcases with ⟨h1, h2⟩ | ⟨h1, h2⟩ | ⟨h1, h2, h3⟩
1232  · have hg : gFun d s K = 1 := by unfold gFun; simp [h1]
1233    rw [hg, mul_one, ← Real.rpow_neg_one]
1234    exact Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_exponent_le hK (by linarith)
1235  · have hg : gFun d s K = Real.log (Real.exp 1 * K) := by unfold gFun; simp [h1]
1236    rw [hg, h2, Real.log_mul (Real.exp_pos 1).ne' hK0.ne', Real.log_exp]
1237    norm_num
1238    rw [Real.rpow_neg_one]
1239    have : 0 ≤ Real.log K := Real.log_nonneg hK
1240    have : K⁻¹ * (1 + Real.log K) = K⁻¹ + K⁻¹ * Real.log K := by ring
1241    have h4 : 0 ≤ K⁻¹ * Real.log K := by positivity
1242    linarith
1243  · have hs : 0 < s := by
1244      have : (0 : ℝ) < d / 2 := by
1245        have : (0 : ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast (by omega : 0 < d)
1246        positivity
1247      linarith
1248    have hg : gFun d s K = K ^ (2 - (d : ℝ) / s) := by
1249      unfold gFun
1250      have h4 : ¬ s < (d : ℝ) / 2 := by linarith
1251      have h5 : s ≠ (d : ℝ) / 2 := by linarith
1252      simp [h4, h5]
1253    rw [hg, ← h2, ← Real.rpow_neg_one, ← Real.rpow_add hK0]
1254    exact le_of_eq (by congr 1; ring)
1255

1258section Conv

1260variable {d : ℕ}

1262theorem enorm_sq_add_le (a b : ℝ≥0∞) : (a + b) ^ 2 ≤ 2 * (a ^ 2 + b ^ 2) := by
1263  have h := ENNReal.rpow_add_le_mul_rpow_add_rpow a b (p := 2) (by norm_num)
1264  have e : (2 : ℝ≥0∞) ^ ((2 : ℝ) - 1) = 2 := by norm_num
1265  rw [e] at h
1266  simpa [ENNReal.rpow_two] using h
1267
1268/-- **`L²` convolution estimate** (`A:eq:convolution` in `ℝ≥0∞` form): splitting the kernel at the
1269level `K` (`q = (q - K)₊ + min(q, K)`), Young's inequality gives
1270`‖q * w‖₂² ≤ 2 ‖(q-K)₊‖₁² ‖w‖₂² + 2 ‖w‖₁² ‖min(q,K)‖₂²`. -/
1271theorem conv_sq_le {q w : Torus d → ℝ} (hq : Measurable q) (hq0 : ∀ z, 0 ≤ q z) (hw : Measurable w)
1272    {K : ℝ} (hK : 0 ≤ K) :
1273    ∫⁻ x, ‖ResponseAux.cv q w x‖ₑ ^ 2 ≤
1274      2 * (((∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (q y - K)) ^ 2 * ∫⁻ y, ‖w y‖ₑ ^ 2) +
1275        ((∫⁻ y, ‖w y‖ₑ) ^ 2 * ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (min (q y) K ^ 2))) := by
1276  set q1 : Torus d → ℝ := fun z => max (q z - K) 0 with hq1
1277  set q2 : Torus d → ℝ := fun z => min (q z) K with hq2
1278  have hq1m : Measurable q1 := (hq.sub_const K).max measurable_const
1279  have hq2m : Measurable q2 := hq.min measurable_const
1280  have hq10 : ∀ z, 0 ≤ q1 z := fun z => le_max_right _ _
1281  have hq20 : ∀ z, 0 ≤ q2 z := fun z => le_min (hq0 z) hK
1282  have hsplit : ∀ z, q z = q1 z + q2 z := by
1283    intro z
1284    simp only [hq1, hq2]
1285    rcases le_total (q z) K with h | h
1286    · rw [max_eq_right (by linarith), min_eq_left h]; ring
1287    · rw [max_eq_left (by linarith), min_eq_right h]; ring
1288  have hen1 : ∀ z, ‖q1 z‖ₑ = ENNReal.ofReal (q z - K) := by
1289    intro z
1290    rw [Real.enorm_of_nonneg (hq10 z), hq1]
1291    simp only
1292    rw [ENNReal.ofReal_max]; simp
1293  have hen2 : ∀ z, ‖q2 z‖ₑ ^ 2 = ENNReal.ofReal (min (q z) K ^ 2) := by
1294    intro z
1295    rw [Real.enorm_of_nonneg (hq20 z), ← ENNReal.ofReal_pow (hq20 z)]
1296  have hpt : ∀ x, ‖ResponseAux.cv q w x‖ₑ ≤
1297      (∫⁻ y, ‖q1 (x - y)‖ₑ * ‖w y‖ₑ) + ∫⁻ y, ‖q2 (x - y)‖ₑ * ‖w y‖ₑ := by
1298    intro x
1299    have h1 : ‖ResponseAux.cv q w x‖ₑ ≤ ∫⁻ y, ‖q (x - y)‖ₑ * ‖w y‖ₑ := by
1300      refine (enorm_integral_le_lintegral_enorm _).trans (le_of_eq ?_)
1301      simp [enorm_mul, ResponseAux.cv]
1302    refine h1.trans (le_of_eq ?_)
1303    rw [← lintegral_add_left]
1304    · refine lintegral_congr fun y => ?_
1305      rw [← add_mul]
1306      congr 1
1307      rw [hsplit (x - y), Real.enorm_of_nonneg (add_nonneg (hq10 _) (hq20 _)),
1308        ENNReal.ofReal_add (hq10 _) (hq20 _), Real.enorm_of_nonneg (hq10 _),
1309        Real.enorm_of_nonneg (hq20 _)]
1310    · exact ((hq1m.comp (measurable_const.sub measurable_id)).enorm).mul hw.enorm
1311  have hE1 := ResponseAux.young_lintegral q1 w hq1m hw
1312  have hE2 : ∫⁻ x, (∫⁻ y, ‖q2 (x - y)‖ₑ * ‖w y‖ₑ) ^ 2 ≤ (∫⁻ y, ‖w y‖ₑ) ^ 2 * ∫⁻ y, ‖q2 y‖ₑ ^ 2 := by
1313    have := ResponseAux.young_lintegral w q2 hw hq2m
1314    refine le_trans (le_of_eq ?_) this
1315    refine lintegral_congr fun x => ?_
1316    congr 1
1317    -- commute the convolution
1318    have := ResponseAux.lintegral_sub_left (fun u => ‖w u‖ₑ * ‖q2 (x - u)‖ₑ) x
1319    simp only [sub_sub_cancel] at this
1320    rw [this]
1321    refine lintegral_congr fun y => ?_
1322    rw [mul_comm]
1323  calc ∫⁻ x, ‖ResponseAux.cv q w x‖ₑ ^ 2
1324      ≤ ∫⁻ x, 2 * (((∫⁻ y, ‖q1 (x - y)‖ₑ * ‖w y‖ₑ) ^ 2) + ((∫⁻ y, ‖q2 (x - y)‖ₑ * ‖w y‖ₑ) ^ 2)) :=
1325        lintegral_mono fun x => (pow_le_pow_left' (hpt x) 2).trans (enorm_sq_add_le _ _)
1326    _ = 2 * ((∫⁻ x, (∫⁻ y, ‖q1 (x - y)‖ₑ * ‖w y‖ₑ) ^ 2) + (∫⁻ x, (∫⁻ y, ‖q2 (x - y)‖ₑ * ‖w y‖ₑ) ^ 2)) := by
1327        have hm1 : Measurable fun x : Torus d => (∫⁻ y, ‖q1 (x - y)‖ₑ * ‖w y‖ₑ) ^ 2 := by
1328          refine Measurable.pow_const ?_ 2
1329          exact Measurable.lintegral_prod_right' (f := fun p : Torus d × Torus d =>
1330            ‖q1 (p.1 - p.2)‖ₑ * ‖w p.2‖ₑ)
1331            (((hq1m.comp (measurable_fst.sub measurable_snd)).enorm).mul (hw.comp measurable_snd).enorm)
1332        rw [lintegral_const_mul' _ _ (by norm_num), lintegral_add_left hm1]
1333    _ ≤ 2 * (((∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (q y - K)) ^ 2 * ∫⁻ y, ‖w y‖ₑ ^ 2) +
1334          ((∫⁻ y, ‖w y‖ₑ) ^ 2 * ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (min (q y) K ^ 2))) := by
1335        gcongr
1336        · simpa only [hen1] using hE1
1337        · simpa only [hen2] using hE2
1338
1339end Conv
1340
1341section Absorb

1343variable {d : ℕ}
1344
1345theorem mul_log_ge {θ C : ℝ} (hθ : 0 < θ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) (hθC : θ ≤ C) :
1346    (θ - 1) ^ 2 / C ≤ (θ - 1) * Real.log θ := by
1347  have hC0 : 0 < C := by linarith
1348  rcases le_total 1 θ with h | h
1349  · -- θ ≥ 1: log θ ≥ (θ-1)/θ
1350    have h1 : (θ - 1) / θ ≤ Real.log θ := by
1351      have := Real.one_sub_inv_le_log_of_pos hθ
1352      calc (θ - 1) / θ = 1 - θ⁻¹ := by field_simp
1353        _ ≤ _ := this
1354    have h2 : (θ - 1) / C ≤ (θ - 1) / θ := div_le_div_of_nonneg_left (by linarith) hθ hθC
1355    calc (θ - 1) ^ 2 / C = (θ - 1) * ((θ - 1) / C) := by ring
1356      _ ≤ (θ - 1) * Real.log θ := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (h2.trans h1) (by linarith)
1357  · have h1 : Real.log θ ≤ θ - 1 := Real.log_le_sub_one_of_pos hθ
1358    have h2 : (θ - 1) ^ 2 / C ≤ (θ - 1) ^ 2 := div_le_self (sq_nonneg _) hC
1359    calc (θ - 1) ^ 2 / C ≤ (θ - 1) ^ 2 := h2
1360      _ = (θ - 1) * (θ - 1) := by ring
1361      _ ≤ (θ - 1) * Real.log θ := mul_le_mul_of_nonpos_left h1 (by linarith)
1362
1363theorem integral_mul_self_eq {α : Type*} [MeasurableSpace α] {ν : Measure α} {f : α → ℝ}
1364    (hf : MemLp f 2 ν) : ∫ x, f x * f x ∂ν = (eLpNorm f 2 ν).toReal ^ 2 := by
1365  have h := real_inner_self_eq_norm_sq (hf.toLp f)
1366  rw [L2.inner_def, Lp.norm_toLp] at h
1367  rw [← h]
1368  refine integral_congr_ae ?_
1369  filter_upwards [hf.coeFn_toLp] with x h1
1370  rw [h1]
1371  simp [sq]
1372
1373theorem integral_mul_le_eLpNorm {α : Type*} [MeasurableSpace α] {ν : Measure α} {f g : α → ℝ}
1374    (hf : MemLp f 2 ν) (hg : MemLp g 2 ν) :
1375    ∫ x, f x * g x ∂ν ≤ (eLpNorm f 2 ν).toReal * (eLpNorm g 2 ν).toReal := by
1376  have h := real_inner_le_norm (hf.toLp f) (hg.toLp g)
1377  rw [L2.inner_def, Lp.norm_toLp, Lp.norm_toLp] at h
1378  refine le_trans (le_of_eq ?_) h
1379  refine integral_congr_ae ?_
1380  filter_upwards [hf.coeFn_toLp, hg.coeFn_toLp] with x h1 h2
1381  rw [h1, h2]
1382  simp [mul_comm]
1383
1384/-- **Absorption** (`A:eq:marginal-absorption`): if `log θ + β K̃_k(θ - 1) = Φ + c` with `θ ∈ (0, C]`,
1385`∫ θ dμ = 1` and `k` positive definite, then `‖θ - 1‖_{L²(μ)} ≤ C ‖Φ‖_{L²(μ)}`. -/
1386theorem absorption {ρ : Torus d → ℝ} (hρ : IsProbDensity ρ) {Λ : ℝ} (hΛ : ∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ)
1387    {k : Torus d → ℝ} (hk : Measurable k) (hki : Integrable k)
1388    (ĝ : Lattice d → ℝ) (hĝ : ∀ n, 0 ≤ ĝ n)
1389    (hcoef : ∀ n, ∫ x, Torus.mode (-n) x * (k x : ℂ) = (ĝ n : ℂ))
1390    {β : ℝ} (hβ : 0 ≤ β) {θ : Torus d → ℝ} (hθm : Measurable θ) (hθ0 : ∀ x, 0 < θ x)
1391    {C : ℝ} (hC : 1 ≤ C) (hθC : ∀ᵐ x ∂(withDensityReal ρ), θ x ≤ C) (hθ1 : ∫ x, θ x ∂(withDensityReal ρ) = 1)
1392    {Φ : Torus d → ℝ} (hΦ : MemLp Φ 2 (withDensityReal ρ)) {c : ℝ}
1393    (heq : ∀ x, Real.log (θ x) + β * ResponseAux.Kt k (withDensityReal ρ) (fun y => θ y - 1) x = Φ x + c) :
1394    eLpNorm (fun x => θ x - 1) 2 (withDensityReal ρ) ≤
1395      ENNReal.ofReal C * eLpNorm Φ 2 (withDensityReal ρ) := by
1396  haveI := isProb_withDensityReal hρ
1397  set μ := withDensityReal ρ with hμ
1398  set z : Torus d → ℝ := fun x => θ x - 1 with hz
1399  have hzm : Measurable z := hθm.sub_const 1
1400  have hzb : ∀ᵐ x ∂μ, ‖z x‖ ≤ C := hθC.mono fun x hx => by
1401    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_le]
1402    have := hθ0 x
1403    constructor <;> simp only [hz] <;> linarith
1404  have hz2 : MemLp z 2 μ := MemLp.of_bound hzm.aestronglyMeasurable C hzb
1405  have hz1 : Integrable z μ := hz2.integrable one_le_two
1406  have hzint : ∫ x, z x ∂μ = 0 := by
1407    simp only [hz]
1408    rw [integral_sub (hz1.add (integrable_const 1) |>.congr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => by simp [hz]))
1409      (integrable_const _), hθ1]
1410    simp
1411  set Kz := ResponseAux.Kt k μ z with hKz
1412  obtain ⟨hKz2, -⟩ := ResponseAux.memLp_Kt hρ hΛ hk hki hz2
1413  have hlog : ∀ x, Real.log (θ x) = Φ x + c - β * Kz x := fun x => by
1414    have := heq x; simp only [hz, hKz] at this ⊢; linarith
1415  have hnn := ResponseAux.Kt_form_nonneg hρ hΛ hk hki ĝ hĝ hcoef hz2
1416  have i1 : Integrable (fun x => z x * Φ x) μ := MemLp.integrable_mul hz2 hΦ
1417  have i2 : Integrable (fun x => z x * Kz x) μ := MemLp.integrable_mul hz2 hKz2
1418  have i3 : Integrable (fun x => z x * c) μ := hz1.mul_const c
1419  have hid : ∀ x, z x * Real.log (θ x) = z x * Φ x + z x * c - β * (z x * Kz x) := fun x => by
1420    rw [hlog x]; ring
1421  have hi : Integrable (fun x => z x * Real.log (θ x)) μ := by
1422    have := (i1.add i3).sub (i2.const_mul β)
1423    exact this.congr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => by simp only [Pi.add_apply, Pi.sub_apply]; rw [hid x])
1424  have hsq : Integrable (fun x => (z x) ^ 2 / C) μ := (hz2.integrable_sq.div_const C)
1425  have hmono := integral_mono_ae hsq hi (hθC.mono fun x hx => mul_log_ge (hθ0 x) hC hx)
1426  have hrhs : ∫ x, z x * Real.log (θ x) ∂μ = ∫ x, z x * Φ x ∂μ - β * ∫ x, Kz x * z x ∂μ := by
1427    simp_rw [hid]
1428    have i13 : Integrable (fun x => z x * Φ x + z x * c) μ := i1.add i3
1429    have i2' : Integrable (fun x => β * (z x * Kz x)) μ := i2.const_mul β
1430    rw [integral_sub i13 i2', integral_add i1 i3, integral_mul_const, hzint,
1431      integral_const_mul]
1432    simp_rw [mul_comm (Kz _) (z _)]
1433    ring
1434  have hK0 : 0 ≤ β * ∫ x, Kz x * z x ∂μ := mul_nonneg hβ hnn
1435  set a := (eLpNorm z 2 μ).toReal with ha
1436  set b := (eLpNorm Φ 2 μ).toReal with hb
1437  have hzz : ∫ x, z x * z x ∂μ = a ^ 2 := integral_mul_self_eq hz2
1438  have hCS : ∫ x, z x * Φ x ∂μ ≤ a * b := integral_mul_le_eLpNorm hz2 hΦ
1439  have hlhs : ∫ x, (z x) ^ 2 / C ∂μ = a ^ 2 / C := by
1440    rw [integral_div]
1441    congr 1
1442    rw [← hzz]; congr 1; funext x; ring
1443  have key : a ^ 2 / C ≤ a * b := by
1444    calc a ^ 2 / C = ∫ x, (z x) ^ 2 / C ∂μ := hlhs.symm
1445      _ ≤ _ := hmono
1446      _ = _ := hrhs
1447      _ ≤ ∫ x, z x * Φ x ∂μ := by linarith
1448      _ ≤ a * b := hCS
1449  have ha0 : 0 ≤ a := ENNReal.toReal_nonneg
1450  have hb0 : 0 ≤ b := ENNReal.toReal_nonneg
1451  have hC0 : 0 < C := by linarith
1452  have hab : a ≤ C * b := by
1453    rcases ha0.eq_or_lt with h | h
1454    · rw [← h]; positivity
1455    · have : a / C ≤ b := by
1456        have h2 : a ^ 2 / C / a ≤ a * b / a := div_le_div_of_nonneg_right key h.le
1457        have e1 : a ^ 2 / C / a = a / C := by field_simp
1458        have e2 : a * b / a = b := by field_simp
1459        rwa [e1, e2] at h2
1460      rw [div_le_iff₀ hC0] at this
1461      linarith
1462  have hzfin : eLpNorm z 2 μ ≠ ⊤ := hz2.eLpNorm_ne_top
1463  have hΦfin : eLpNorm Φ 2 μ ≠ ⊤ := hΦ.eLpNorm_ne_top
1464  rw [← ENNReal.ofReal_toReal hzfin, ← ENNReal.ofReal_toReal hΦfin, ← ENNReal.ofReal_mul hC0.le]
1465  exact ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal hab
1466
1467end Absorb

1469section TorusInt
