1473/-- Integral of a translated nonnegative kernel against a measure dominated by `Cν · ρ dx`. -/
1474theorem integral_kernel_le {ρ : Torus d → ℝ} {Λ : ℝ} (hΛ : ∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ) (hΛ0 : 0 ≤ Λ)
1475    {ν : Measure (Torus d)} {Cν : ℝ}
1476    (hν : ν ≤ ENNReal.ofReal Cν • withDensityReal ρ) (hCν : 0 ≤ Cν)
1477    {h : Torus d → ℝ} (hm : Measurable h) (h0 : ∀ z, 0 ≤ h z) {c : ℝ} (hc0 : 0 ≤ c)
1478    (hc : ∫⁻ z, ENNReal.ofReal (h z) ≤ ENNReal.ofReal c) (x : Torus d) :
1479    ∫ y, h (x - y) ∂ν ≤ Cν * (Λ * c) := by
1480  have hcm : Measurable fun y : Torus d => h (x - y) := hm.comp (measurable_const.sub measurable_id)
1481  rw [integral_eq_lintegral_of_nonneg_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => h0 _) hcm.aestronglyMeasurable]
1482  refine ENNReal.toReal_le_of_le_ofReal (by positivity) ?_
1483  calc ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (h (x - y)) ∂ν
1484      ≤ ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (h (x - y)) ∂(ENNReal.ofReal Cν • withDensityReal ρ) :=
1485        lintegral_mono' hν le_rfl
1486    _ = ENNReal.ofReal Cν * ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (h (x - y)) ∂(withDensityReal ρ) := by
1487        rw [lintegral_smul_measure, smul_eq_mul]
1488    _ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal Cν * (ENNReal.ofReal Λ * ∫⁻ z, ENNReal.ofReal (h z)) := by
1489        gcongr
1490        exact KernelAux2.lintegral_conv_le ρ Λ hΛ (fun z => ENNReal.ofReal (h z)) x
1491    _ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal Cν * (ENNReal.ofReal Λ * ENNReal.ofReal c) := by gcongr
1492    _ = ENNReal.ofReal (Cν * (Λ * c)) := by
1493        rw [← ENNReal.ofReal_mul hΛ0, ← ENNReal.ofReal_mul hCν]
1494
1495
1496theorem cavD_torus (hd : 2 ≤ d) {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d)
1497    {Λ : ℝ} (hΛ1 : 1 ≤ Λ)
1498    {q : Torus d → ℝ} (hqm : Measurable q) (hq0 : ∀ z, 0 ≤ q z) (hqe : ∀ z, q (-z) = q z)
1499    {c : ℝ} (hc0 : 0 ≤ c) (hqc : ∫⁻ z, ENNReal.ofReal (q z) ≤ ENNReal.ofReal c)
1500    {K0 : ℝ} (hK0 : 0 < K0)
1501    (hw : ∀ r : ℝ, 0 < r → (volume : Measure (Torus d)) {z : Torus d | r < q z} ≤
1502      ENNReal.ofReal (K0 * r ^ (-(pOf d s))))
1503    {β : ℝ} (hβ : 0 < β) {Cν : ℝ} (hCν : 1 ≤ Cν)  :
1504    ∃ (CD : ℝ) (N₁ : ℕ), 0 ≤ CD ∧ ∀ (ρ : Torus d → ℝ), IsProbDensity ρ → (∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ) →
1505      ∀ (V : WeakPotential (withDensityReal ρ)),
1506      (∀ x y, V.q x y = q (x - y)) → Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + β * V.γ) ≤ Cν → weakKappa V β ≤ 1 / 2 →
1507      ∀ (N n : ℕ) [NeZero n], N₁ ≤ N → n ≤ N → ∀ x : Torus d,
1508        0 ≤ Real.log (cavU V (β / N) n (fun e : Empty => e.elim) x) +
1509          β / N * n * ∫ y, V.q y x ∂(cavMarg V (β / N) n) ∧
1510        Real.log (cavU V (β / N) n (fun e : Empty => e.elim) x) +
1511          β / N * n * ∫ y, V.q y x ∂(cavMarg V (β / N) n) ≤ CD * deltaN d s N := by
1512  have hp := one_lt_pOf hd hs0 hsd
1513  set p := pOf d s with hpdef
1514  obtain ⟨c₁, hc₁, hR2⟩ := second_moment_gFun hd hs0 hsd hK0
1515  obtain ⟨c', hc', hscale⟩ := gFun_mul_le hd hs0 hsd (c := 1 / β) (by positivity)
1516  have hΛ0 : 0 ≤ Λ := by linarith
1517  set mb : ℝ := β * (Cν * (Λ * c)) with hmb
1518  set CD : ℝ := Real.exp mb * β ^ 2 * Cν * Λ * c₁ * c' +
1519      β * Cν * Λ * (K0 * (p / (p - 1))) * β ^ (p - 1) with hCD
1520  refine ⟨CD, ⌈β⌉₊ + 1, by positivity, ?_⟩
1521  intro ρ hρ hΛ V hVq hVC hκ N n _ hN hn x
1522  haveI := isProb_withDensityReal hρ
1523  have hNβ : β ≤ N := by
1524    have : (⌈β⌉₊ : ℝ) + 1 ≤ N := by exact_mod_cast hN
1525    linarith [Nat.le_ceil β]
1526  have hN1 : 1 ≤ N := by omega
1527  have hN0 : 0 < N := hN1
1528  have hNpos : (0 : ℝ) < N := by exact_mod_cast hN0
1529  set lam : ℝ := β / N with hlam
1530  have hlampos : 0 < lam := by positivity
1531  have hVqx : ∀ y, V.q y x = q (x - y) := fun y => by rw [hVq, ← hqe, neg_sub]
1532  have hν : cavMarg V lam n ≤ ENNReal.ofReal Cν • withDensityReal ρ := by
1533    have h1 := marginal_le_smul (B := Empty) V hβ.le hN0 (n := n) (by simpa using hn)
1534      (fun e : Empty => e.elim) 0
1535    refine h1.trans ?_
1536    intro A
1537    simp only [Measure.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul]
1538    gcongr
1539  have hCν0 : 0 ≤ Cν := by linarith
1540  have hI1 : ∫ y, V.q y x ∂(cavMarg V lam n) ≤ Cν * (Λ * c) := by
1541    simp_rw [hVqx]
1542    exact integral_kernel_le hΛ hΛ0 hν hCν0 hqm hq0 hc0 hqc x
1543  have hI0 : 0 ≤ ∫ y, V.q y x ∂(cavMarg V lam n) := integral_nonneg fun y => V.q_nonneg _ _
1544  have hlamn : lam * n ≤ β := by
1545    have : (n : ℝ) ≤ N := by exact_mod_cast hn
1546    calc lam * n ≤ lam * N := by gcongr
1547      _ = β := by rw [hlam]; field_simp
1548  have hlamn' : (n : ℝ) * lam ≤ β := by linarith [hlamn, mul_comm (n : ℝ) lam]
1549  have hm : lam * n * ∫ y, V.q y x ∂(cavMarg V lam n) ≤ mb := by
1550    calc lam * n * ∫ y, V.q y x ∂(cavMarg V lam n) ≤ β * ∫ y, V.q y x ∂(cavMarg V lam n) := by gcongr
1551      _ ≤ β * (Cν * (Λ * c)) := by gcongr
1552  obtain ⟨h1, h2⟩ := cavD_bounds V hβ.le hN0 hn hκ x hm
1553  refine ⟨h1, h2.trans ?_⟩
1554  set ν := cavMarg V lam n with hνdef
1555  set K : ℝ := N / β with hK
1556  have hKpos : 0 < K := by positivity
1557  have hK1 : 1 ≤ K := by rw [hK, le_div_iff₀ hβ]; linarith
1558  have hKlam : lam * K = 1 := by rw [hlam, hK]; field_simp
1559  -- the second-moment term
1560  have hA : ∫ y, (min (lam * V.q y x) 1) ^ 2 ∂ν ≤ Cν * (Λ * (lam ^ 2 * (c₁ * gFun d s K))) := by
1561    simp_rw [hVqx]
1562    refine integral_kernel_le (h := fun z => (min (lam * q z) 1) ^ 2) hΛ hΛ0 hν hCν0
1563      ((hqm.const_mul lam).min measurable_const |>.pow_const 2) (fun z => sq_nonneg _)
1564      (by have := hR2 K hK1; have := one_le_gFun hd hs0 hsd hK1; positivity) ?_ x
1565    have e : ∀ z, ENNReal.ofReal ((min (lam * q z) 1) ^ 2) =
1566        ENNReal.ofReal (lam ^ 2) * ENNReal.ofReal (min (q z) K ^ 2) := by
1567      intro z
1568      rw [← ENNReal.ofReal_mul (by positivity)]
1569      congr 1
1570      have : min (lam * q z) 1 = lam * min (q z) K := by
1571        rw [mul_min_of_nonneg _ _ hlampos.le, hKlam]
1572      rw [this]; ring
1573    simp_rw [e]
1574    rw [lintegral_const_mul (ENNReal.ofReal (lam ^ 2)) (f := fun z => ENNReal.ofReal (min (q z) K ^ 2))
1575      (ENNReal.measurable_ofReal.comp ((hqm.min measurable_const).pow_const 2))]
1576    calc ENNReal.ofReal (lam ^ 2) * ∫⁻ z, ENNReal.ofReal (min (q z) K ^ 2)
1577        ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (lam ^ 2) * ENNReal.ofReal (1 + 2 * K0 * ∫ t in (1 : ℝ)..K, t ^ (1 - p)) := by
1578          gcongr
1579          exact lintegral_minsq_le hqm hq0 hK1 hp hK0 hw
1580      _ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (lam ^ 2) * ENNReal.ofReal (c₁ * gFun d s K) := by
1581          gcongr
1582          exact hR2 K hK1
1583      _ = _ := by rw [← ENNReal.ofReal_mul (by positivity)]
1584  -- the tail term
1585  set T : ℝ := K0 * K ^ (1 - p) * (p / (p - 1)) with hT
1586  have hT0 : 0 ≤ T := by
1587    have : 0 < p - 1 := by linarith
1588    positivity
1589  have hB : ∫ y, max (lam * V.q y x - 1) 0 ∂ν ≤ Cν * (Λ * (lam * T)) := by
1590    simp_rw [hVqx]
1591    refine integral_kernel_le (h := fun z => max (lam * q z - 1) 0) hΛ hΛ0 hν hCν0
1592      (((hqm.const_mul lam).sub_const 1).max measurable_const) (fun z => le_max_right _ _)
1593      (by positivity) ?_ x
1594    have e : ∀ z, ENNReal.ofReal (max (lam * q z - 1) 0) = ENNReal.ofReal lam * ENNReal.ofReal (q z - K) := by
1595      intro z
1596      rw [← ENNReal.ofReal_mul hlampos.le, ENNReal.ofReal_max]
1597      simp only [ENNReal.ofReal_zero]
1598      rw [max_eq_left (by simp)]
1599      congr 1
1600      rw [mul_sub, hKlam]
1601    simp_rw [e]
1602    rw [lintegral_const_mul (ENNReal.ofReal lam) (f := fun z => ENNReal.ofReal (q z - K))
1603      (ENNReal.measurable_ofReal.comp (hqm.sub_const K))]
1604    calc ENNReal.ofReal lam * ∫⁻ z, ENNReal.ofReal (q z - K)
1605        ≤ ENNReal.ofReal lam * ENNReal.ofReal T := by
1606          gcongr
1607          exact lintegral_tail_le hqm hq0 hKpos hp hK0 hw
1608      _ = _ := by rw [← ENNReal.ofReal_mul hlampos.le]
1609  -- assemble
1610  have hN1' : (1 : ℝ) ≤ N := by exact_mod_cast hN1
1611  have hG := deltaN_eq_gFun hd hs0 hsd hN1
1612  have hgN1 := one_le_gFun hd hs0 hsd hN1'
1613  have hgK : gFun d s K ≤ c' * gFun d s N := by
1614    have hK' : K = 1 / β * N := by rw [hK]; field_simp
1615    rw [hK']
1616    exact hscale N hN1' (by rw [← hK']; exact hK1)
1617  have hKr : K ^ (1 - p) ≤ β ^ (p - 1) * ((N : ℝ)⁻¹ * gFun d s N) := by
1618    have h1 := rpow_one_sub_le_gFun hd hs0 hsd hN1'
1619    have e : K ^ (1 - p) = (N : ℝ) ^ (1 - p) * β ^ (p - 1) := by
1620      rw [hK, Real.div_rpow hNpos.le hβ.le, div_eq_mul_inv, ← Real.rpow_neg hβ.le]
1621      congr 2; ring
1622    rw [e, mul_comm]
1623    exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h1 (by positivity)
1624  have hgK0 : 0 ≤ gFun d s K := by have := one_le_gFun hd hs0 hsd hK1; linarith
1625  have hn0 : (0 : ℝ) ≤ n := Nat.cast_nonneg n
1626  have hE1' : (n : ℝ) * ∫ y, (min (lam * V.q y x) 1) ^ 2 ∂ν ≤ β * lam * Cν * Λ * c₁ * gFun d s K := by
1627    have h0 : (0 : ℝ) ≤ lam * Cν * Λ * c₁ * gFun d s K := by positivity
1628    calc (n : ℝ) * ∫ y, (min (lam * V.q y x) 1) ^ 2 ∂ν
1629        ≤ n * (Cν * (Λ * (lam ^ 2 * (c₁ * gFun d s K)))) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hA hn0
1630      _ = (n * lam) * (lam * Cν * Λ * c₁ * gFun d s K) := by ring
1631      _ ≤ β * (lam * Cν * Λ * c₁ * gFun d s K) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hlamn' h0
1632      _ = _ := by ring
1633  have hE1 : Real.exp mb * (n * ∫ y, (min (lam * V.q y x) 1) ^ 2 ∂ν) ≤
1634      Real.exp mb * (β * lam * Cν * Λ * c₁ * gFun d s K) :=
1635    mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hE1' (Real.exp_pos _).le
1636  have hE2 : (n : ℝ) * ∫ y, max (lam * V.q y x - 1) 0 ∂ν ≤ β * Cν * Λ * T := by
1637    have h0 : (0 : ℝ) ≤ Cν * Λ * T := by positivity
1638    calc (n : ℝ) * ∫ y, max (lam * V.q y x - 1) 0 ∂ν ≤ n * (Cν * (Λ * (lam * T))) :=
1639          mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hB hn0
1640      _ = (n * lam) * (Cν * Λ * T) := by ring
1641      _ ≤ β * (Cν * Λ * T) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hlamn' h0
1642      _ = _ := by ring
1643  have hNi : (0 : ℝ) < (N : ℝ)⁻¹ := by positivity
1644  have hlam' : lam = β * (N : ℝ)⁻¹ := by rw [hlam]; ring
1645  calc Real.exp mb * (n * ∫ y, (min (lam * V.q y x) 1) ^ 2 ∂ν) + n * ∫ y, max (lam * V.q y x - 1) 0 ∂ν
1646      ≤ Real.exp mb * (β * lam * Cν * Λ * c₁ * gFun d s K) + β * Cν * Λ * T := add_le_add hE1 hE2
1647    _ ≤ Real.exp mb * (β * lam * Cν * Λ * c₁ * (c' * gFun d s N)) +
1648          β * Cν * Λ * (K0 * (β ^ (p - 1) * ((N : ℝ)⁻¹ * gFun d s N)) * (p / (p - 1))) := by
1649        have hp1 : 0 < p - 1 := by linarith
1650        have hexpp : 0 < Real.exp mb := Real.exp_pos _
1651        have h1 : Real.exp mb * (β * lam * Cν * Λ * c₁ * gFun d s K) ≤
1652            Real.exp mb * (β * lam * Cν * Λ * c₁ * (c' * gFun d s N)) := by
1653          refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ hexpp.le
1654          have : 0 ≤ β * lam * Cν * Λ * c₁ := by positivity
1655          exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hgK this
1656        have h2 : β * Cν * Λ * T ≤
1657            β * Cν * Λ * (K0 * (β ^ (p - 1) * ((N : ℝ)⁻¹ * gFun d s N)) * (p / (p - 1))) := by
1658          have : 0 ≤ β * Cν * Λ := by positivity
1659          refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ this
1660          simp only [hT]
1661          have : 0 ≤ p / (p - 1) := by positivity
1662          exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hKr hK0.le) this
1663        exact add_le_add h1 h2
1664    _ = CD * deltaN d s N := by
1665        rw [hG, hCD, hlam']
1666        ring
1667end TorusInt

1669section Setting
1670
1671variable {d M : ℕ} {μ : Measure (Torus d)} {q gL : Torus d → ℝ}
1672  {Ψ : Torus d → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin M)} {γ : ℝ} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ]
1673
1674/-- The weak potential of the calibrated reference with a one-body field `hh = t·Ξ`, `|hh| ≤ K`:
1675`φ = β q*μ + hh`, pair kernel `q(x - y)`, `M = βγ + K`, `M' = 0` (`∫ hh dμ = 0` for `hh = t·Ξ`). -/
1676def weakPotK (h : RefHyp μ q gL Ψ γ) {β : ℝ} (hβ : 0 ≤ β) (t : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin M)) {K : ℝ}
1677    (ht : ∀ x, |inner ℝ t (Xi μ Ψ x)| ≤ K) : WeakPotential μ where
1678  φ := fun x => β * convMeasure q μ x + inner ℝ t (Xi μ Ψ x)
1679  q := fun x y => q (x - y)
1680  M := β * γ + K
1681  M' := 0
1682  γ := γ
1683  measurable_φ := (h.measurable_conv.const_mul β).add (measurable_const.inner h.measurable_Xi)
1684  measurable_q := h.q_meas.comp (measurable_fst.sub measurable_snd)
1685  q_nonneg := fun x y => h.q_nonneg _
1686  q_symm := fun x y => by rw [← h.q_even (x - y), neg_sub]
1687  γ_nonneg := h.gamma_nonneg
1688  φ_le := fun x => by
1689    have h1 : β * convMeasure q μ x ≤ β * γ := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (h.conv_le x) hβ
1690    have h2 := (le_abs_self _).trans (ht x)
1691    linarith
1692  integrable_φ := by
1693    have hK : 0 ≤ K := (abs_nonneg _).trans (ht 0)
1694    refine integrable_of_abs_le ((h.measurable_conv.const_mul β).add (measurable_const.inner h.measurable_Xi))
1695      (β * γ + K) fun x => ?_
1696    have h1 : 0 ≤ β * convMeasure q μ x := mul_nonneg hβ (h.conv_nonneg x)
1697    have h1' : β * convMeasure q μ x ≤ β * γ := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (h.conv_le x) hβ
1698    have h2 := ht x
1699    rw [abs_le] at h2 ⊢
1700    constructor <;> linarith [h2.1, h2.2]
1701  neg_le_integral_φ := by
1702    have hi1 : Integrable (fun x => β * convMeasure q μ x) μ :=
1703      integrable_of_abs_le (h.measurable_conv.const_mul β) (β * γ) fun x => by
1704        rw [abs_of_nonneg (mul_nonneg hβ (h.conv_nonneg x))]
1705        exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (h.conv_le x) hβ
1706    have hK : 0 ≤ K := (abs_nonneg _).trans (ht 0)
1707    have hi2 : Integrable (fun x => inner ℝ t (Xi μ Ψ x)) μ :=
1708      integrable_of_abs_le (measurable_const.inner h.measurable_Xi) K ht
1709    rw [integral_add hi1 hi2, integral_inner h.integrable_Xi t, h.integral_Xi]
1710    have : 0 ≤ ∫ x, β * convMeasure q μ x ∂μ :=
1711      integral_nonneg fun x => mul_nonneg hβ (h.conv_nonneg x)
1712    simpa using this
1713  integrable_q := h.q_int
1714  row_le := h.q_row
1715
1716variable {β : ℝ} {N : ℕ}
1717
1718theorem RN_eq_fieldLawK (h : RefHyp μ q gL Ψ γ) (hβ : 0 ≤ β) {K : ℝ}
1719    (ht : ∀ x, |inner ℝ (tN q μ Ψ β N) (Xi μ Ψ x)| ≤ K) :
1720    RN q μ Ψ β N = fieldLaw (weakPotK h hβ (tN q μ Ψ β N) ht) (weakPotK h hβ (tN q μ Ψ β N) ht).φ
1721      (β / N) N := by
1722  rw [h.RN_eq_tilted hβ, fieldLaw_eq_tilted]
1723  · congr 1
1724    funext X
1725    simp only [E0, Yf, weakPotK, pairEnergy, Finset.sum_add_distrib]
1726    ring
1727  · have := h.integrable_exp_E0_Yf hβ N (tN q μ Ψ β N)
1728    convert this using 2 with X
1729    simp only [E0, Yf, weakPotK, pairEnergy, Finset.sum_add_distrib]
1730    ring_nf
1731
1732/-- `|t_N · Ξ(x)| ≤ 4 β g_L(0)` for every `N ≥ 1`. -/
1733theorem abs_tN_inner_le (h : RefHyp μ q gL Ψ γ) (hβ : 0 < β) (hN : 0 < N) (x : Torus d) :
1734    |inner ℝ (tN q μ Ψ β N) (Xi μ Ψ x)| ≤ 4 * β * gL 0 := by
1735  haveI := h.isProb_R0 hβ.le N
1736  have h1 := h.tN_norm_le hβ hN
1737  have h2 : ‖∫ X, empXi μ Ψ X ∂(R0 q μ β N)‖ ≤ 2 * Real.sqrt (gL 0) := by
1738    have := norm_integral_le_of_norm_le_const (μ := R0 q μ β N) (f := empXi μ Ψ)
1739      (C := 2 * Real.sqrt (gL 0)) (Filter.Eventually.of_forall (h.norm_empXi_le hN))
1740    simpa using this
1741  calc |inner ℝ (tN q μ Ψ β N) (Xi μ Ψ x)| ≤ ‖tN q μ Ψ β N‖ * ‖Xi μ Ψ x‖ := abs_real_inner_le_norm _ _
1742    _ ≤ (β * (2 * Real.sqrt (gL 0))) * (2 * Real.sqrt (gL 0)) := by
1743        refine mul_le_mul (h1.trans (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h2 hβ.le)) (h.norm_Xi_le x)
1744          (norm_nonneg _) (by positivity)
1745    _ = 4 * β * (Real.sqrt (gL 0) ^ 2) := by ring
1746    _ = 4 * β * gL 0 := by rw [Real.sq_sqrt h.gL_zero_nonneg]
1747
1748/-- **(i), measure form**: `π_N ≤ e^{M + βγ} μ` for all `N ≥ 1` (any bound `K` of the field `t_N·Ξ`). -/
1749theorem firstMarginal_RN_leK (h : RefHyp μ q gL Ψ γ) (hβ : 0 < β) (hN : 1 ≤ N) {K : ℝ}
1750    (ht : ∀ x, |inner ℝ (tN q μ Ψ β N) (Xi μ Ψ x)| ≤ K) :
1751    firstMarginal (RN q μ Ψ β N) ≤
1752      ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp ((β * γ + K) + 0 + β * γ)) • μ := by
1753  have hN0 : 0 < N := hN
1754  set V := weakPotK h hβ.le (tN q μ Ψ β N) ht with hV
1755  have hRN := RN_eq_fieldLawK h hβ.le ht (N := N)
1756  rw [← hV] at hRN
1757  have hm := marginal_le_smul (B := Empty) V hβ.le hN0 (n := N) (by simp) (fun e => e.elim) ⟨0, hN0⟩
1758  rw [pinnedLaw_empty] at hm
1759  unfold firstMarginal
1760  rw [dif_pos hN0, hRN]
1761  refine hm.trans (le_of_eq ?_)
1762  rfl
1763
1764/-- **(i), measure form**: `π_N ≤ e^{M + βγ} μ` for all `N ≥ 1`. -/
1765theorem firstMarginal_RN_le (h : RefHyp μ q gL Ψ γ) (hβ : 0 < β) (hN : 1 ≤ N) :
1766    firstMarginal (RN q μ Ψ β N) ≤
1767      ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp ((β * γ + 4 * β * gL 0) + 0 + β * γ)) • μ :=
1768  firstMarginal_RN_leK h hβ hN (abs_tN_inner_le h hβ (Nat.succ_le_iff.1 hN))
1769
1770/-- `κ ≤ 1/2` for `V = weakPotK` with `K = 1`, from `KappaSmall`. -/
1771theorem weakKappa_weakPotK_le (h : RefHyp μ q gL Ψ γ) (hβ : 0 ≤ β) {t : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin M)}
1772    (ht : ∀ x, |inner ℝ t (Xi μ Ψ x)| ≤ 1) (hκ : KappaSmall β γ) :
1773    weakKappa (weakPotK h hβ t ht) β ≤ 1 / 2 := by
1774  have e : weakKappa (weakPotK h hβ t ht) β = 4 * Real.exp ((β * γ + 1) + 0 + β * γ) * (β * γ) := rfl
1775  rw [e]
1776  have : (β * γ + 1) + 0 + β * γ = 2 * (β * γ) + 1 := by ring
1777  rw [this]
1778  exact hκ
1779
1780end Setting

1787theorem reference_marginal_density_le (hd : 2 ≤ d) {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {τ : ℝ} (hτ : 0 < τ)
1788    (hτ1 : τ ≤ 1) (L : ℕ) {Λ : ℝ} (hΛ1 : 1 ≤ Λ) {β : ℝ} (hβ : 0 < β)
1789    {M : ℕ} (Ψ : Torus d → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin M))
1790    (hΨ : ∀ x y, (weakSplit d s τ L).gL (x - y) = inner ℝ (Ψ x) (Ψ y)) :
1791    ∃ C : ℝ, ∀ ρ : Torus d → ℝ, IsProbDensity ρ → (∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ) → ∀ N : ℕ, 1 ≤ N →
1792      firstMarginal (RN (weakSplit d s τ L).q (withDensityReal ρ) Ψ β N) ≪ withDensityReal ρ ∧
1793        ∀ᵐ x ∂(withDensityReal ρ),
1794          (firstMarginal (RN (weakSplit d s τ L).q (withDensityReal ρ) Ψ β N)).rnDeriv
1795            (withDensityReal ρ) x ≤ ENNReal.ofReal C := by
1796  refine ⟨Real.exp ((β * (Λ * (weakSplit d s τ L).c) + 4 * β * (weakSplit d s τ L).gL 0) + 0 +
1797    β * (Λ * (weakSplit d s τ L).c)), fun ρ hρ hρΛ N hN => ?_⟩
1798  haveI := isProb_withDensityReal hρ
1799  have h := refHyp_weakSplit hd hs0 hsd hτ hτ1 L hρ hρΛ Ψ hΨ
1800  have hle := firstMarginal_RN_le h hβ hN
1801  have hle' : firstMarginal (RN (weakSplit d s τ L).q (withDensityReal ρ) Ψ β N) ≤
1802      (withDensityReal ρ).withDensity (fun _ => ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp ((β * (Λ * (weakSplit d s τ L).c) +
1803        4 * β * (weakSplit d s τ L).gL 0) + 0 + β * (Λ * (weakSplit d s τ L).c)))) := by
1804    rw [withDensity_const]; exact hle
1805  haveI : IsFiniteMeasure (firstMarginal (RN (weakSplit d s τ L).q (withDensityReal ρ) Ψ β N)) := by
1806    refine ⟨lt_of_le_of_lt (hle' Set.univ) ?_⟩
1807    rw [withDensity_apply _ MeasurableSet.univ, Measure.restrict_univ, lintegral_const, measure_univ, mul_one]
1808    exact ENNReal.ofReal_lt_top
1809  exact PoleCavityAux.rnDeriv_le_of_le_withDensity _ _ _ hle'
1810
1811end Main

1813section Cavity

1815variable {d M : ℕ} {μ : Measure (Torus d)} {q gL : Torus d → ℝ}

1819omit [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] in
1820theorem pinField_empty {E : Type*} [MeasurableSpace E] {μ : Measure E} (V : WeakPotential μ) (lam : ℝ)
1821    (a : Empty → E) : pinField V lam a = V.φ := by
1822  funext z; simp [pinField]
1823
1824/-- The explicit one-point density `θ = Θ / ∫ Θ` of the first marginal (`n + 1` particles). -/
1825def theta (V : WeakPotential μ) (lam : ℝ) (n : ℕ) (x : Torus d) : ℝ :=
1826  cavTheta V lam n (fun e : Empty => e.elim) x / ∫ x', cavTheta V lam n (fun e : Empty => e.elim) x' ∂μ
1827
1828theorem firstMarginal_RN_eq (h : RefHyp μ q gL Ψ γ) (hβ : 0 < β) {K : ℝ}
1829    (ht : ∀ x, |inner ℝ (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) (Xi μ Ψ x)| ≤ K) :
1830    firstMarginal (RN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) =
1831      μ.withDensity (fun x => ENNReal.ofReal (theta (weakPotK h hβ.le (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) ht)
1832        (β / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) n x)) := by
1833  set V := weakPotK h hβ.le (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) ht with hV
1834  have hRN := RN_eq_fieldLawK h hβ.le ht (N := n + 1)
1835  rw [← hV] at hRN
1836  have hlam : 0 ≤ β / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) := div_nonneg hβ.le (Nat.cast_nonneg _)
1837  unfold firstMarginal
1838  rw [dif_pos (Nat.succ_pos n), hRN, ← pinnedLaw_empty V _ _ (fun e : Empty => e.elim)]
1839  exact marginal_eq_normalized V hlam (fun e : Empty => e.elim) (n := n) 0
1840
1841end Cavity

1845variable {d M : ℕ} {μ : Measure (Torus d)} {q gL : Torus d → ℝ}

1849theorem measurable_theta (V : WeakPotential μ) {lam : ℝ} (hlam : 0 ≤ lam) (n : ℕ) :
1850    Measurable (theta V lam n) :=
1851  (measurable_cavTheta V hlam (fun e : Empty => e.elim) n).div_const _
1852
1853theorem theta_pos (V : WeakPotential μ) {lam : ℝ} (hlam : 0 ≤ lam) (n : ℕ) (x : Torus d) :
1854    0 < theta V lam n x :=
1855  div_pos (cavTheta_pos V hlam _ n x) (integral_cavTheta_pos V hlam _ n)
1856
1857theorem integral_theta (V : WeakPotential μ) {lam : ℝ} (hlam : 0 ≤ lam) (n : ℕ) :
1858    ∫ x, theta V lam n x ∂μ = 1 := by
1859  unfold theta
1860  rw [integral_div]
1861  exact div_self (integral_cavTheta_pos V hlam _ n).ne'
1862
1863theorem integrable_theta (V : WeakPotential μ) {lam : ℝ} (hlam : 0 ≤ lam) (n : ℕ) :
1864    Integrable (theta V lam n) μ :=
1865  (integrable_cavTheta V hlam (fun e : Empty => e.elim) n).div_const _
1866
1867/-- **The cavity equation** (pointwise): `log θ = φ + log u - log Z`. -/
1868theorem log_theta_eq (V : WeakPotential μ) {lam : ℝ} (hlam : 0 ≤ lam) (n : ℕ) (x : Torus d) :
1869    Real.log (theta V lam n x) = V.φ x + Real.log (cavU V lam n (fun e : Empty => e.elim) x) -
1870      Real.log (∫ x', cavTheta V lam n (fun e : Empty => e.elim) x' ∂μ) := by
1871  have hZ := integral_cavTheta_pos V hlam (fun e : Empty => e.elim) n
1872  have h1 := cavTheta_pos V hlam (fun e : Empty => e.elim) n x
1873  have e : cavTheta V lam n (fun e : Empty => e.elim) x = Real.exp (V.φ x) *
1874      cavU V lam n (fun e : Empty => e.elim) x := by
1875    unfold cavTheta; rw [pinField_empty]
1876  unfold theta
1877  rw [Real.log_div h1.ne' hZ.ne', e, Real.log_mul (Real.exp_pos _).ne' (cavU_pos V hlam _ n x).ne',
1878    Real.log_exp]
1879

1881/-- `Ξ̄_N = ∫ Ξ dπ_N` (all one-coordinate marginals of `R_N` coincide). -/
1882theorem Xibar_eq_integral (h : RefHyp μ q gL Ψ γ) (hβ : 0 < β) {K : ℝ}
1883    (ht : ∀ x, |inner ℝ (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) (Xi μ Ψ x)| ≤ K) :
1884    Xibar q μ Ψ β (n + 1) = ∫ y, Xi μ Ψ y ∂(firstMarginal (RN q μ Ψ β (n + 1))) := by
1885  set V := weakPotK h hβ.le (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) ht with hV
1886  have hRN := RN_eq_fieldLawK h hβ.le ht (N := n + 1)
1887  rw [← hV] at hRN
1888  haveI := h.isProb_RN hβ.le (N := n + 1)
1889  have hmarg : ∀ i : Fin (n + 1), (RN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)).map (fun X => X i) =
1890      firstMarginal (RN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) := by
1891    intro i
1892    unfold firstMarginal
1893    rw [dif_pos (Nat.succ_pos n)]
1894    rw [hRN]
1895    exact map_eval_eq_of_symm μ _ (measurable_fieldDensity V.measurable_φ V _ _)
1896      (fun σ y => fieldDensity_comp_perm V _ _ σ y) i ⟨0, Nat.succ_pos n⟩
1897  unfold Xibar empXi
1898  have hint : ∀ i : Fin (n + 1), Integrable (fun X : Config d (n + 1) => Xi μ Ψ (X i)) (RN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) :=
1899    fun i => Integrable.of_bound (h.measurable_Xi.comp (measurable_pi_apply i)).aestronglyMeasurable
1900      (2 * Real.sqrt (gL 0)) (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun X => h.norm_Xi_le _)
1901  rw [integral_smul, integral_finsetSum _ fun i _ => hint i]
1902  have h2 : ∀ i : Fin (n + 1), ∫ X : Config d (n + 1), Xi μ Ψ (X i) ∂(RN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) =
1903      ∫ y, Xi μ Ψ y ∂(firstMarginal (RN q μ Ψ β (n + 1))) := by
1904    intro i
1905    rw [← hmarg i, integral_map (measurable_pi_apply i).aemeasurable h.measurable_Xi.aestronglyMeasurable]
1906  simp_rw [h2]
1907  simp only [Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin]
1908  rw [← Nat.cast_smul_eq_nsmul ℝ, smul_smul]
1909  have : (1 / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) * ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) = 1 := by field_simp
1910  rw [this, one_smul]
1911

1913theorem gL_symm (h : RefHyp μ q gL Ψ γ) (x y : Torus d) : gL (x - y) = gL (y - x) := by
1914  rw [h.gL_eq, h.gL_eq, real_inner_comm]

1916theorem measurable_gL_sub (h : RefHyp μ q gL Ψ γ) (x : Torus d) : Measurable fun y => gL (x - y) := by
1917  have : (fun y => gL (x - y)) = fun y => inner ℝ (Ψ x) (Ψ y) := funext (h.gL_eq x)
1918  rw [this]; exact measurable_const.inner h.Ψ_meas
1919
1920theorem abs_gL_le (h : RefHyp μ q gL Ψ γ) (z : Torus d) : |gL z| ≤ gL 0 := by
1921  have h1 := h.gL_eq z 0
1922  rw [sub_zero] at h1
1923  rw [h1]
1924  calc |inner ℝ (Ψ z) (Ψ 0)| ≤ ‖Ψ z‖ * ‖Ψ 0‖ := abs_real_inner_le_norm _ _
1925    _ ≤ Real.sqrt (gL 0) * Real.sqrt (gL 0) :=
1926        mul_le_mul (h.norm_Ψ_le z) (h.norm_Ψ_le 0) (norm_nonneg _) (Real.sqrt_nonneg _)
1927    _ = gL 0 := Real.mul_self_sqrt h.gL_zero_nonneg
1928
1929/-- **Calibration identity** (`A:eq:calibration-potential`): `t_N·Ξ = -β g_L*(π - μ)` up to a constant. -/
1930theorem hh_eq (h : RefHyp μ q gL Ψ γ) (hβ : 0 < β) {K : ℝ}
1931    (ht : ∀ x, |inner ℝ (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) (Xi μ Ψ x)| ≤ K) :
1932    ∃ c₀ : ℝ, ∀ x, inner ℝ (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) (Xi μ Ψ x) =
1933      -β * ResponseAux.Kt gL μ (fun y => theta (weakPotK h hβ.le (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) ht)
1934        (β / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) n y - 1) x + c₀ := by
1935  set V := weakPotK h hβ.le (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) ht with hV
1936  set lam := β / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) with hlam
1937  have hlam0 : 0 ≤ lam := div_nonneg hβ.le (Nat.cast_nonneg _)
1938  set θ := theta V lam n with hθ
1939  have hθ0 : ∀ x, 0 < θ x := theta_pos V hlam0 n
1940  have hθ1 : ∫ x, θ x ∂μ = 1 := integral_theta V hlam0 n
1941  have hθi : Integrable θ μ := integrable_theta V hlam0 n
1942  have hθm : Measurable θ := measurable_theta V hlam0 n
1943  have hmarg := firstMarginal_RN_eq h hβ ht (n := n)
1944  have hX := Xibar_eq_integral h hβ ht (n := n)
1945  have htN := h.tN_eq hβ (Nat.succ_pos n)
1946  set Ξ := Xi μ Ψ with hΞ
1947  set Ψbar := ∫ y, Ψ y ∂μ with hΨbar
1948  set u := Xibar q μ Ψ β (n + 1) with hu
1949  have hu' : u = ∫ y, θ y • Ξ y ∂μ := by
1950    rw [hX, hmarg, integral_withDensity_eq_integral_toReal_smul (f := fun x => ENNReal.ofReal (θ x))
1951      (ENNReal.measurable_ofReal.comp hθm) (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun _ => ENNReal.ofReal_lt_top)]
1952    refine integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => ?_)
1953    show (ENNReal.ofReal (θ y)).toReal • Ξ y = θ y • Ξ y
1954    rw [ENNReal.toReal_ofReal (hθ0 y).le]
1955  have hΞi : ∀ x, Integrable (fun y => inner ℝ (Ξ x) (Ξ y) * θ y) μ := by
1956    intro x
1957    refine Integrable.bdd_mul (c := ‖Ξ x‖ * (2 * Real.sqrt (gL 0))) hθi ?_ ?_
1958    · exact (measurable_const.inner h.measurable_Xi).aestronglyMeasurable
1959    · refine Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => ?_
1960      calc ‖inner ℝ (Ξ x) (Ξ y)‖ ≤ ‖Ξ x‖ * ‖Ξ y‖ := norm_inner_le_norm _ _
1961        _ ≤ _ := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (h.norm_Xi_le y) (norm_nonneg _)
1962  have hΨi : Integrable Ψ μ := h.integrable_Ψ
1963  -- the constant `C1 = ∫ θ ⟪Ψ̄, Ψ⟫`
1964  set C1 : ℝ := ∫ y, inner ℝ Ψbar (Ψ y) * θ y ∂μ with hC1
1965  have hgi : ∀ x, Integrable (fun y => gL (x - y) * θ y) μ := by
1966    intro x
1967    refine Integrable.bdd_mul (c := gL 0) hθi ?_ (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => ?_)
1968    · exact (measurable_gL_sub h x).aestronglyMeasurable
1969    · rw [Real.norm_eq_abs]; exact abs_gL_le h _
1970  refine ⟨-β * (inner ℝ Ψbar Ψbar - C1), fun x => ?_⟩
1971  have hint : Integrable (fun y => θ y • Ξ y) μ := by
1972    refine Integrable.mono' (hθi.mul_const (2 * Real.sqrt (gL 0)))
1973      (hθm.smul h.measurable_Xi).aestronglyMeasurable (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => ?_)
1974    rw [norm_smul, Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_pos (hθ0 y)]
1975    exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (h.norm_Xi_le y) (hθ0 y).le
1976  have e1 : inner ℝ (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) (Ξ x) = -β * inner ℝ u (Ξ x) := by
1977    rw [htN, real_inner_smul_left]
1978  have e2 : inner ℝ u (Ξ x) = ∫ y, inner ℝ (Ξ x) (Ξ y) * θ y ∂μ := by
1979    rw [real_inner_comm, hu', ← integral_inner hint (Ξ x)]
1980    refine integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => ?_)
1981    simp only [real_inner_smul_right]
1982    ring
1983  have hexp : ∀ y, inner ℝ (Ξ x) (Ξ y) * θ y = gL (x - y) * θ y - inner ℝ (Ψ x) Ψbar * θ y -
1984      inner ℝ Ψbar (Ψ y) * θ y + inner ℝ Ψbar Ψbar * θ y := by
1985    intro y
1986    simp only [hΞ, Xi, ← hΨbar, inner_sub_left, inner_sub_right, h.gL_eq x y]
1987    rw [real_inner_comm (Ψ y) Ψbar]
1988    ring
1989  have hi2 : Integrable (fun y => inner ℝ Ψbar (Ψ y) * θ y) μ := by
1990    refine Integrable.bdd_mul (c := ‖Ψbar‖ * Real.sqrt (gL 0)) hθi
1991      (measurable_const.inner h.Ψ_meas).aestronglyMeasurable (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => ?_)
1992    calc ‖inner ℝ Ψbar (Ψ y)‖ ≤ ‖Ψbar‖ * ‖Ψ y‖ := norm_inner_le_norm _ _
1993      _ ≤ _ := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (h.norm_Ψ_le y) (norm_nonneg _)
1994  have e3 : ∫ y, inner ℝ (Ξ x) (Ξ y) * θ y ∂μ =
1995      ∫ y, gL (x - y) * θ y ∂μ - inner ℝ (Ψ x) Ψbar - C1 + inner ℝ Ψbar Ψbar := by
1996    simp_rw [hexp]
1997    have j2 : Integrable (fun y => inner ℝ (Ψ x) Ψbar * θ y) μ := hθi.const_mul _
1998    have j4 : Integrable (fun y => inner ℝ Ψbar Ψbar * θ y) μ := hθi.const_mul _
1999    have j12 : Integrable (fun y => gL (x - y) * θ y - inner ℝ (Ψ x) Ψbar * θ y) μ := (hgi x).sub j2
2000    have j123 : Integrable (fun y => gL (x - y) * θ y - inner ℝ (Ψ x) Ψbar * θ y -
2001        inner ℝ Ψbar (Ψ y) * θ y) μ := j12.sub hi2
2002    rw [integral_add j123 j4, integral_sub j12 hi2, integral_sub (hgi x) j2,
2003      integral_const_mul, integral_const_mul, hθ1]
2004    ring
2005  have e4 : ResponseAux.Kt gL μ (fun y => θ y - 1) x = ∫ y, gL (x - y) * θ y ∂μ - inner ℝ (Ψ x) Ψbar := by
2006    unfold ResponseAux.Kt
2007    have hg1 : Integrable (fun y => gL (x - y)) μ :=
2008      Integrable.of_bound (measurable_gL_sub h x).aestronglyMeasurable (gL 0)
2009        (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => by rw [Real.norm_eq_abs]; exact abs_gL_le h _)
2010    have : ∫ y, gL (x - y) ∂μ = inner ℝ (Ψ x) Ψbar := by
2011      rw [hΨbar, ← integral_inner hΨi (Ψ x)]
2012      refine integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => ?_)
2013      exact h.gL_eq x y
2014    simp_rw [mul_sub, mul_one]
2015    rw [integral_sub (hgi x) hg1, this]
2016  rw [e1, e2, e3, e4]
2017  ring
2018
2019end Cavity2

2021section TV
2022
2023variable {α : Type*} [MeasurableSpace α] {μ : Measure α}
2024
2025theorem real_withDensity {θ : α → ℝ} (hθ0 : ∀ x, 0 ≤ θ x) (hθi : Integrable θ μ) {A : Set α}
2026    (hA : MeasurableSet A) :
2027    (μ.withDensity fun x => ENNReal.ofReal (θ x)).real A = ∫ x in A, θ x ∂μ := by
2028  unfold Measure.real
2029  rw [withDensity_apply _ hA, ← ofReal_integral_eq_lintegral_ofReal hθi.integrableOn
2030    ((ae_restrict_iff' hA).2 (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x _ => hθ0 x))]
2031  exact ENNReal.toReal_ofReal (setIntegral_nonneg hA fun x _ => hθ0 x)
2032
2033/-- `L¹` distance of densities from the total-variation (event) distance of the measures. -/
2034theorem integral_abs_sub_le {θ θ' : α → ℝ} (hθm : Measurable θ) (hθ'm : Measurable θ')
2035    (hθ0 : ∀ x, 0 ≤ θ x) (hθ'0 : ∀ x, 0 ≤ θ' x) (hθi : Integrable θ μ) (hθ'i : Integrable θ' μ)
2036    {ε : ℝ} (h : ∀ A : Set α, MeasurableSet A →
2037      |(μ.withDensity fun x => ENNReal.ofReal (θ x)).real A -
2038        (μ.withDensity fun x => ENNReal.ofReal (θ' x)).real A| ≤ ε) :
2039    ∫ x, |θ x - θ' x| ∂μ ≤ 2 * ε := by
2040  set S : Set α := {x | θ' x < θ x} with hS
2041  have hSm : MeasurableSet S := measurableSet_lt hθ'm hθm
2042  have hSc : MeasurableSet Sᶜ := hSm.compl
2043  have h1 : ∫ x, |θ x - θ' x| ∂μ = (∫ x in S, (θ x - θ' x) ∂μ) + ∫ x in Sᶜ, (θ' x - θ x) ∂μ := by
2044    have habs : Integrable (fun x => |θ x - θ' x|) μ := (hθi.sub hθ'i).abs
2045    rw [← integral_add_compl hSm habs]
2046    congr 1
2047    · refine setIntegral_congr_fun hSm fun x hx => ?_
2048      exact abs_of_pos (sub_pos.2 hx)
2049    · refine setIntegral_congr_fun hSc fun x hx => ?_
2050      have : θ x ≤ θ' x := not_lt.1 hx
2051      rw [abs_of_nonpos (by linarith)]; ring
2052  have h2 : ∫ x in S, (θ x - θ' x) ∂μ =
2053      (μ.withDensity fun x => ENNReal.ofReal (θ x)).real S -
2054        (μ.withDensity fun x => ENNReal.ofReal (θ' x)).real S := by
2055    rw [real_withDensity hθ0 hθi hSm, real_withDensity hθ'0 hθ'i hSm,
2056      integral_sub hθi.integrableOn hθ'i.integrableOn]
2057  have h3 : ∫ x in Sᶜ, (θ' x - θ x) ∂μ =
2058      (μ.withDensity fun x => ENNReal.ofReal (θ' x)).real Sᶜ -
2059        (μ.withDensity fun x => ENNReal.ofReal (θ x)).real Sᶜ := by
2060    rw [real_withDensity hθ0 hθi hSc, real_withDensity hθ'0 hθ'i hSc,
2061      integral_sub hθ'i.integrableOn hθi.integrableOn]
2062  rw [h1, h2, h3]
2063  have a1 := h S hSm
2064  have a2 := h Sᶜ hSc
2065  rw [abs_le] at a1 a2
2066  linarith [a1.2, a2.1]
2067
2068end TV

2070section ConvL2
2071
2072variable {d : ℕ}
2073
2074/-- `L²(μ)` bound of the convolution of the kernel with the density difference `ρ(θ - θ')`. -/
2075theorem cv_L2_bound {ρ : Torus d → ℝ} (hρ : IsProbDensity ρ) {Λ : ℝ} (hΛ : ∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ)
2076    (hΛ0 : 0 ≤ Λ) {q : Torus d → ℝ} (hqm : Measurable q) (hq0 : ∀ z, 0 ≤ q z)
2077    {θ θ' : Torus d → ℝ} (hθm : Measurable θ) (hθ'm : Measurable θ') {Cb : ℝ} (hCb : 0 ≤ Cb)
2078    (hθb : ∀ x, |θ x - θ' x| ≤ Cb) {η : ℝ} (hη0 : 0 ≤ η)
2079    (hη : ∫ x, |θ x - θ' x| ∂(withDensityReal ρ) ≤ η) {K : ℝ} (hK : 0 ≤ K) :
2080    ∫⁻ x, ‖ResponseAux.cv q (fun y => ρ y * (θ y - θ' y)) x‖ₑ ^ 2 ∂(withDensityReal ρ) ≤
2081      ENNReal.ofReal Λ * (2 * ((∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (q y - K)) ^ 2 * ENNReal.ofReal (Λ * Cb * η) +
2082        ENNReal.ofReal η ^ 2 * ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (min (q y) K ^ 2))) := by
2083  set w : Torus d → ℝ := fun y => ρ y * (θ y - θ' y) with hw
2084  have hwm : Measurable w := hρ.measurable.mul (hθm.sub hθ'm)
2085  -- step a: from `μ` to Lebesgue measure
2086  have ha : ∫⁻ x, ‖ResponseAux.cv q w x‖ₑ ^ 2 ∂(withDensityReal ρ) ≤
2087      ENNReal.ofReal Λ * ∫⁻ x, ‖ResponseAux.cv q w x‖ₑ ^ 2 := by
2088    calc ∫⁻ x, ‖ResponseAux.cv q w x‖ₑ ^ 2 ∂(withDensityReal ρ)
2089        ≤ ∫⁻ x, ‖ResponseAux.cv q w x‖ₑ ^ 2 ∂(ENNReal.ofReal Λ • (volume : Measure (Torus d))) :=
2090          lintegral_mono' (ResponseAux.dens_le Λ hΛ) le_rfl
2091      _ = _ := by rw [lintegral_smul_measure, smul_eq_mul]
2092  -- step c: `L¹` norm of `w`
2093  have hb : ∫⁻ y, ‖w y‖ₑ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal η := by
2094    have hint : Integrable (fun x => |θ x - θ' x|) (withDensityReal ρ) := by
2095      haveI := isProb_withDensityReal hρ
2096      exact Integrable.of_bound (hθm.sub hθ'm).abs.aestronglyMeasurable Cb
2097        (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => by rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_abs]; exact hθb x)
2098    have h1 : ∫ x, |θ x - θ' x| ∂(withDensityReal ρ) = ∫ x, ρ x * |θ x - θ' x| := ResponseAux.integral_dens hρ _
2099    have hint2 : Integrable (fun x => ρ x * |θ x - θ' x|) volume := by
2100      refine Integrable.mono' ((ResponseAux.integrable_rho hρ).mul_const Cb)
2101        (hρ.measurable.mul (hθm.sub hθ'm).abs).aestronglyMeasurable (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => ?_)
2102      rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (mul_nonneg (hρ.nonneg x) (abs_nonneg _))]
2103      exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hθb x) (hρ.nonneg x)
2104    have e : ∀ y, ‖w y‖ₑ = ENNReal.ofReal (ρ y * |θ y - θ' y|) := by
2105      intro y
2106      rw [hw]
2107      simp only
2108      rw [← Real.enorm_of_nonneg (mul_nonneg (hρ.nonneg y) (abs_nonneg _)), enorm_mul, enorm_mul]
2109      simp [Real.enorm_of_nonneg (hρ.nonneg y)]
2110    simp_rw [e]
2111    rw [← ofReal_integral_eq_lintegral_ofReal hint2
2112      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => mul_nonneg (hρ.nonneg x) (abs_nonneg _)), ← h1]
2113    exact ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal hη
2114  -- step d: `L²` norm of `w`
2115  have hd : ∫⁻ y, ‖w y‖ₑ ^ 2 ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (Λ * Cb) * ENNReal.ofReal η := by
2116    calc ∫⁻ y, ‖w y‖ₑ ^ 2 ≤ ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (Λ * Cb) * ‖w y‖ₑ := by
2117          refine lintegral_mono fun y => ?_
2118          rw [sq]
2119          gcongr
2120          rw [Real.enorm_eq_ofReal_abs]
2121          refine ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal ?_
2122          rw [hw]; simp only
2123          rw [abs_mul, abs_of_nonneg (hρ.nonneg y)]
2124          exact mul_le_mul (hΛ y) (hθb y) (abs_nonneg _) hΛ0
2125      _ = ENNReal.ofReal (Λ * Cb) * ∫⁻ y, ‖w y‖ₑ := lintegral_const_mul' _ _ ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top
2126      _ ≤ _ := by gcongr
2127  have hconv := conv_sq_le hqm hq0 hwm hK
2128  have hd' : ∫⁻ y, ‖w y‖ₑ ^ 2 ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (Λ * Cb * η) := by
2129    rw [ENNReal.ofReal_mul (mul_nonneg hΛ0 hCb)]; exact hd
2130  refine ha.trans (mul_le_mul_right (hconv.trans ?_) _)
2131  gcongr
2132
2133end ConvL2
2134
2135section CavEq
2136
2137variable {d M : ℕ} {μ : Measure (Torus d)} {q gL : Torus d → ℝ}
2138  {Ψ : Torus d → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin M)} {γ : ℝ} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ]
2139  {β : ℝ} {n : ℕ}
2140
2141/-- **The exact cavity equation for the reference marginal** (`A:eq:reference-cavity-equation`):
2142`log θ + β (g_L + q) * (π - μ) = D + β q*(π - ν) + λ q*ν + const`, with `θ = dπ/dμ`, `ν` the
2143cavity marginal, `D = log u + m` the cavity correction and `m = λ n q*ν`. -/
2144theorem cavity_equation (h : RefHyp μ q gL Ψ γ) (hβ : 0 < β) {K : ℝ}
2145    (ht : ∀ x, |inner ℝ (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) (Xi μ Ψ x)| ≤ K) [NeZero n]
2146    {Cz : ℝ} (hCz : ∀ᵐ y ∂μ, |theta (weakPotK h hβ.le (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) ht)
2147      (β / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) n y - 1| ≤ Cz) :
2148    ∃ c : ℝ, ∀ x, Real.log (theta (weakPotK h hβ.le (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) ht) (β / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) n x) +
2149      β * ResponseAux.Kt (fun w => gL w + q w) μ
2150        (fun y => theta (weakPotK h hβ.le (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) ht) (β / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) n y - 1) x =
2151      (Real.log (cavU (weakPotK h hβ.le (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) ht) (β / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) n
2152          (fun e : Empty => e.elim) x) +
2153        β / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) * n *
2154          ∫ y, (weakPotK h hβ.le (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) ht).q y x
2155            ∂(cavMarg (weakPotK h hβ.le (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) ht) (β / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) n)) +
2156      β * ((∫ y, q (x - y) * theta (weakPotK h hβ.le (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) ht) (β / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) n y ∂μ) -
2157        ∫ y, q (x - y) ∂(cavMarg (weakPotK h hβ.le (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) ht) (β / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) n)) +
2158      β / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) *
2159        ∫ y, q (x - y) ∂(cavMarg (weakPotK h hβ.le (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) ht) (β / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) n) + c := by
2160  set V := weakPotK h hβ.le (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) ht with hV
2161  set lam : ℝ := β / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) with hlam
2162  have hlam0 : 0 ≤ lam := div_nonneg hβ.le (Nat.cast_nonneg _)
2163  set θ := theta V lam n with hθ
2164  set ν := cavMarg V lam n with hν
2165  have hθ0 : ∀ x, 0 < θ x := theta_pos V hlam0 n
2166  have hθ1 : ∫ x, θ x ∂μ = 1 := integral_theta V hlam0 n
2167  have hθi : Integrable θ μ := integrable_theta V hlam0 n
2168  have hθm : Measurable θ := measurable_theta V hlam0 n
2169  obtain ⟨c₀, hc₀⟩ := hh_eq h hβ ht (n := n)
2170  have hlamn : lam * (n + 1 : ℕ) = β := by
2171    rw [hlam]; field_simp
2172  have hVqx : ∀ x y, V.q y x = q (x - y) := fun x y => by
2173    show q (y - x) = q (x - y)
2174    rw [← h.q_even (x - y), neg_sub]
2175  refine ⟨c₀ - Real.log (∫ x', cavTheta V lam n (fun e : Empty => e.elim) x' ∂μ), fun x => ?_⟩
2176  have e0 := log_theta_eq V hlam0 n x
2177  have e1 : V.φ x = β * convMeasure q μ x + inner ℝ (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) (Xi μ Ψ x) := rfl
2178  have e2 := hc₀ x
2179  -- integrability
2180  have hzi : Integrable (fun y => θ y - 1) μ := hθi.sub (integrable_const _)
2181  have hgi : Integrable (fun y => gL (x - y) * (θ y - 1)) μ := by
2182    refine Integrable.bdd_mul (c := gL 0) hzi (measurable_gL_sub h x).aestronglyMeasurable
2183      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => ?_)
2184    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs]; exact abs_gL_le h _
2185  have hzb : ∀ᵐ y ∂μ, ‖θ y - 1‖ ≤ Cz := hCz.mono fun y hy => by rwa [Real.norm_eq_abs]
2186  have hqi : Integrable (fun y => q (x - y) * (θ y - 1)) μ := by
2187    have := Integrable.bdd_mul (c := Cz) (h.q_int x) hzi.aestronglyMeasurable hzb
2188    exact this.congr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => by simp [mul_comm])
2189  have hq1 : Integrable (fun y => q (x - y)) μ := h.q_int x
2190  have hqθ : Integrable (fun y => q (x - y) * θ y) μ := by
2191    have := hqi.add hq1
2192    exact this.congr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => by simp only [Pi.add_apply]; ring)
2193  have hKt : ResponseAux.Kt (fun w => gL w + q w) μ (fun y => θ y - 1) x =
2194      ResponseAux.Kt gL μ (fun y => θ y - 1) x +
2195        ((∫ y, q (x - y) * θ y ∂μ) - convMeasure q μ x) := by
2196    unfold ResponseAux.Kt
2197    simp_rw [add_mul]
2198    rw [integral_add hgi hqi]
2199    congr 1
2200    simp_rw [mul_sub, mul_one]
2201    rw [integral_sub hqθ hq1]
2202    rfl
2203  rw [hKt]
2204  have hm : (∫ y, V.q y x ∂ν) = ∫ y, q (x - y) ∂ν := by simp_rw [hVqx]
2205  rw [hm]
2206  rw [e0, e1, e2]
2207  have hn1 : lam * n = β - lam := by
2208    have : lam * ((n : ℝ) + 1) = β := by exact_mod_cast hlamn
2209    linarith
2210  have : lam * ↑n * ∫ y, q (x - y) ∂ν = (β - lam) * ∫ y, q (x - y) ∂ν := by rw [hn1]
2211  linarith
2212
2213end CavEq

2215section Event2

2217variable {d M : ℕ} {μ : Measure (Torus d)} {q gL : Torus d → ℝ}
2218  {Ψ : Torus d → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin M)} {γ : ℝ} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ]

2221/-- **Cavity versus full marginal** (`A:eq:weak-pin-comparison`): `|π_N(A) - ν_n(A)| ≤ κ/N`. -/
2222theorem event_pi_nu (h : RefHyp μ q gL Ψ γ) (hβ : 0 < β) {K : ℝ}
2223    (ht : ∀ x, |inner ℝ (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) (Xi μ Ψ x)| ≤ K) [NeZero n]
2224    (hκ : weakKappa (weakPotK h hβ.le (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) ht) β ≤ 1 / 2) {A : Set (Torus d)}
2225    (hA : MeasurableSet A) :
2226    |(firstMarginal (RN q μ Ψ β (n + 1))).real A -
2227        (cavMarg (weakPotK h hβ.le (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) ht) (β / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) n).real A| ≤
2228      weakKappa (weakPotK h hβ.le (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) ht) β / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) := by
2229  set V := weakPotK h hβ.le (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) ht with hV
2230  have hRN := RN_eq_fieldLawK h hβ.le ht (N := n + 1)
2231  rw [← hV] at hRN
2232  have hn : 0 < n := Nat.pos_of_ne_zero (NeZero.ne n)
2233  have h1 := rvh_pin_removed (B := Empty) V hβ.le (N := n + 1) (n := n) (Nat.succ_pos n) (by simp)
2234    (fun e : Empty => e.elim) hκ (Fin.last n) ⟨0, hn⟩ hA
2235  rw [pinnedLaw_empty, pinnedLaw_empty] at h1
2236  have e : (Fin.last n).succAbove (⟨0, hn⟩ : Fin n) = ⟨0, Nat.succ_pos n⟩ := by
2237    ext; simp [Fin.succAbove, Fin.lt_def]; omega
2238  rw [e] at h1
2239  unfold firstMarginal cavMarg
2240  rw [dif_pos (Nat.succ_pos n), hRN, pinnedLaw_empty]
2241  exact h1
2242
2243end Event2

2249/-- `q*π - q*ν = q * (ρ(θ - θ'))` for two measures with bounded densities. -/
2250theorem cv_identity {ρ : Torus d → ℝ} (hρ : IsProbDensity ρ) {Λ : ℝ} (hΛ : ∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ)
2251    {q : Torus d → ℝ} (hqm : Measurable q) (hq0 : ∀ z, 0 ≤ q z) (hqi : Integrable q)
2252    {θ θ' : Torus d → ℝ} (hθm : Measurable θ) (hθ'm : Measurable θ') (hθ0 : ∀ x, 0 ≤ θ x)
2253    (hθ'0 : ∀ x, 0 ≤ θ' x) {Cb : ℝ} (hθb : ∀ x, θ x ≤ Cb) (hθ'b : ∀ x, θ' x ≤ Cb) (x : Torus d) :
2254    (∫ y, q (x - y) * θ y ∂(withDensityReal ρ)) -
2255        ∫ y, q (x - y) ∂((withDensityReal ρ).withDensity fun y => ENNReal.ofReal (θ' y)) =
2256      ResponseAux.cv q (fun y => ρ y * (θ y - θ' y)) x := by
2257  haveI : (volume : Measure (Torus d)).IsNegInvariant :=
2258    MeasureTheory.Measure.pi.isNegInvariant (fun _ : Fin d => (volume : Measure UnitAddCircle))
2259  have hq1 : Integrable (fun y => q (x - y)) volume := hqi.comp_sub_left x
2260  have hρm := hρ.measurable
2261  have hb : ∀ (f : Torus d → ℝ), Measurable f → (∀ y, 0 ≤ f y) → (∀ y, f y ≤ Cb) →
2262      Integrable (fun y => q (x - y) * (ρ y * f y)) volume := by
2263    intro f hf hf0 hfb
2264    refine Integrable.mono' (hq1.mul_const (Λ * Cb))
2265      ((hqm.comp (measurable_const.sub measurable_id)).mul (hρm.mul hf)).aestronglyMeasurable
2266      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => ?_)
2267    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (mul_nonneg (hq0 _) (mul_nonneg (hρ.nonneg y) (hf0 y)))]
2268    exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (mul_le_mul (hΛ y) (hfb y) (hf0 y) (le_trans (hρ.nonneg y) (hΛ y))) (hq0 _)
2269  have e1 : ∫ y, q (x - y) * θ y ∂(withDensityReal ρ) = ∫ y, q (x - y) * (ρ y * θ y) := by
2270    rw [ResponseAux.integral_dens hρ]
2271    refine integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => ?_)
2272    ring
2273  have e2 : ∫ y, q (x - y) ∂((withDensityReal ρ).withDensity fun y => ENNReal.ofReal (θ' y)) =
2274      ∫ y, q (x - y) * (ρ y * θ' y) := by
2275    rw [integral_withDensity_eq_integral_toReal_smul (f := fun y => ENNReal.ofReal (θ' y))
2276      (ENNReal.measurable_ofReal.comp hθ'm) (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun _ => ENNReal.ofReal_lt_top)]
2277    simp_rw [ENNReal.toReal_ofReal (hθ'0 _), smul_eq_mul]
2278    rw [ResponseAux.integral_dens hρ]
2279    refine integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => ?_)
2280    ring
2281  rw [e1, e2, ← integral_sub (hb θ hθm hθ0 hθb) (hb θ' hθ'm hθ'0 hθ'b)]
2282  unfold ResponseAux.cv
2283  refine integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => ?_)
2284  ring
2285
2286end CvId

2292/-- The `L²(μ)` estimate of `q * (ρ(θ - θ'))` at the rate `N⁻¹ g(N)` (`K = N`), with `T₀, c₁` constants. -/
2293theorem cv_L2_rate (hd : 2 ≤ d) {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d)
2294    {q : Torus d → ℝ} (hqm : Measurable q) (hq0 : ∀ z, 0 ≤ q z) {K0 : ℝ} (hK0 : 0 < K0)
2295    (hw : ∀ r : ℝ, 0 < r → (volume : Measure (Torus d)) {z : Torus d | r < q z} ≤
2296      ENNReal.ofReal (K0 * r ^ (-(pOf d s)))) :
2297    ∃ T₀ c₁ : ℝ, 0 ≤ T₀ ∧ 0 < c₁ ∧ ∀ (ρ : Torus d → ℝ), IsProbDensity ρ → ∀ {Λ : ℝ}, (∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ) → 0 ≤ Λ →
2298      ∀ {θ θ' : Torus d → ℝ}, Measurable θ → Measurable θ' → ∀ {Cb : ℝ}, 0 ≤ Cb →
2299      (∀ x, |θ x - θ' x| ≤ Cb) → ∀ {η : ℝ}, 0 ≤ η → ∫ x, |θ x - θ' x| ∂(withDensityReal ρ) ≤ η →
2300      ∀ {N : ℕ}, 1 ≤ N →
2301      ∫⁻ x, ‖ResponseAux.cv q (fun y => ρ y * (θ y - θ' y)) x‖ₑ ^ 2 ∂(withDensityReal ρ) ≤
2302        ENNReal.ofReal (Λ * (2 * ((T₀ * ((N : ℝ)⁻¹ * gFun d s N)) ^ 2 * (Λ * Cb * η) +
2303          η ^ 2 * (c₁ * gFun d s N)))) := by
2304  have hp := one_lt_pOf hd hs0 hsd
2305  set p := pOf d s with hpdef
2306  obtain ⟨c₁, hc₁, hR2⟩ := second_moment_gFun hd hs0 hsd hK0
2307  have hpp : 0 < p - 1 := by linarith
2308  have hT00 : 0 ≤ K0 * (p / (p - 1)) := by positivity
2309  refine ⟨K0 * (p / (p - 1)), c₁, hT00, hc₁, ?_⟩
2310  intro ρ hρ Λ hΛ hΛ0 θ θ' hθm hθ'm Cb hCb hθb η hη0 hη N hN
2311  have hN1 : (1 : ℝ) ≤ N := by exact_mod_cast hN
2312  have hNpos : (0 : ℝ) < N := by linarith
2313  have h1 := cv_L2_bound hρ hΛ hΛ0 hqm hq0 hθm hθ'm hCb hθb hη0 hη (K := (N : ℝ)) hNpos.le
2314  refine h1.trans ?_
2315  have hT : ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (q y - N) ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (K0 * (p / (p - 1)) * ((N : ℝ)⁻¹ * gFun d s N)) := by
2316    refine (lintegral_tail_le hqm hq0 hNpos hp hK0 hw).trans (ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal ?_)
2317    have h2 := rpow_one_sub_le_gFun hd hs0 hsd hN1
2318    calc K0 * (N : ℝ) ^ (1 - p) * (p / (p - 1)) = (K0 * (p / (p - 1))) * (N : ℝ) ^ (1 - p) := by ring
2319      _ ≤ (K0 * (p / (p - 1))) * ((N : ℝ)⁻¹ * gFun d s N) := by gcongr
2320  have hR : ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (min (q y) N ^ 2) ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (c₁ * gFun d s N) :=
2321    (lintegral_minsq_le hqm hq0 hN1 hp hK0 hw).trans (ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal (hR2 N hN1))
2322  have hg1 : 1 ≤ gFun d s N := one_le_gFun hd hs0 hsd hN1
2323  have hT0 : 0 ≤ K0 * (p / (p - 1)) * ((N : ℝ)⁻¹ * gFun d s N) := by positivity
2324  have : ENNReal.ofReal Λ * (2 * ((∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (q y - N)) ^ 2 * ENNReal.ofReal (Λ * Cb * η) +
2325      ENNReal.ofReal η ^ 2 * ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (min (q y) N ^ 2))) ≤
2326      ENNReal.ofReal Λ * (2 * (ENNReal.ofReal (K0 * (p / (p - 1)) * ((N : ℝ)⁻¹ * gFun d s N)) ^ 2 *
2327        ENNReal.ofReal (Λ * Cb * η) + ENNReal.ofReal η ^ 2 * ENNReal.ofReal (c₁ * gFun d s N))) := by
2328    gcongr
2329  refine this.trans (le_of_eq ?_)
2330  have hη2 : 0 ≤ η := hη0
2331  rw [← ENNReal.ofReal_pow hT0, ← ENNReal.ofReal_pow hη2,
2332    ← ENNReal.ofReal_mul (by positivity), ← ENNReal.ofReal_mul (by positivity),
2333    ← ENNReal.ofReal_add (by positivity) (by have := hc₁; positivity)]
2334  rw [ENNReal.ofReal_mul hΛ0, ENNReal.ofReal_mul (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 2), ENNReal.ofReal_ofNat]
2335
2336end CoreCv

2342theorem measurable_integral_kernel {α β : Type*} [MeasurableSpace α] [MeasurableSpace β] {ν : Measure β}
2343    [SFinite ν] {F : α × β → ℝ} (hF : Measurable F) : Measurable fun x => ∫ y, F (x, y) ∂ν :=
2344  (hF.stronglyMeasurable.integral_prod_right' (ν := ν)).measurable

2346theorem eLpNorm_two_le_const {α : Type*} [MeasurableSpace α] {μ : Measure α} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ]
2347    {f : α → ℝ} (hf : AEStronglyMeasurable f μ) {C : ℝ} (hC : ∀ x, |f x| ≤ C) :
2348    eLpNorm f 2 μ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal C := by
2349  refine (eLpNorm_le_of_ae_bound hf (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => by
2350    simpa [Real.norm_eq_abs] using hC x)).trans ?_
2351  simp

2353theorem core_L2 (hd : 2 ≤ d) {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {Λ : ℝ} (hΛ1 : 1 ≤ Λ) {β : ℝ} (hβ : 0 < β)
2354    {q gL : Torus d → ℝ} {Ψ : Torus d → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin M)} {c : ℝ} (hc0 : 0 ≤ c)
2355    (hRef : ∀ ρ : Torus d → ℝ, IsProbDensity ρ → (∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ) →
2356      RefHyp (withDensityReal ρ) q gL Ψ (Λ * c))
2357    (hqm : Measurable q) (hq0 : ∀ z, 0 ≤ q z) (hqe : ∀ z, q (-z) = q z) (hqi : Integrable q)
2358    (hqc : ∫⁻ z, ENNReal.ofReal (q z) ≤ ENNReal.ofReal c)
2359    {K0 : ℝ} (hK0 : 0 < K0)
2360    (hw : ∀ r : ℝ, 0 < r → (volume : Measure (Torus d)) {z : Torus d | r < q z} ≤
2361      ENNReal.ofReal (K0 * r ^ (-(pOf d s))))
2362    (hkm : Measurable (fun w => gL w + q w)) (hki : Integrable (fun w => gL w + q w))
2363    (ĝ : Lattice d → ℝ) (hĝ : ∀ n, 0 ≤ ĝ n)
2364    (hcoef : ∀ n, ∫ x, Torus.mode (-n) x * (((gL x + q x : ℝ)) : ℂ) = (ĝ n : ℂ))
2365    (hκ : KappaSmall β (Λ * c)) :
2366    ∃ (C : ℝ) (N₁ : ℕ), ∀ ρ : Torus d → ℝ, IsProbDensity ρ → (∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ) → ∀ N : ℕ, N₁ ≤ N →
2367      ∀ (ht : ∀ x, |inner ℝ (tN q (withDensityReal ρ) Ψ β N) (Xi (withDensityReal ρ) Ψ x)| ≤ 1),
2368      firstMarginal (RN q (withDensityReal ρ) Ψ β N) ≪ withDensityReal ρ ∧
2369        eLpNorm (fun y => ((firstMarginal (RN q (withDensityReal ρ) Ψ β N)).rnDeriv
2370          (withDensityReal ρ) y).toReal - 1) 2 (withDensityReal ρ) ≤
2371          ENNReal.ofReal (C * deltaN d s N) := by
2372  set γ : ℝ := Λ * c with hγ
2373  have hΛ0 : 0 ≤ Λ := by linarith
2374  have hγ0 : 0 ≤ γ := mul_nonneg hΛ0 hc0
2375  set Cν : ℝ := Real.exp ((β * γ + 1) + 0 + β * γ) with hCν
2376  have hCν1 : 1 ≤ Cν := Real.one_le_exp (by positivity)
2377  obtain ⟨CD, ND, hCD0, hD⟩ := cavD_torus hd hs0 hsd hΛ1 hqm hq0 hqe hc0 hqc hK0 hw hβ hCν1
2378  obtain ⟨T₀, c₁, hT₀, hc₁, hconv⟩ := cv_L2_rate hd hs0 hsd hqm hq0 hK0 hw
2379  set κ₀ : ℝ := 4 * Cν * (β * γ) with hκ₀
2380  set E : ℝ := Λ * (2 * (T₀ ^ 2 * (Λ * Cν * (2 * κ₀)) + (2 * κ₀) ^ 2 * c₁)) with hE
2381  have hE0 : 0 ≤ E := by positivity
2382  set Ccv : ℝ := Real.sqrt E with hCcv
2383  set Cphi : ℝ := CD + β * Ccv + β * (Cν * (Λ * c)) with hCphi
2384  have hCphi0 : 0 ≤ Cphi := by positivity
2385  refine ⟨Cν * Cphi, max ND 2, ?_⟩
2386  intro ρ hρ hΛρ N hN ht
2387  obtain ⟨n, rfl⟩ : ∃ n, N = n + 1 := ⟨N - 1, by omega⟩
2388  have hn : 1 ≤ n := by omega
2389  haveI : NeZero n := ⟨by omega⟩
2390  haveI := isProb_withDensityReal hρ
2391  set μ := withDensityReal ρ with hμ
2392  have h := hRef ρ hρ hΛρ
2393  set V := weakPotK h hβ.le (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) ht with hV
2394  set lam : ℝ := β / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) with hlam
2395  have hlam0 : 0 ≤ lam := div_nonneg hβ.le (Nat.cast_nonneg _)
2396  set θ := theta V lam n with hθdef
2397  have hθ0 : ∀ x, 0 < θ x := theta_pos V hlam0 n
2398  have hθm : Measurable θ := measurable_theta V hlam0 n
2399  have hθ1 : ∫ x, θ x ∂μ = 1 := integral_theta V hlam0 n
2400  have hθi : Integrable θ μ := integrable_theta V hlam0 n
2401  have hVq : ∀ x y, V.q x y = q (x - y) := fun _ _ => rfl
2402  have hVC : Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + β * V.γ) ≤ Cν := le_of_eq rfl
2403  have hκV : weakKappa V β ≤ 1 / 2 := weakKappa_weakPotK_le h hβ.le ht hκ
2404  have hN0 : 0 < n + 1 := Nat.succ_pos n
2405  set ν := cavMarg V lam n with hν
2406  haveI hνp : IsProbabilityMeasure ν := by
2407    haveI := isProb_pinnedLaw V hlam0 n (fun e : Empty => e.elim)
2408    have : IsProbabilityMeasure (cavMarg V lam n) := by unfold cavMarg; infer_instance
2409    exact this
2410  have hπle : firstMarginal (RN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) ≤ ENNReal.ofReal Cν • μ :=
2411    firstMarginal_RN_leK h hβ hN0 ht
2412  have hπeq := firstMarginal_RN_eq h hβ ht (n := n)
2413  rw [← hV] at hπeq
2414  have hνle : ν ≤ ENNReal.ofReal Cν • μ := by
2415    have := marginal_le_smul (B := Empty) V hβ.le hN0 (n := n) (by simp) (fun e : Empty => e.elim) 0
2416    exact this
2417  haveI : IsFiniteMeasure (firstMarginal (RN q μ Ψ β (n + 1))) :=
2418    ⟨lt_of_le_of_lt (hπle Set.univ) (by simp [Measure.smul_apply])⟩
2419  have hπ_ac := PoleCavityAux.rnDeriv_le_of_le_withDensity μ (firstMarginal (RN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)))
2420    (fun _ => ENNReal.ofReal Cν) (by rw [withDensity_const]; exact hπle)
2421  have hrn : (firstMarginal (RN q μ Ψ β (n + 1))).rnDeriv μ =ᵐ[μ] fun y => ENNReal.ofReal (θ y) := by
2422    rw [hπeq]; exact Measure.rnDeriv_withDensity μ (ENNReal.measurable_ofReal.comp hθm)
2423  have hθC : ∀ᵐ y ∂μ, θ y ≤ Cν := by
2424    filter_upwards [hrn, hπ_ac.2] with y h1 h2
2425    rw [h1] at h2
2426    exact (ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal_iff (by linarith)).1 h2
2427  refine ⟨hπ_ac.1, ?_⟩
2428  have hfin : (fun y => ((firstMarginal (RN q μ Ψ β (n + 1))).rnDeriv μ y).toReal - 1) =ᵐ[μ]
2429      fun y => θ y - 1 := by
2430    filter_upwards [hrn] with y h1
2431    rw [h1, ENNReal.toReal_ofReal (hθ0 y).le]
2432  rw [eLpNorm_congr_ae hfin]
2433  have hCz : ∀ᵐ y ∂μ, |θ y - 1| ≤ Cν := hθC.mono fun y hy => by
2434    rw [abs_le]; constructor <;> linarith [hθ0 y]
2435  obtain ⟨c', hc'⟩ := cavity_equation h hβ ht (n := n) (Cz := Cν) hCz
2436  -- clipped densities
2437  have hCν0 : 0 ≤ Cν := by linarith
2438  set θc : Torus d → ℝ := fun y => min (θ y) Cν with hθc
2439  have hθcm : Measurable θc := hθm.min measurable_const
2440  have hθc0 : ∀ y, 0 ≤ θc y := fun y => le_min (hθ0 y).le hCν0
2441  have hθcb : ∀ y, θc y ≤ Cν := fun y => min_le_right _ _
2442  have hθc_ae : ∀ᵐ y ∂μ, θc y = θ y := hθC.mono fun y hy => min_eq_left hy
2443  have hνac : ν ≪ μ := Measure.absolutelyContinuous_of_le_smul hνle
2444  haveI : IsFiniteMeasure ν := inferInstance
2445  have hν_rn := PoleCavityAux.rnDeriv_le_of_le_withDensity μ ν (fun _ => ENNReal.ofReal Cν)
2446    (by rw [withDensity_const]; exact hνle)
2447  set θ' : Torus d → ℝ := fun y => (ν.rnDeriv μ y).toReal with hθ'
2448  set θ'c : Torus d → ℝ := fun y => min (θ' y) Cν with hθ'c
2449  have hθ'm : Measurable θ' := (Measure.measurable_rnDeriv ν μ).ennreal_toReal
2450  have hθ'cm : Measurable θ'c := hθ'm.min measurable_const
2451  have hθ'c0 : ∀ y, 0 ≤ θ'c y := fun y => le_min ENNReal.toReal_nonneg hCν0
2452  have hθ'cb : ∀ y, θ'c y ≤ Cν := fun y => min_le_right _ _
2453  have hνeq : ν = μ.withDensity (fun y => ENNReal.ofReal (θ'c y)) := by
2454    calc ν = μ.withDensity (ν.rnDeriv μ) := (Measure.withDensity_rnDeriv_eq ν μ hνac).symm
2455      _ = _ := by
2456        refine withDensity_congr_ae ?_
2457        filter_upwards [Measure.rnDeriv_lt_top ν μ, hν_rn.2] with y h1 h2
2458        have h3 : (ν.rnDeriv μ y).toReal ≤ Cν := ENNReal.toReal_le_of_le_ofReal hCν0 h2
2459        simp only [hθ'c, hθ', min_eq_left h3, ENNReal.ofReal_toReal h1.ne]
2460  have hπeq' : firstMarginal (RN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) = μ.withDensity (fun y => ENNReal.ofReal (θc y)) := by
2461    rw [hπeq]
2462    refine withDensity_congr_ae ?_
2463    filter_upwards [hθc_ae] with y hy
2464    rw [hy]
2465  have hθci : Integrable θc μ := Integrable.of_bound hθcm.aestronglyMeasurable Cν
2466    (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => by rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (hθc0 y)]; exact hθcb y)
2467  have hθ'ci : Integrable θ'c μ := Integrable.of_bound hθ'cm.aestronglyMeasurable Cν
2468    (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => by rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (hθ'c0 y)]; exact hθ'cb y)
2469  have hTV : ∫ y, |θc y - θ'c y| ∂μ ≤ 2 * (weakKappa V β / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) := by
2470    refine integral_abs_sub_le hθcm hθ'cm hθc0 hθ'c0 hθci hθ'ci (fun A hA => ?_)
2471    rw [← hπeq', ← hνeq]
2472    exact event_pi_nu h hβ ht hκV hA
2473  -- the convolution term
2474  set w : Torus d → ℝ := fun y => ρ y * (θc y - θ'c y) with hw
2475  have hcvid : ∀ x, (∫ y, q (x - y) * θ y ∂μ) - ∫ y, q (x - y) ∂ν = ResponseAux.cv q w x := by
2476    intro x
2477    have e1 : ∫ y, q (x - y) * θ y ∂μ = ∫ y, q (x - y) * θc y ∂μ :=
2478      integral_congr_ae (hθc_ae.mono fun y hy => by show q (x - y) * θ y = q (x - y) * θc y; rw [hy])
2479    rw [e1]
2480    have := cv_identity hρ hΛρ hqm hq0 hqi hθcm hθ'cm hθc0 hθ'c0 hθcb hθ'cb x
2481    rw [← hνeq] at this
2482    exact this
2483  set Dfun : Torus d → ℝ := fun x => Real.log (cavU V lam n (fun e : Empty => e.elim) x) +
2484    lam * n * ∫ y, V.q y x ∂ν with hDfun
2485  set Qν : Torus d → ℝ := fun x => ∫ y, q (x - y) ∂ν with hQν
2486  set Φ : Torus d → ℝ := fun x => Dfun x + β * ResponseAux.cv q w x + lam * Qν x with hΦ
2487  have heq : ∀ x, Real.log (θ x) + β * ResponseAux.Kt (fun w => gL w + q w) μ (fun y => θ y - 1) x =
2488      Φ x + c' := by
2489    intro x
2490    rw [hc' x]
2491    simp only [hΦ, hDfun, hQν]
2492    rw [← hcvid x]
2493  -- the rate `δ = N⁻¹ g(N)`
2494  set N : ℝ := ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) with hNdef
2495  have hN2 : (2 : ℝ) ≤ N := by
2496    have : 2 ≤ n + 1 := by omega
2497    show (2 : ℝ) ≤ ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)
2498    exact_mod_cast this
2499  have hNpos : 0 < N := by linarith
2500  have hN1 : (1 : ℝ) ≤ N := by linarith
2501  have hδ : deltaN d s (n + 1) = N⁻¹ * gFun d s N := deltaN_eq_gFun hd hs0 hsd (by omega)
2502  have hg1 : 1 ≤ gFun d s N := one_le_gFun hd hs0 hsd hN1
2503  set δ : ℝ := deltaN d s (n + 1) with hδdef
2504  have hδ0 : 0 ≤ δ := by rw [hδ]; positivity
2505  have hδ1 : N⁻¹ ≤ δ := by
2506    rw [hδ]
2507    calc N⁻¹ = N⁻¹ * 1 := (mul_one _).symm
2508      _ ≤ N⁻¹ * gFun d s N := by gcongr
2509  have hlam_le : lam ≤ β * δ := by
2510    have : lam = β * N⁻¹ := by rw [hlam]; ring
2511    rw [this]; gcongr
2512  -- the cavity correction `D`
2513  have hDb : ∀ x, 0 ≤ Dfun x ∧ Dfun x ≤ CD * δ := fun x =>
2514    hD ρ hρ hΛρ V hVq hVC hκV (n + 1) n (by omega) (Nat.le_succ n) x
2515  have hDm : Measurable Dfun := by
2516    refine ((Real.measurable_log.comp (measurable_cavU V hlam0 n _)).add (measurable_const.mul ?_))
2517    exact measurable_integral_kernel (F := fun p : Torus d × Torus d => V.q p.2 p.1)
2518      (V.measurable_q.comp (measurable_snd.prodMk measurable_fst))
2519  have hDL2 : eLpNorm Dfun 2 μ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (CD * δ) :=
2520    eLpNorm_two_le_const hDm.aestronglyMeasurable fun x => by
2521      rw [abs_of_nonneg (hDb x).1]; exact (hDb x).2
2522  -- the convolution term
2523  have hwm : Measurable w := hρ.measurable.mul (hθcm.sub hθ'cm)
2524  have hcvm : Measurable (ResponseAux.cv q w) := by
2525    unfold ResponseAux.cv
2526    exact measurable_integral_kernel (F := fun p : Torus d × Torus d => q (p.1 - p.2) * w p.2)
2527      ((hqm.comp (measurable_fst.sub measurable_snd)).mul (hwm.comp measurable_snd))
2528  have hκ0 : 0 ≤ weakKappa V β := weakKappa_nonneg V hβ.le
2529  have hκeq : weakKappa V β = κ₀ := rfl
2530  set η : ℝ := 2 * (weakKappa V β / N) with hη
2531  have hη0 : 0 ≤ η := by positivity
2532  have hb : ∀ y, |θc y - θ'c y| ≤ Cν := fun y => by
2533    rw [abs_le]; constructor <;> linarith [hθc0 y, hθ'c0 y, hθcb y, hθ'cb y]
2534  have h1 := hconv ρ hρ hΛρ hΛ0 hθcm hθ'cm hCν0 hb hη0 hTV (N := n + 1) (by omega)
2535  have hE' : Λ * (2 * ((T₀ * (N⁻¹ * gFun d s N)) ^ 2 * (Λ * Cν * η) + η ^ 2 * (c₁ * gFun d s N))) ≤
2536      E * δ ^ 2 := by
2537    have ha0 : 0 < N⁻¹ := inv_pos.2 hNpos
2538    have ha1 : N⁻¹ ≤ 1 := inv_le_one_of_one_le₀ hN1
2539    set a : ℝ := N⁻¹ with ha
2540    set g : ℝ := gFun d s N with hg
2541    have hηa : η = 2 * κ₀ * a := by rw [hη, hκeq, div_eq_mul_inv]; ring
2542    have hX : δ ^ 2 = (a * g) ^ 2 := by rw [hδ]
2543    have hX0 : 0 ≤ (a * g) ^ 2 := sq_nonneg _
2544    have t1 : (T₀ * (a * g)) ^ 2 * (Λ * Cν * η) ≤ T₀ ^ 2 * (Λ * Cν * (2 * κ₀)) * (a * g) ^ 2 := by
2545      rw [hηa]
2546      have : (T₀ * (a * g)) ^ 2 * (Λ * Cν * (2 * κ₀ * a)) =
2547          (T₀ ^ 2 * (Λ * Cν * (2 * κ₀)) * (a * g) ^ 2) * a := by ring
2548      rw [this]
2549      have hnn : 0 ≤ T₀ ^ 2 * (Λ * Cν * (2 * κ₀)) * (a * g) ^ 2 := by positivity
2550      calc (T₀ ^ 2 * (Λ * Cν * (2 * κ₀)) * (a * g) ^ 2) * a ≤ (T₀ ^ 2 * (Λ * Cν * (2 * κ₀)) * (a * g) ^ 2) * 1 :=
2551            mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ha1 hnn
2552        _ = _ := mul_one _
2553    have t2 : η ^ 2 * (c₁ * g) ≤ (2 * κ₀) ^ 2 * c₁ * (a * g) ^ 2 := by
2554      rw [hηa]
2555      have : (2 * κ₀ * a) ^ 2 * (c₁ * g) = ((2 * κ₀) ^ 2 * c₁ * (a ^ 2 * g)) := by ring
2556      rw [this]
2557      have : (2 * κ₀) ^ 2 * c₁ * (a * g) ^ 2 = ((2 * κ₀) ^ 2 * c₁ * (a ^ 2 * g)) * g := by ring
2558      rw [this]
2559      have hnn : 0 ≤ (2 * κ₀) ^ 2 * c₁ * (a ^ 2 * g) := by positivity
2560      calc (2 * κ₀) ^ 2 * c₁ * (a ^ 2 * g) = ((2 * κ₀) ^ 2 * c₁ * (a ^ 2 * g)) * 1 := (mul_one _).symm
2561        _ ≤ _ := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hg1 hnn
2562    rw [hX]
2563    calc Λ * (2 * ((T₀ * (a * g)) ^ 2 * (Λ * Cν * η) + η ^ 2 * (c₁ * g)))
2564        ≤ Λ * (2 * (T₀ ^ 2 * (Λ * Cν * (2 * κ₀)) * (a * g) ^ 2 + (2 * κ₀) ^ 2 * c₁ * (a * g) ^ 2)) := by
2565          gcongr
2566      _ = E * (a * g) ^ 2 := by rw [hE]; ring
2567  have hAE : AEStronglyMeasurable (ResponseAux.cv q w) μ := hcvm.aestronglyMeasurable
2568  have hsq : (eLpNorm (ResponseAux.cv q w) 2 μ) ^ 2 ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (Ccv * δ) ^ 2 := by
2569    rw [ResponseAux.eLpNorm_two_sq hAE, ← ENNReal.ofReal_pow (by positivity), mul_pow, hCcv,
2570      Real.sq_sqrt hE0]
2571    exact h1.trans (ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal hE')
2572  have hcvL2 : eLpNorm (ResponseAux.cv q w) 2 μ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (Ccv * δ) :=
2573    (ENNReal.pow_le_pow_left_iff two_ne_zero).1 hsq
2574  -- the `λ q*ν` term
2575  have hQνm : Measurable Qν := measurable_integral_kernel (F := fun p : Torus d × Torus d => q (p.1 - p.2))
2576    (hqm.comp (measurable_fst.sub measurable_snd))
2577  have hQb : ∀ x, 0 ≤ Qν x ∧ Qν x ≤ Cν * (Λ * c) := fun x =>
2578    ⟨integral_nonneg fun y => hq0 _, integral_kernel_le hΛρ hΛ0 hνle hCν0 hqm hq0 hc0 hqc x⟩
2579  have hQL2 : eLpNorm (fun x => lam * Qν x) 2 μ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (β * (Cν * (Λ * c)) * δ) :=
2580    eLpNorm_two_le_const (hQνm.const_mul lam).aestronglyMeasurable fun x => by
2581      rw [abs_of_nonneg (mul_nonneg hlam0 (hQb x).1)]
2582      calc lam * Qν x ≤ (β * δ) * (Cν * (Λ * c)) :=
2583            mul_le_mul hlam_le (hQb x).2 (hQb x).1 (by positivity)
2584        _ = _ := by ring
2585  -- Minkowski
2586  have hΦe : Φ = (Dfun + fun x => β * ResponseAux.cv q w x) + fun x => lam * Qν x := by
2587    funext x; simp [hΦ]
2588  have hΦnorm : eLpNorm Φ 2 μ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (Cphi * δ) := by
2589    have m1 : AEStronglyMeasurable Dfun μ := hDm.aestronglyMeasurable
2590    have m2 : AEStronglyMeasurable (fun x => β * ResponseAux.cv q w x) μ :=
2591      (hcvm.const_mul β).aestronglyMeasurable
2592    have m3 : AEStronglyMeasurable (fun x => lam * Qν x) μ := (hQνm.const_mul lam).aestronglyMeasurable
2593    have e2 : eLpNorm (fun x => β * ResponseAux.cv q w x) 2 μ =
2594        ENNReal.ofReal β * eLpNorm (ResponseAux.cv q w) 2 μ := by
2595      have := eLpNorm_const_smul (p := 2) (μ := μ) β (ResponseAux.cv q w)
2596      rw [show (fun x => β * ResponseAux.cv q w x) = β • ResponseAux.cv q w from rfl, this,
2597        Real.enorm_of_nonneg hβ.le]
2598    rw [hΦe]
2599    refine (eLpNorm_add_le (by norm_num)).trans ?_
2600    refine (add_le_add_left (eLpNorm_add_le (by norm_num)) _).trans ?_
2601    rw [e2]
2602    calc eLpNorm Dfun 2 μ + ENNReal.ofReal β * eLpNorm (ResponseAux.cv q w) 2 μ +
2603          eLpNorm (fun x => lam * Qν x) 2 μ
2604        ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (CD * δ) + ENNReal.ofReal β * ENNReal.ofReal (Ccv * δ) +
2605          ENNReal.ofReal (β * (Cν * (Λ * c)) * δ) := by gcongr
2606      _ = ENNReal.ofReal (Cphi * δ) := by
2607          rw [← ENNReal.ofReal_mul hβ.le, ← ENNReal.ofReal_add (by positivity) (by positivity),
2608            ← ENNReal.ofReal_add (by positivity) (by positivity)]
2609          congr 1
2610          rw [hCphi]; ring
2611  have hΦm : Measurable Φ := (hDm.add (hcvm.const_mul β)).add (hQνm.const_mul lam)
2612  have hΦmem : MemLp Φ 2 μ := lt_of_le_of_lt hΦnorm ENNReal.ofReal_lt_top
2613  -- absorption
2614  have habs := absorption hρ hΛρ hkm hki ĝ hĝ hcoef hβ.le hθm hθ0 hCν1 hθC hθ1 hΦmem heq
2615  refine habs.trans ?_
2616  calc ENNReal.ofReal Cν * eLpNorm Φ 2 μ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal Cν * ENNReal.ofReal (Cphi * δ) := by gcongr
2617    _ = ENNReal.ofReal (Cν * Cphi * δ) := by
2618        rw [← ENNReal.ofReal_mul hCν0]; congr 1; ring
2619
2620end CoreSetup

2627/-- The two descriptions of the weak reference `R⁰_N` agree. -/
2628theorem R0_eq_R0small (h : RefHyp μ q gL Ψ γ) (hβ : 0 ≤ β) (N : ℕ) :
2629    R0 q μ β N = RefSmall.R0small q β μ N := by
2630  have e : E0 q μ β N = RefSmall.R0w q β μ N := by
2631    funext X
2632    unfold E0 RefSmall.R0w
2633    rw [← Finset.mul_sum, RefSmall.pairSum_eq_half h.q_even]
2634  have hRs : RefSmall.R0small q β μ N = tiltMeasure (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) (E0 q μ β N) := by
2635    rw [e]; rfl
2636  rw [hRs]
2637  unfold R0
2638  exact (RefSmall.tiltMeasure_eq_tilted _ _ (h.measurable_E0 β N) (β * N * γ) (h.E0_le hβ N)).symm
2639
2640/-- `∫ μ_N(Ξ) dP = ∫ Ξ dπ` when all one-coordinate marginals of `P` coincide. -/
2641theorem integral_empXi_eq (h : RefHyp μ q gL Ψ γ) {n : ℕ} (P : Measure (Config d (n + 1)))
2642    [IsProbabilityMeasure P] (hmarg : ∀ i : Fin (n + 1), P.map (fun X => X i) = P.map (fun X => X 0)) :
2643    ∫ X, empXi μ Ψ X ∂P = ∫ y, Xi μ Ψ y ∂(P.map (fun X => X 0)) := by
2644  unfold empXi
2645  have hint : ∀ i : Fin (n + 1), Integrable (fun X : Config d (n + 1) => Xi μ Ψ (X i)) P :=
2646    fun i => Integrable.of_bound (h.measurable_Xi.comp (measurable_pi_apply i)).aestronglyMeasurable
2647      (2 * Real.sqrt (gL 0)) (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun X => h.norm_Xi_le _)
2648  rw [integral_smul, integral_finsetSum _ fun i _ => hint i]
2649  have h2 : ∀ i : Fin (n + 1), ∫ X : Config d (n + 1), Xi μ Ψ (X i) ∂P =
2650      ∫ y, Xi μ Ψ y ∂(P.map (fun X => X 0)) := by
2651    intro i
2652    rw [← hmarg i, integral_map (measurable_pi_apply i).aemeasurable h.measurable_Xi.aestronglyMeasurable]
2653  simp_rw [h2]
2654  simp only [Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin]
2655  rw [← Nat.cast_smul_eq_nsmul ℝ, smul_smul]
2656  have : (1 / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) * ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) = 1 := by field_simp
2657  rw [this, one_smul]
2658
2659
2660theorem aN_nonneg {s : ℝ} {N : ℕ} (hN : 1 ≤ N) : 0 ≤ aN d s N := by
2661  unfold aN
2662  have hN0 : (1 : ℝ) ≤ N := by exact_mod_cast hN
2663  refine mul_nonneg ?_ (Real.rpow_nonneg (by linarith) _)
2664  split_ifs
2665  · have := Real.log_nonneg hN0; linarith
2666  · linarith
2667
2668open HRS.External.Entropy InformationTheory in
2669/-- **Uniform smallness of the calibration field** `‖t_N·Ξ‖_∞ → 0`, uniformly over the densities
2670`ρ ≤ Λ` (quantitative form of `sup_calibration_field_le_one`). -/
2671theorem small_field (hd : 2 ≤ d) {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {τ : ℝ} (hτ : 0 < τ)
2672    (hτ1 : τ ≤ 1) (L : ℕ) {Λ : ℝ} (hΛ1 : 1 ≤ Λ) {β : ℝ} (hβ : 0 < β)
2673    (Ψ : Torus d → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin M))
2674    (hΨ : ∀ x y, (weakSplit d s τ L).gL (x - y) = inner ℝ (Ψ x) (Ψ y)) :
2675    ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε → ∃ N₀ : ℕ, ∀ ρ : Torus d → ℝ, IsProbDensity ρ → (∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ) →
2676      ∀ N : ℕ, N₀ ≤ N → ∀ x : Torus d,
2677        |inner ℝ (tN (weakSplit d s τ L).q (withDensityReal ρ) Ψ β N) (Xi (withDensityReal ρ) Ψ x)| ≤ ε := by
2678  intro ε hε
2679  obtain ⟨C, hC0, N₁, hN₁2, hKL⟩ := RefSmall.klDiv_R0small_weakSplit_le hd hs0 hsd hτ L hΛ1 hβ.le
2680  set g0 : ℝ := (weakSplit d s τ L).gL 0 with hg0
2681  have hg00 : 0 ≤ g0 := by
2682    have := hΨ 0 0
2683    rw [sub_self] at this
2684    rw [hg0, this]; exact real_inner_self_nonneg
2685  set B : ℝ := 2 * Real.sqrt g0 with hB
2686  have hB0 : 0 ≤ B := by positivity
2687  set f : ℕ → ℝ := fun N => β * (2 * B * Real.sqrt (β * C * aN d s N / 2)) * B with hf
2688  have hf0 : Tendsto f atTop (𝓝 0) := by
2689    have h1 := (RefSmall.tendsto_aN (by omega : 1 ≤ d) hs0 hsd)
2690    have h2 : Tendsto (fun N : ℕ => β * C * aN d s N / 2) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
2691      simpa using (h1.const_mul (β * C)).div_const 2
2692    have h3 : Tendsto (fun N : ℕ => Real.sqrt (β * C * aN d s N / 2)) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
2693      have := (Real.continuous_sqrt.tendsto 0).comp h2
2694      rw [Real.sqrt_zero] at this
2695      exact this
2696    have h4 := ((h3.const_mul (2 * B)).const_mul β).mul_const B
2697    simpa [hf] using h4
2698  obtain ⟨N₂, hN₂⟩ := eventually_atTop.1 (hf0.eventually (gt_mem_nhds hε))
2699  refine ⟨max N₁ N₂, fun ρ hρ hΛρ N hN x => ?_⟩
2700  haveI := isProb_withDensityReal hρ
2701  set μ := withDensityReal ρ with hμ
2702  have h := refHyp_weakSplit hd hs0 hsd hτ hτ1 L hρ hΛρ Ψ hΨ
2703  obtain ⟨n, rfl⟩ : ∃ n, N = n + 1 := ⟨N - 1, by have := le_trans (le_max_left N₁ N₂) hN; omega⟩
2704  have hN1 : N₁ ≤ n + 1 := le_trans (le_max_left _ _) hN
2705  have hfN := hN₂ (n + 1) (le_trans (le_max_right _ _) hN)
2706  have hq := RefSmall.smallKernel_weakSplit hd hs0 hsd hτ L
2707  set P := RefSmall.R0small (weakSplit d s τ L).q β μ (n + 1) with hP
2708  haveI : IsProbabilityMeasure P := hq.isProb_R0small hρ hΛρ hβ.le (n + 1)
2709  have hPex := RefSmall.R0small_isExchangeable hq.meas hq.even β μ (n + 1)
2710  have hmarg : ∀ i : Fin (n + 1), P.map (fun X => X i) = P.map (fun X => X 0) :=
2711    fun i => hPex.marginal_eq i 0
2712  have hR0 : R0 (weakSplit d s τ L).q μ β (n + 1) = P := R0_eq_R0small h hβ.le (n + 1)
2713  have h1 := h.tN_norm_le hβ (Nat.succ_pos n)
2714  rw [hR0, integral_empXi_eq h P hmarg] at h1
2715  set π0 := P.map (fun X => X 0) with hπ0
2716  have h2 : ‖∫ y, Xi μ Ψ y ∂π0‖ ≤ 2 * B * tvDist π0 μ := by
2717    have := RefSmall.norm_integral_sub_le_two_mul_tvDist π0 μ h.measurable_Xi hB0
2718      (fun y => h.norm_Xi_le y)
2719    rwa [h.integral_Xi, sub_zero] at this
2720  have h3 : tvDist π0 μ ≤ Real.sqrt (β * C * aN d s (n + 1) / 2) := by
2721    have hmul := hPex.mul_klDiv_marginal_le μ 0 P
2722    have hK := hKL ρ hρ hΛρ (n + 1) hN1
2723    have ha0 := aN_nonneg (s := s) (d := d) (N := n + 1) (by omega)
2724    have hn0 : (0 : ℝ) < ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) := by positivity
2725    have e : ENNReal.ofReal (β * ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) * (C * aN d s (n + 1))) =
2726        ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ≥0∞) * ENNReal.ofReal (β * (C * aN d s (n + 1))) := by
2727      rw [show β * ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) * (C * aN d s (n + 1)) =
2728        ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) * (β * (C * aN d s (n + 1))) by ring,
2729        ENNReal.ofReal_mul hn0.le, ENNReal.ofReal_natCast]
2730    have hK2 : InformationTheory.klDiv π0 μ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (β * (C * aN d s (n + 1))) := by
2731      rw [e] at hK
2732      have := hmul.trans hK
2733      exact (ENNReal.mul_le_mul_iff_right (by simp) (by simp)).1 this
2734    have hKne : InformationTheory.klDiv π0 μ ≠ ⊤ := ne_top_of_le_ne_top ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top hK2
2735    refine (tvDist_le_sqrt_klDiv π0 μ hKne).trans (Real.sqrt_le_sqrt ?_)
2736    have := ENNReal.toReal_mono ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top hK2
2737    rw [ENNReal.toReal_ofReal (by positivity)] at this
2738    linarith
2739  have h4 : ‖tN (weakSplit d s τ L).q μ Ψ β (n + 1)‖ ≤ β * (2 * B * Real.sqrt (β * C * aN d s (n + 1) / 2)) :=
2740    h1.trans (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (h2.trans (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h3 (by positivity))) hβ.le)
2741  calc |inner ℝ (tN (weakSplit d s τ L).q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) (Xi μ Ψ x)|
2742      ≤ ‖tN (weakSplit d s τ L).q μ Ψ β (n + 1)‖ * ‖Xi μ Ψ x‖ := abs_real_inner_le_norm _ _
2743    _ ≤ (β * (2 * B * Real.sqrt (β * C * aN d s (n + 1) / 2))) * B :=
2744        mul_le_mul h4 (h.norm_Xi_le x) (norm_nonneg _) (by positivity)
2745    _ = f (n + 1) := rfl
2746    _ ≤ ε := hfN.le
2747
2748end Small
