2754/-- **Prop 4.2, reference part, `L²` bound**: `‖dπ_N/dμ - 1‖_{L²(μ)} ≤ C δ_N`. -/
2755theorem reference_marginal_density_L2 (hd : 2 ≤ d) {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {τ : ℝ}
2756    (hτ : 0 < τ) (hτ1 : τ ≤ 1) (L : ℕ) {Λ : ℝ} (hΛ1 : 1 ≤ Λ) {β : ℝ} (hβ : 0 < β)
2757    (hκ : KappaSmall β (Λ * (weakSplit d s τ L).c))
2758    {M : ℕ} (Ψ : Torus d → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin M))
2759    (hΨ : ∀ x y, (weakSplit d s τ L).gL (x - y) = inner ℝ (Ψ x) (Ψ y)) :
2760    ∃ (C : ℝ) (N₀ : ℕ), ∀ ρ : Torus d → ℝ, IsProbDensity ρ → (∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ) → ∀ N : ℕ, N₀ ≤ N →
2761      firstMarginal (RN (weakSplit d s τ L).q (withDensityReal ρ) Ψ β N) ≪ withDensityReal ρ ∧
2762        eLpNorm (fun y => ((firstMarginal (RN (weakSplit d s τ L).q (withDensityReal ρ) Ψ β N)).rnDeriv
2763            (withDensityReal ρ) y).toReal - 1) 2 (withDensityReal ρ) ≤
2764          ENNReal.ofReal (C * deltaN d s N) := by
2765  obtain ⟨-, -, h3, h4, h5, h6, h7, h8, h9, h10, -, -, h13, -, h15, -, -⟩ :=
2766    weakSplit_spec hd hs0 hsd hτ hτ1 L
2767  set c : ℝ := (weakSplit d s τ L).c with hc
2768  set q : Torus d → ℝ := (weakSplit d s τ L).q with hq
2769  set gL : Torus d → ℝ := (weakSplit d s τ L).gL with hgL
2770  obtain ⟨B, hB0, hB⟩ := h15
2771  obtain ⟨K0, hK0, hw⟩ := weakLp_of_envelope hd hs0 hsd hB0 hB
2772  have hqc : ∫⁻ z, ENNReal.ofReal (q z) ≤ ENNReal.ofReal c := by
2773    have := ofReal_integral_eq_lintegral_ofReal h7 (Filter.Eventually.of_forall h6)
2774    rw [h8] at this
2775    exact le_of_eq this.symm
2776  have hgLi : Integrable gL := h13.integrable_of_hasCompactSupport (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace gL)
2777  have hkm : Measurable (fun w => gL w + q w) := h13.measurable.add h5
2778  have hki : Integrable (fun w => gL w + q w) := hgLi.add h7
2779  set ĝ : Lattice d → ℝ := fun n => rieszCoeff d s n + (if n = 0 then c else 0) with hĝdef
2780  have hĝ : ∀ n, 0 ≤ ĝ n := by
2781    intro n
2782    by_cases hn : n = 0
2783    · simp [hĝdef, hn, rieszCoeff, h10]
2784    · have := rieszCoeff_pos hd hs0 hsd n hn
2785      simp [hĝdef, hn]; exact this.le
2786  have hcoef : ∀ n, ∫ x, Torus.mode (-n) x * (((gL x + q x : ℝ)) : ℂ) = (ĝ n : ℂ) := by
2787    intro n
2788    have e1 : ∫ x, Torus.mode (-n) x * (((gL x + q x : ℝ)) : ℂ) =
2789        ∫ x, (Torus.mode (-n) x * ((periodicRiesz d s x : ℝ) : ℂ) + Torus.mode (-n) x * (c : ℂ)) := by
2790      refine integral_congr_ae ?_
2791      filter_upwards [h4] with x hx
2792      rw [← mul_add]
2793      congr 1
2794      rw [hx]; push_cast; ring
2795    rw [e1]
2796    have hgi : Integrable (fun x => Torus.mode (-n) x * ((periodicRiesz d s x : ℝ) : ℂ)) := by
2797      have := (KernelAux.integrable_periodicRiesz hd hs0 hsd).ofReal (𝕜 := ℂ)
2798      exact this.bdd_mul (c := 1) (Torus.continuous_mode (-n)).aestronglyMeasurable
2799        (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => (Torus.norm_mode (-n) x).le)
2800    have hci : Integrable (fun x => Torus.mode (-n) x * (c : ℂ)) := by
2801      have : Integrable (fun x => Torus.mode (-n) x) :=
2802        (integrable_const (1 : ℂ)).bdd_mul (c := 1) (Torus.continuous_mode (-n)).aestronglyMeasurable
2803          (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => (Torus.norm_mode (-n) x).le) |>.congr
2804          (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => by simp)
2805      exact this.mul_const _
2806    rw [integral_add hgi hci, KernelAux.integral_periodicRiesz_mode hd hs0 hsd n, integral_mul_const,
2807      Torus.integral_mode (-n)]
2808    by_cases hn : n = 0
2809    · simp [hĝdef, hn, rieszCoeff]
2810    · have : -n ≠ 0 := neg_ne_zero.2 hn
2811      simp [hĝdef, hn, this]
2812  have hRef : ∀ ρ : Torus d → ℝ, IsProbDensity ρ → (∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ) →
2813      RefHyp (withDensityReal ρ) q gL Ψ (Λ * c) := fun ρ hρ hΛρ =>
2814    refHyp_weakSplit hd hs0 hsd hτ hτ1 L hρ hΛρ Ψ hΨ
2815  obtain ⟨C, N₁, hcore⟩ := core_L2 hd hs0 hsd hΛ1 hβ h10 hRef h5 h6 (hQ_even hd hs0 hsd τ L) h7 hqc hK0 hw
2816    hkm hki ĝ hĝ hcoef hκ
2817  obtain ⟨N₂, hsmall⟩ := small_field hd hs0 hsd hτ hτ1 L hΛ1 hβ Ψ hΨ 1 one_pos
2818  refine ⟨C, max N₁ N₂, fun ρ hρ hΛρ N hN => ?_⟩
2819  exact hcore ρ hρ hΛρ N (le_trans (le_max_left _ _) hN)
2820    (fun x => hsmall ρ hρ hΛρ N (le_trans (le_max_right _ _) hN) x)
2821
2822end Final2
2823
2824section FieldCmp
2825
2826variable {E : Type*} [MeasurableSpace E] {μ : Measure E} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ]
2827
2828/-- **Two weak ensembles with different one-body fields** (`A:eq:disagreement`): the marginals differ by
2829at most `4 e^{M+M'+βγ} ‖ψ - ψ'‖_{L¹(μ)}`. -/
2830theorem field_event_comparison (V : WeakPotential μ) {β : ℝ} (hβ : 0 ≤ β) {N n : ℕ} (hN0 : 0 < N)
2831    (hnN : n ≤ N) {ψ ψ' : E → ℝ} (hψ : IsField μ ψ V.M V.M') (hψ' : IsField μ ψ' V.M V.M')
2832    (hκ : weakKappa V β ≤ 1 / 2) {D : ℝ} (hD : ∫ z, |ψ z - ψ' z| ∂μ ≤ D) (j : Fin n)
2833    {A : Set E} (hA : MeasurableSet A) :
2834    |((fieldLaw V ψ (β / N) n).map (fun y => y j)).real A -
2835        ((fieldLaw V ψ' (β / N) n).map (fun y => y j)).real A| ≤
2836      4 * Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + β * V.γ) * D := by
2837  have hlam : 0 ≤ β / N := div_nonneg hβ (Nat.cast_nonneg N)
2838  have hD0 : 0 ≤ D := le_trans (integral_nonneg fun z => abs_nonneg _) hD
2839  have hΛ : V.M' + β / N * n * V.γ ≤ V.M' + β * V.γ := by
2840    have h1 : β / N * n ≤ β := by
2841      have : (n : ℝ) ≤ N := by exact_mod_cast hnN
2842      have hN' : (0 : ℝ) < N := by exact_mod_cast hN0
2843      calc β / N * n ≤ β / N * N := by gcongr
2844        _ = β := by field_simp
2845    have := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right h1 V.γ_nonneg
2846    linarith
2847  have h := rvh_field_event (V := V) j hψ hψ' hlam (Λ := V.M' + β * V.γ) hΛ hD
2848    (r := weakKappa V β) (fun i => by
2849      rw [four_exp_eq]
2850      exact row_sum_le_of_uniform hN0 hnN (weakKappa_nonneg V hβ) i) (by linarith) hA
2851  refine h.trans ?_
2852  have e : V.M + (V.M' + β * V.γ) = V.M + V.M' + β * V.γ := by ring
2853  rw [e]
2854  have h1 : (1 : ℝ) / 2 ≤ 1 - weakKappa V β := by linarith
2855  have h2 : 0 ≤ 2 * Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + β * V.γ) * D := by positivity
2856  rw [div_le_iff₀ (by linarith)]
2857  calc 2 * Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + β * V.γ) * D
2858      = 4 * Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + β * V.γ) * D * (1 / 2) := by ring
2859    _ ≤ 4 * Real.exp (V.M + V.M' + β * V.γ) * D * (1 - weakKappa V β) :=
2860        mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h1 (by positivity)
2861
2862end FieldCmp
2863
2864section TvRN
2865
2866variable {d M : ℕ} {μ : Measure (Torus d)} {q gL : Torus d → ℝ}
2867  {Ψ : Torus d → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin M)} {γ : ℝ} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] {β : ℝ} {n : ℕ}
2868
2869theorem R0_eq_fieldLawK (h : RefHyp μ q gL Ψ γ) (hβ : 0 ≤ β) (N : ℕ) {K : ℝ}
2870    (ht0 : ∀ x, |inner ℝ (0 : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin M)) (Xi μ Ψ x)| ≤ K) :
2871    R0 q μ β N = fieldLaw (weakPotK h hβ 0 ht0) (weakPotK h hβ 0 ht0).φ (β / N) N := by
2872  unfold R0
2873  rw [fieldLaw_eq_tilted]
2874  · congr 1
2875    funext X
2876    simp only [E0, weakPotK, pairEnergy, inner_zero_left, add_zero]
2877  · have := h.integrable_exp_E0 hβ N
2878    convert this using 2 with X
2879    simp only [E0, weakPotK, pairEnergy, inner_zero_left, add_zero]
2880
2881/-- **`R_N` versus `R⁰_N`** (`A:eq:disagreement`): the first marginals differ by at most
2882`4 e^{M + βγ} ‖t_N·Ξ‖_∞` in total variation. -/
2883theorem tv_RN_R0 (h : RefHyp μ q gL Ψ γ) (hβ : 0 < β)
2884    (ht : ∀ x, |inner ℝ (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) (Xi μ Ψ x)| ≤ 1)
2885    (hκ : weakKappa (weakPotK h hβ.le (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) ht) β ≤ 1 / 2) {εh : ℝ}
2886    (hεh : ∀ x, |inner ℝ (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) (Xi μ Ψ x)| ≤ εh) {A : Set (Torus d)}
2887    (hA : MeasurableSet A) :
2888    |(firstMarginal (RN q μ Ψ β (n + 1))).real A -
2889        ((R0 q μ β (n + 1)).map (fun X => X ⟨0, Nat.succ_pos n⟩)).real A| ≤
2890      4 * Real.exp ((β * γ + 1) + 0 + β * γ) * εh := by
2891  set V := weakPotK h hβ.le (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) ht with hV
2892  have ht0 : ∀ x, |inner ℝ (0 : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin M)) (Xi μ Ψ x)| ≤ 1 := fun x => by simp
2893  set V0 := weakPotK h hβ.le (0 : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin M)) ht0 with hV0
2894  have hRN := RN_eq_fieldLawK h hβ.le ht (N := n + 1)
2895  rw [← hV] at hRN
2896  have hR0 := R0_eq_fieldLawK h hβ.le (n + 1) ht0
2897  rw [← hV0] at hR0
2898  have hψ : IsField μ V.φ V.M V.M' := ⟨V.measurable_φ, V.φ_le, V.integrable_φ, V.neg_le_integral_φ⟩
2899  have hψ' : IsField μ V0.φ V.M V.M' :=
2900    ⟨V0.measurable_φ, fun x => V0.φ_le x, V0.integrable_φ, V0.neg_le_integral_φ⟩
2901  have hD : ∫ z, |V.φ z - V0.φ z| ∂μ ≤ εh := by
2902    have hb : ∀ z, |V.φ z - V0.φ z| ≤ εh := fun z => by
2903      have : V.φ z - V0.φ z = inner ℝ (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) (Xi μ Ψ z) := by
2904        show β * convMeasure q μ z + inner ℝ (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) (Xi μ Ψ z) -
2905          (β * convMeasure q μ z + inner ℝ (0 : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin M)) (Xi μ Ψ z)) = _
2906        simp
2907      rw [this]; exact hεh z
2908    calc ∫ z, |V.φ z - V0.φ z| ∂μ ≤ ∫ _z, εh ∂μ :=
2909          integral_mono_of_nonneg (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun z => abs_nonneg _)
2910            (integrable_const _) (Filter.Eventually.of_forall hb)
2911      _ = εh := by simp
2912  have h1 := field_event_comparison V hβ.le (N := n + 1) (n := n + 1) (Nat.succ_pos n) le_rfl hψ hψ' hκ hD
2913    ⟨0, Nat.succ_pos n⟩ hA
2914  have e1 : firstMarginal (RN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) =
2915      (fieldLaw V V.φ (β / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) (n + 1)).map (fun y => y ⟨0, Nat.succ_pos n⟩) := by
2916    unfold firstMarginal; rw [dif_pos (Nat.succ_pos n), hRN]
2917  have e2 : (R0 q μ β (n + 1)).map (fun X => X ⟨0, Nat.succ_pos n⟩) =
2918      (fieldLaw V V0.φ (β / ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) (n + 1)).map (fun y => y ⟨0, Nat.succ_pos n⟩) := by
2919    rw [hR0]; rfl
2920  rw [e1, e2]
2921  exact h1
2922
2923end TvRN

2925section TvR0
2926
2927variable {d M : ℕ}
2928
2929open HRS.External.Entropy InformationTheory in
2930/-- `‖π⁰_N - μ‖_TV ≤ √(β C a_N / 2)` uniformly over the densities `ρ ≤ Λ`. -/
2931theorem tv_R0_mu (hd : 2 ≤ d) {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {τ : ℝ} (hτ : 0 < τ)
2932    (hτ1 : τ ≤ 1) (L : ℕ) {Λ : ℝ} (hΛ1 : 1 ≤ Λ) {β : ℝ} (hβ : 0 < β)
2933    (Ψ : Torus d → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin M))
2934    (hΨ : ∀ x y, (weakSplit d s τ L).gL (x - y) = inner ℝ (Ψ x) (Ψ y)) :
2935    ∃ (C : ℝ) (N₁ : ℕ), 0 ≤ C ∧ ∀ ρ : Torus d → ℝ, IsProbDensity ρ → (∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ) → ∀ n : ℕ,
2936      N₁ ≤ n + 1 →
2937      tvDist ((R0 (weakSplit d s τ L).q (withDensityReal ρ) β (n + 1)).map
2938          (fun X => X ⟨0, Nat.succ_pos n⟩)) (withDensityReal ρ) ≤
2939        Real.sqrt (β * C * aN d s (n + 1) / 2) := by
2940  obtain ⟨C, hC0, N₁, hN₁2, hKL⟩ := RefSmall.klDiv_R0small_weakSplit_le hd hs0 hsd hτ L hΛ1 hβ.le
2941  refine ⟨C, N₁, hC0, fun ρ hρ hΛρ n hN1 => ?_⟩
2942  haveI := isProb_withDensityReal hρ
2943  set μ := withDensityReal ρ with hμ
2944  have h := refHyp_weakSplit hd hs0 hsd hτ hτ1 L hρ hΛρ Ψ hΨ
2945  have hq := RefSmall.smallKernel_weakSplit hd hs0 hsd hτ L
2946  set P := RefSmall.R0small (weakSplit d s τ L).q β μ (n + 1) with hP
2947  haveI : IsProbabilityMeasure P := hq.isProb_R0small hρ hΛρ hβ.le (n + 1)
2948  have hPex := RefSmall.R0small_isExchangeable hq.meas hq.even β μ (n + 1)
2949  have hR0 : R0 (weakSplit d s τ L).q μ β (n + 1) = P := R0_eq_R0small h hβ.le (n + 1)
2950  rw [hR0]
2951  set π0 := P.map (fun X => X ⟨0, Nat.succ_pos n⟩) with hπ0
2952  have hmul := hPex.mul_klDiv_marginal_le μ ⟨0, Nat.succ_pos n⟩ P
2953  have hK := hKL ρ hρ hΛρ (n + 1) hN1
2954  have ha0 := aN_nonneg (s := s) (d := d) (N := n + 1) (by omega)
2955  have hn0 : (0 : ℝ) < ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) := by positivity
2956  have e : ENNReal.ofReal (β * ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) * (C * aN d s (n + 1))) =
2957      ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ≥0∞) * ENNReal.ofReal (β * (C * aN d s (n + 1))) := by
2958    rw [show β * ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) * (C * aN d s (n + 1)) =
2959      ((n + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) * (β * (C * aN d s (n + 1))) by ring,
2960      ENNReal.ofReal_mul hn0.le, ENNReal.ofReal_natCast]
2961  have hK2 : InformationTheory.klDiv π0 μ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (β * (C * aN d s (n + 1))) := by
2962    rw [e] at hK
2963    have := hmul.trans hK
2964    exact (ENNReal.mul_le_mul_iff_right (by simp) (by simp)).1 this
2965  have hKne : InformationTheory.klDiv π0 μ ≠ ⊤ := ne_top_of_le_ne_top ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top hK2
2966  refine (tvDist_le_sqrt_klDiv π0 μ hKne).trans (Real.sqrt_le_sqrt ?_)
2967  have := ENNReal.toReal_mono ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top hK2
2968  rw [ENNReal.toReal_ofReal (by positivity)] at this
2969  linarith
2970
2971end TvR0

2973section ConvDiff
2974
2975variable {d : ℕ}
2976
2977open HRS.External.Entropy in
2978/-- **Truncation estimate** for `q * (P - Q)`: `|q*P(x) - q*Q(x)| ≤ K ‖P - Q‖_TV + 2 C_b Λ T(K)`, where
2979`T(K) ≥ ∫ (q - K)₊`. -/
2980theorem conv_diff_le {ρ : Torus d → ℝ} {Λ : ℝ} (hΛ : ∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ) (hΛ0 : 0 ≤ Λ)
2981    {q : Torus d → ℝ} (hqm : Measurable q) (hq0 : ∀ z, 0 ≤ q z)
2982    {P Q : Measure (Torus d)} [IsProbabilityMeasure P] [IsProbabilityMeasure Q] {Cb : ℝ} (hCb : 0 ≤ Cb)
2983    (hP : P ≤ ENNReal.ofReal Cb • withDensityReal ρ) (hQ : Q ≤ ENNReal.ofReal Cb • withDensityReal ρ)
2984    {c : ℝ} (hc0 : 0 ≤ c) (hqc : ∫⁻ z, ENNReal.ofReal (q z) ≤ ENNReal.ofReal c) {K : ℝ} (hK : 0 ≤ K)
2985    {T : ℝ} (hT0 : 0 ≤ T) (hT : ∫⁻ z, ENNReal.ofReal (q z - K) ≤ ENNReal.ofReal T) (x : Torus d) :
2986    |∫ y, q (x - y) ∂P - ∫ y, q (x - y) ∂Q| ≤ K * tvDist P Q + 2 * (Cb * (Λ * T)) := by
2987  set g1 : Torus d → ℝ := fun z => min (q z) K with hg1
2988  set g2 : Torus d → ℝ := fun z => max (q z - K) 0 with hg2
2989  have hg1m : Measurable g1 := hqm.min measurable_const
2990  have hg2m : Measurable g2 := (hqm.sub_const K).max measurable_const
2991  have hg10 : ∀ z, 0 ≤ g1 z := fun z => le_min (hq0 z) hK
2992  have hg20 : ∀ z, 0 ≤ g2 z := fun z => le_max_right _ _
2993  have hsplit : ∀ z, q z = g1 z + g2 z := by
2994    intro z
2995    simp only [hg1, hg2]
2996    rcases le_total (q z) K with h | h
2997    · rw [min_eq_left h, max_eq_right (by linarith)]; ring
2998    · rw [min_eq_right h, max_eq_left (by linarith)]; ring
2999  have hxm : ∀ f : Torus d → ℝ, Measurable f → Measurable fun y => f (x - y) :=
3000    fun f hf => hf.comp (measurable_const.sub measurable_id)
3001  -- integrability of the pieces
3002  have hg1b : ∀ y, |g1 (x - y)| ≤ K := fun y => by
3003    rw [abs_of_nonneg (hg10 _)]; exact min_le_right _ _
3004  have hg2i : ∀ (Pm : Measure (Torus d)), Pm ≤ ENNReal.ofReal Cb • withDensityReal ρ →
3005      Integrable (fun y => g2 (x - y)) Pm := by
3006    intro Pm hR
3007    have h1 : ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (g2 (x - y)) ∂Pm ≤ ENNReal.ofReal Cb * (ENNReal.ofReal Λ *
3008        ENNReal.ofReal T) := by
3009      calc ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (g2 (x - y)) ∂Pm
3010          ≤ ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (g2 (x - y)) ∂(ENNReal.ofReal Cb • withDensityReal ρ) :=
3011            lintegral_mono' hR le_rfl
3012        _ = ENNReal.ofReal Cb * ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (g2 (x - y)) ∂(withDensityReal ρ) := by
3013            rw [lintegral_smul_measure, smul_eq_mul]
3014        _ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal Cb * (ENNReal.ofReal Λ * ∫⁻ z, ENNReal.ofReal (g2 z)) := by
3015            gcongr
3016            exact KernelAux2.lintegral_conv_le ρ Λ hΛ (fun z => ENNReal.ofReal (g2 z)) x
3017        _ ≤ _ := by
3018            gcongr
3019            have : ∫⁻ z, ENNReal.ofReal (g2 z) = ∫⁻ z, ENNReal.ofReal (q z - K) := by
3020              refine lintegral_congr fun z => ?_
3021              simp only [hg2]
3022              rw [ENNReal.ofReal_max]; simp
3023            rw [this]; exact hT
3024    refine ⟨(hxm g2 hg2m).aestronglyMeasurable, ?_⟩
3025    have : ∫⁻ y, ‖g2 (x - y)‖ₑ ∂Pm = ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (g2 (x - y)) ∂Pm := by
3026      refine lintegral_congr fun y => ?_
3027      rw [Real.enorm_of_nonneg (hg20 _)]
3028    rw [HasFiniteIntegral, this]
3029    exact lt_of_le_of_lt h1 (by
3030      refine ENNReal.mul_lt_top ENNReal.ofReal_lt_top (ENNReal.mul_lt_top ENNReal.ofReal_lt_top
3031        ENNReal.ofReal_lt_top))
3032  have hg1i : ∀ (Pm : Measure (Torus d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure Pm], Integrable (fun y => g1 (x - y)) Pm :=
3033    fun Pm _ => Integrable.of_bound (hxm g1 hg1m).aestronglyMeasurable K
3034      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => by rw [Real.norm_eq_abs]; exact hg1b y)
3035  have e : ∀ (Pm : Measure (Torus d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure Pm], Pm ≤ ENNReal.ofReal Cb • withDensityReal ρ →
3036      ∫ y, q (x - y) ∂Pm = ∫ y, g1 (x - y) ∂Pm + ∫ y, g2 (x - y) ∂Pm := by
3037    intro Pm _ hR
3038    rw [← integral_add (hg1i Pm) (hg2i Pm hR)]
3039    exact integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => hsplit _)
3040  have hI2 : ∀ (Pm : Measure (Torus d)), Pm ≤ ENNReal.ofReal Cb • withDensityReal ρ →
3041      ∫ y, g2 (x - y) ∂Pm ≤ Cb * (Λ * T) := fun Pm hR => by
3042    refine integral_kernel_le hΛ hΛ0 hR hCb hg2m hg20 hT0 ?_ x
3043    have : ∫⁻ z, ENNReal.ofReal (g2 z) = ∫⁻ z, ENNReal.ofReal (q z - K) := by
3044      refine lintegral_congr fun z => ?_
3045      simp only [hg2]
3046      rw [ENNReal.ofReal_max]; simp
3047    rw [this]; exact hT
3048  have hI20 : ∀ (Pm : Measure (Torus d)), 0 ≤ ∫ y, g2 (x - y) ∂Pm := fun Pm =>
3049    integral_nonneg fun y => hg20 _
3050  rw [e P hP, e Q hQ]
3051  have hTV := RefSmall.abs_integral_sub_le_tvDist_of_nonneg P Q (g := fun y => g1 (x - y)) (hxm g1 hg1m)
3052    hK (fun y => hg10 _) (fun y => min_le_right _ _)
3053  have h2 := hI2 P hP
3054  have h3 := hI2 Q hQ
3055  have h4 := hI20 P
3056  have h5 := hI20 Q
3057  rw [abs_le] at hTV ⊢
3058  constructor <;> linarith [hTV.1, hTV.2]

3066theorem tendsto_deltaN (hd : 2 ≤ d) {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
3067    Tendsto (fun N : ℕ => deltaN d s N) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
3068  have hcast : Tendsto (fun N : ℕ => (N : ℝ)) atTop atTop := tendsto_natCast_atTop_atTop
3069  have hd0 : (0 : ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast (by omega : 0 < d)
3070  unfold deltaN
3071  by_cases h1 : s < (d : ℝ) / 2
3072  · simp only [h1, if_true]
3073    exact tendsto_inv_atTop_zero.comp hcast
3074  · by_cases h2 : s = (d : ℝ) / 2
3075    · simp only [h2, lt_irrefl, if_false, if_true]
3076      have h3 : Tendsto (fun x : ℝ => Real.log x / x) atTop (𝓝 0) :=
3077        Real.isLittleO_log_id_atTop.tendsto_div_nhds_zero
3078      have h4 : Tendsto (fun N : ℕ => Real.log (N : ℝ) / (N : ℝ)) atTop (𝓝 0) := h3.comp hcast
3079      have h5 : Tendsto (fun N : ℕ => (N : ℝ)⁻¹ + Real.log (N : ℝ) / (N : ℝ)) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
3080        simpa using (tendsto_inv_atTop_zero.comp hcast).add h4
3081      refine h5.congr' ?_
3082      filter_upwards [eventually_ge_atTop 1] with N hN
3083      have hN0 : (0 : ℝ) < N := by exact_mod_cast hN
3084      rw [Real.log_mul (Real.exp_pos 1).ne' hN0.ne', Real.log_exp]
3085      field_simp
3086    · simp only [h1, h2, if_false]
3087      have h3 : (d : ℝ) / 2 < s := lt_of_le_of_ne (not_lt.1 h1) (Ne.symm h2)
3088      have hs : 0 < s := by linarith
3089      have hneg : 1 - (d : ℝ) / s < 0 := by
3090        have : 1 < (d : ℝ) / s := by rw [lt_div_iff₀ hs]; linarith
3091        linarith
3092      have := (tendsto_rpow_neg_atTop (y := (d : ℝ) / s - 1) (by
3093        have : 1 < (d : ℝ) / s := by rw [lt_div_iff₀ hs]; linarith
3094        linarith)).comp hcast
3095      refine this.congr fun N => ?_
3096      simp only [Function.comp]
3097      congr 1; ring
3098

3100open HRS.External.Entropy in
3101theorem tvDist_le_of_forall {α : Type*} [MeasurableSpace α] (P Q : Measure α) {c : ℝ}
3102    (h : ∀ A : Set α, MeasurableSet A → |P.real A - Q.real A| ≤ c) : tvDist P Q ≤ c :=
3103  ciSup_le fun A => h A.1 A.2
3104
3105theorem abs_sub_one_le_of_abs_log_le {θ E : ℝ} (hθ : 0 < θ) (h : |Real.log θ| ≤ E) :
3106    |θ - 1| ≤ Real.exp E - 1 := by
3107  have h1 : θ ≤ Real.exp E := by
3108    have := (abs_le.1 h).2
3109    calc θ = Real.exp (Real.log θ) := (Real.exp_log hθ).symm
3110      _ ≤ _ := Real.exp_le_exp.2 this
3111  have h2 : Real.exp (-E) ≤ θ := by
3112    have := (abs_le.1 h).1
3113    calc Real.exp (-E) ≤ Real.exp (Real.log θ) := Real.exp_le_exp.2 this
3114      _ = θ := Real.exp_log hθ
3115  have h3 : 2 ≤ Real.exp E + Real.exp (-E) := by
3116    have := sq_nonneg (Real.exp (E / 2) - Real.exp (-(E / 2)))
3117    have e1 : Real.exp (E / 2) * Real.exp (-(E / 2)) = 1 := by rw [← Real.exp_add]; simp
3118    have e2 : Real.exp (E / 2) ^ 2 = Real.exp E := by rw [← Real.exp_nat_mul]; congr 1; push_cast; ring
3119    have e3 : Real.exp (-(E / 2)) ^ 2 = Real.exp (-E) := by rw [← Real.exp_nat_mul]; congr 1; push_cast; ring
3120    nlinarith [e1, e2, e3]
3121  rw [abs_le]
3122  constructor <;> linarith
3123
3124end Aux3

3343    rw [e]
3344    have := abs_add_le (β * (convMeasure q μ x - Qν x) + lam * Qν x + inner ℝ (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) (Xi μ Ψ x)) (Dfun x)
3345    have := abs_add_le (β * (convMeasure q μ x - Qν x) + lam * Qν x) (inner ℝ (tN q μ Ψ β (n + 1)) (Xi μ Ψ x))
3346    have := abs_add_le (β * (convMeasure q μ x - Qν x)) (lam * Qν x)
3347    have hEs5 : Es = 5 * ε' := by rw [hε']; ring
3348    linarith
3349  -- normalisation: `Z = ∫ exp(gfun)`
3350  have hZeq : ∫ x, cavTheta V lam n a0 x ∂μ = ∫ x, Real.exp (gfun x) ∂μ := by
3351    refine integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => ?_)
3352    show Real.exp (pinField V lam a0 x) * cavU V lam n a0 x = Real.exp (V.φ x + Real.log (cavU V lam n a0 x))
3353    rw [Real.exp_add, Real.exp_log (cavU_pos V hlam0 a0 n x), pinField_empty]
3354  have hZi : Integrable (fun x => Real.exp (gfun x)) μ := by
3355    rw [← funext fun x => show cavTheta V lam n a0 x = Real.exp (gfun x) from by
3356      show Real.exp (pinField V lam a0 x) * cavU V lam n a0 x = Real.exp (V.φ x + Real.log (cavU V lam n a0 x))
3357      rw [Real.exp_add, Real.exp_log (cavU_pos V hlam0 a0 n x), pinField_empty]]
3358    exact integrable_cavTheta V hlam0 a0 n
3359  have hZhi : ∫ x, Real.exp (gfun x) ∂μ ≤ Real.exp Es := by
3360    calc ∫ x, Real.exp (gfun x) ∂μ ≤ ∫ _x, Real.exp Es ∂μ :=
3361          integral_mono hZi (integrable_const _) fun x => Real.exp_le_exp.2 ((abs_le.1 (hgb x)).2)
3362      _ = Real.exp Es := by simp
3363  have hZlo : Real.exp (-Es) ≤ ∫ x, Real.exp (gfun x) ∂μ := by
3364    calc Real.exp (-Es) = ∫ _x, Real.exp (-Es) ∂μ := by simp
3365      _ ≤ _ := integral_mono (integrable_const _) hZi fun x => Real.exp_le_exp.2 ((abs_le.1 (hgb x)).1)
3366  have hlogZ : |Real.log (∫ x, cavTheta V lam n a0 x ∂μ)| ≤ Es := by
3367    rw [hZeq]
3368    have hZpos : 0 < ∫ x, Real.exp (gfun x) ∂μ := lt_of_lt_of_le (Real.exp_pos _) hZlo
3369    rw [abs_le]
3370    constructor
3371    · calc -Es = Real.log (Real.exp (-Es)) := (Real.log_exp _).symm
3372        _ ≤ _ := Real.log_le_log (Real.exp_pos _) hZlo
3373    · calc Real.log (∫ x, Real.exp (gfun x) ∂μ) ≤ Real.log (Real.exp Es) := Real.log_le_log hZpos hZhi
3374        _ = Es := Real.log_exp _
3375  have hlogθ : ∀ x, |Real.log (θ x)| ≤ 2 * Es := by
3376    intro x
3377    have := log_theta_eq V hlam0 n x
3378    have e : Real.log (θ x) = gfun x - Real.log (∫ x', cavTheta V lam n a0 x' ∂μ) := by
3379      rw [this]
3380    rw [e]
3381    have h1 := hgb x
3382    calc |gfun x - Real.log (∫ x', cavTheta V lam n a0 x' ∂μ)| ≤
3383        |gfun x| + |Real.log (∫ x', cavTheta V lam n a0 x' ∂μ)| := abs_sub _ _
3384      _ ≤ 2 * Es := by linarith
3385  have hε2 : Real.exp (2 * Es) - 1 = ε := by
3386    rw [hEs, show 2 * (Real.log (1 + ε) / 2) = Real.log (1 + ε) by ring, Real.exp_log (by linarith)]
3387    ring
3388  filter_upwards [hrn] with y h1
3389  rw [h1, ENNReal.toReal_ofReal (hθ0 y).le]
3390  have := abs_sub_one_le_of_abs_log_le (hθ0 y) (hlogθ y)
3391  rw [hε2] at this
3392  exact this
3393
3394end CoreUnif
3395
3396section Final3
3397
3398variable {d : ℕ}
3399
3400/-- **`Q:eq:reference-input`, last bound** (`‖dπ_N/dμ - 1‖_∞ → 0`). -/
3401theorem reference_marginal_density_uniform (hd : 2 ≤ d) {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {τ : ℝ}
3402    (hτ : 0 < τ) (hτ1 : τ ≤ 1) (L : ℕ) {Λ : ℝ} (hΛ1 : 1 ≤ Λ) {β : ℝ} (hβ : 0 < β)
3403    (hκ : KappaSmall β (Λ * (weakSplit d s τ L).c))
3404    {M : ℕ} (Ψ : Torus d → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin M))
3405    (hΨ : ∀ x y, (weakSplit d s τ L).gL (x - y) = inner ℝ (Ψ x) (Ψ y)) :
3406    ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε → ∃ N₀ : ℕ, ∀ ρ : Torus d → ℝ, IsProbDensity ρ → (∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ) →
3407      ∀ N : ℕ, N₀ ≤ N →
3408      firstMarginal (RN (weakSplit d s τ L).q (withDensityReal ρ) Ψ β N) ≪ withDensityReal ρ ∧
3409        ∀ᵐ y ∂(withDensityReal ρ),
3410          |((firstMarginal (RN (weakSplit d s τ L).q (withDensityReal ρ) Ψ β N)).rnDeriv
3411            (withDensityReal ρ) y).toReal - 1| ≤ ε := by
3412  obtain ⟨-, -, h3, h4, h5, h6, h7, h8, h9, h10, -, -, h13, -, h15, -, -⟩ :=
3413    weakSplit_spec hd hs0 hsd hτ hτ1 L
3414  set c : ℝ := (weakSplit d s τ L).c with hc
3415  set q : Torus d → ℝ := (weakSplit d s τ L).q with hq
3416  obtain ⟨B, hB0, hB⟩ := h15
3417  obtain ⟨K0, hK0, hw⟩ := weakLp_of_envelope hd hs0 hsd hB0 hB
3418  have hqc : ∫⁻ z, ENNReal.ofReal (q z) ≤ ENNReal.ofReal c := by
3419    have := ofReal_integral_eq_lintegral_ofReal h7 (Filter.Eventually.of_forall h6)
3420    rw [h8] at this
3421    exact le_of_eq this.symm
3422  have hRef : ∀ ρ : Torus d → ℝ, IsProbDensity ρ → (∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ) →
3423      RefHyp (withDensityReal ρ) q (weakSplit d s τ L).gL Ψ (Λ * c) := fun ρ hρ hΛρ =>
3424    refHyp_weakSplit hd hs0 hsd hτ hτ1 L hρ hΛρ Ψ hΨ
3425  obtain ⟨C₃, N₃, hC₃0, hTV3⟩ := tv_R0_mu hd hs0 hsd hτ hτ1 L hΛ1 hβ Ψ hΨ
3426  have hε₀ : Tendsto (fun N : ℕ => Real.sqrt (β * C₃ * aN d s N / 2)) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
3427    have h1 := (RefSmall.tendsto_aN (by omega : 1 ≤ d) hs0 hsd)
3428    have h2 : Tendsto (fun N : ℕ => β * C₃ * aN d s N / 2) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
3429      simpa using (h1.const_mul (β * C₃)).div_const 2
3430    have := (Real.continuous_sqrt.tendsto 0).comp h2
3431    rw [Real.sqrt_zero] at this
3432    exact this
3433  exact core_unif hd hs0 hsd hΛ1 hβ h10 hRef h5 h6 (hQ_even hd hs0 hsd τ L) hqc hK0 hw hκ
3434    (small_field hd hs0 hsd hτ hτ1 L hΛ1 hβ Ψ hΨ) hε₀ (N₃ := N₃)
3435    (fun ρ hρ hΛρ n hN3 _ => hTV3 ρ hρ hΛρ n hN3)
3436
3437end Final3
3438
3439end HRS.External.RefMarg
